### [1] [Thermodynamics of QCD+QED from the lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29423)
*G. Endrődi,E. Garnacho-Velasco,G. Markó,L. Pethő*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文探讨了有限温度下QCD+QED耦合系统的非微扰效应及其格点模拟策略，重点分析电磁耦合对热力学性质与相图结构的影响，并指出需发展新算法以从第一性原理理解该系统的相结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度下QED的引入可能破坏QCD的中心对称性并诱导非微扰效应，从而显著改变相图结构；而零温下QED修正已成熟，故需系统研究有限温QCD+QED的理论结构与数值实现。

Method: 基于格点量子场论框架，结合中心对称性分析、热场动力学与多尺度模拟技术，发展适用于QCD+QED联合系统的格点算法，并开展有限温数值模拟。

Result: 获得QCD+QED在有限温度下的热力学量（如压强、熵密度、准粒子谱）的初步格点结果，揭示电磁耦合对去禁闭相变温度、手征恢复及中心对称破缺模式的调制作用。

Conclusion: QCD+QED在有限温度下呈现非微扰耦合特征，其相结构受电磁相互作用显著影响；现有格点方法面临红外发散、电荷守恒与各向异性等问题，亟需发展满足U(1)规范不变性与中心对称性的新型算法。

Abstract: QED corrections to observables in QCD are by now well-understood at zero temperature, where QED effects are perturbative and required for precision physics observables, such as the neutron-proton mass difference or the muon anomalous magnetic moment, among many others. However, there are indications in the literature that at finite temperature the introduction of QED can lead to non-perturbative effects, affecting the structure of the phase diagram, since the combined theory is exactly center-symmetric. In this proceedings article, we analyze the structure of the combined theory and the different strategies to simulate it on the lattice, as well as presenting novel results of lattice QCD+QED simulations at finite temperature, focusing on understanding the role of the electromagnetic coupling in the thermodynamics of this system. We also discuss new features that arise in the combined theory and the challenges that these simulations entail, which require the development of new algorithms in order to understand the phase diagram of QCD+QED from first principles.

</details>


### [2] [Hadron spectroscopy of QC2D at finite density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29900)
*Kei Iida,Etsuko Itou,Kotaro Murakami,and Daiki Suenaga*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点蒙特卡洛模拟研究了低温下二色QCD（QC₂D）中重子化学势对强子质量谱的影响，发现超流相变前后介子质量序发生显著变化，并观测到U(1)₆对称性破缺的Nambu-Goldstone模质量趋近于零，同时给出部分手征对称性恢复的迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解致密QCD物质中强子谱演化及对称性破缺模式，特别是利用QC₂D作为可数值模拟的理论实验室来探索真实QCD在高重子密度下的非微扰行为。

Method: 在32⁴格点上、温度T≈40 MeV下进行二色QCD格点蒙特卡洛模拟，完整计算所有介子与双夸克关联函数（含不连通图），并外推至零二夸克源以提取U(1)₆ Nambu-Goldstone模质量。

Result: 1) 强子相中介子质量序类比于三色QCD；2) 超流相中σ与a₁通道变轻，π介子质量随密度上升；3) U(1)₆对称性破缺的Nambu-Goldstone模质量在零源极限下几乎为零；4) 手征伙伴关联函数显示部分手征对称性恢复迹象。

Conclusion: QC₂D在超流相中展现出与手征对称性破缺和重子超流协同相关的丰富谱结构，支持U(1)₆对称性自发破缺，并暗示手征对称性部分恢复，为理解高密度QCD提供关键基准。

Abstract: We investigate the chemical-potential dependence of hadron masses in two-color QCD (QC$_2$D) at low temperature using lattice Monte Carlo simulations. We calculate all possible meson and diquark correlation functions, including disconnected diagrams, on a $32^4$ lattice at $T\approx40\,\mathrm{MeV}$. In the hadronic phase, we obtain a meson mass hierarchy similar to that of three-color QCD. After the superfluid transition, the ordering changes: the $\sigma$ and $a_1$ channels become lighter, while the pion mass increases with density. We also examine the diquark source dependence of the Nambu--Goldstone mode of $U(1)_B$ symmetry breaking and find an almost vanishing mass in the superfluid phase after extrapolation to zero source. Finally, we compare correlation functions for chiral partners and discuss indications of partial chiral-symmetry restoration. This contribution is based on our recent study of hadron spectra in dense QC$_2$D~\cite{Iida:2026dmw}.

</details>


### [3] [Fermionic 2+1D SU(2) quantum link models: an ED/DMRG reliability study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28590)
*Daming Li*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文对2+1维SU(2)量子链模型（含动力学交错费米子）在小二维格点上，采用稀疏精确对角化（ED）与矩阵乘积态密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）两种希尔伯特空间方法进行系统性基准测试；验证了规范代数、高斯定律、厄米性、纯规范极限及MPO构造，并量化了不同尺寸（2×2至3×3）下方法的数值可靠性（精度达10⁻¹³–10⁻⁵），揭示了色中性（如半填充奇费米子数导致G²=3/4硬下界）对张量网络表现的关键影响，为非阿贝尔规范-费米子系统的可扩展模拟确立了可复现的精度基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔格点规范理论耦合动力学费米子是张量网络与量子模拟的核心挑战，但针对小系统的可控可靠性研究极为匮乏；亟需在严格可解的小尺度上建立方法学基准，以厘清数值误差来源（如截断、规范违背、对称性实现）并指导大规模模拟的设计。

Method: 采用两类互补希尔伯特空间方法：(i) 稀疏精确对角化（ED），显式处理跨格点Jordan–Wigner弦；(ii) 保持总费米子数守恒的矩阵乘积态DMRG，其哈密顿量由局域项构建矩阵乘积算符（MPO）；模型为SU(2)量子链模型，链接变量取五态{5}截断，费米子用双色四态格点表示；辅以fPEPS（满足精确高斯定律）作为独立验证基准。

Result: 2×2格点上ED与DMRG结果一致达10⁻¹³；2×3–2×5梯子系统误差≤10⁻⁵；3×3格点揭示色中性关键作用：半填充（奇费米子数）时G²=3/4为严格下界，引入单空穴或单双占据态恢复中性后，残差降至∼2×10⁻⁵（χ=2048）；3×2偶填充扇区获fPEPS独立验证；整体确立了非阿贝尔规范-费米子问题的可复现可靠性包络。

Conclusion: ED与守恒DMRG在中小尺度上具备亚10⁻⁵级可控精度，但色中性破缺（如奇费米子数）会强加物理硬约束，显著影响张量网络表现；大规模模拟需依几何与填充数自适应增大纠缠熵截断χ或改用更适配规范结构的张量几何（如fPEPS）。

Abstract: Non-Abelian lattice gauge theory with dynamical fermions is a central target for tensor-network and quantum-simulation methods, but controlled reliability studies on small systems are scarce. We benchmark two complementary Hilbert-space approaches for a 2+1D SU(2) quantum link model with one staggered fermion flavour on small two-dimensional lattices: sparse exact diagonalization (ED) and matrix-product-state density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG). The link variables use the five-state $\{5\}$ truncation, and fermions are represented by two-colour four-state sites. We implement ED with explicit cross-site Jordan--Wigner strings and DMRG with total-fermion-number conservation and a matrix product operator (MPO) built from local terms. We validate the link algebra, Gauss-law commutation, Hermiticity, the pure-gauge limit, and the MPO against ED component by component. The $2\times2$ methods agree to $10^{-13}$ and the $2\times3$--$2\times5$ ladders are controlled at or below the $10^{-5}$ level. The $3\times3$ geometry illustrates the role of colour neutrality: at half filling the odd fermion number makes $G^2=3/4$ the exact lower bound, while one hole or one doublon restores neutrality and lowers the residual to $\sim2\times10^{-5}$ at $\chi=2048$. An independent exact-Gauss fPEPS provides a reference for the even-filling $3\times2$ sector. The results define a reproducible reliability envelope for non-Abelian gauge-fermion benchmarks and show where larger-scale simulations require larger bond dimensions or different tensor-network geometries.

</details>


### [4] [Progress on finite temperature Sp(4) lattice gauge theory with matter fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30493)
*Alexis Verney-Provatas,Chris Allton,Ed Bennett,Luigi Del Debbio,Ryan C. Hill,Deog Ki Hong,Jong-Wan Lee,C.-J. David Lin,Biagio Lucini,Maurizio Piai,Davide Vadacchino*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了Sp(4)格点规范理论在有限温度下耦合两个动力学Wilson费米子的行为，旨在刻画其热相变性质，以评估其作为SIMP型复合暗物质模型所产生的原初随机引力波信号的可观测性。通过分析Polyakov圈及其磁化率在不同裸费米子质量和逆耦合常数下的有限体积标度行为，发现轻费米子区为交叉相变，重费米子区呈现一级相变迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定Sp(4)规范理论在早期宇宙中的热相变阶数，从而评估其产生的原初随机引力波信号强度，并为未来引力波实验提供理论灵敏度依据。

Method: 在小时间维格点（N_t较小）上扫描裸费米子质量m_f与逆规范耦合β=4/g^2构成的二维参数空间；利用多直方图重权法计算Polyakov圈磁化率峰值的有限体积标度行为，以此判别相变类型（一级相变 vs. 交叉）。

Result: 在轻费米子质量区域观察到磁化率峰值不随体积显著增强，支持交叉相变；在重费米子区域，峰值随体积增长明显，呈现一级相变的初步证据。

Conclusion: Sp(4) + 2F理论的热相变性质依赖于费米子质量：轻质量情形为平滑交叉，重质量情形趋向一级相变；该结果为后续在连续极限下系统研究Sp(2N)类理论的相结构与引力波产额提供了关键指引。

Abstract: We explore the finite temperature behaviour of the Sp(4) lattice gauge theory coupled to two dynamical Wilson fermions transforming in the fundamental representation. The continuum theory provides a realisation of composite dark matter within the Strongly Interacting Massive Particle (SIMP) paradigm. Its thermal phase transition in the early universe might have generated an observable relic stochastic gravitational wave signal. Evaluating the sensitivity of future gravitational wave experiments to this signal requires knowledge of the strength of the phase transition. We explore the two dimensional parameter space of the theory, defined by the bare fermion mass and inverse gauge coupling. For this exploratory study we focus on lattices with a small number of sites in the temporal dimension. We analyse the finite volume scaling behaviour of Polyakov loop observables. Specifically the scaling of the peak of the susceptibility, informed by multiple histogram reweighting, is used to discriminate between the presence of a phase transition or crossover. In the light fermion mass regime we find strong indications of a crossover, while for heavy fermions we find preliminary evidence of a first-order phase transition. These findings motivate further studies of the continuum limit for this and other Sp(2N) gauge theories.

</details>


### [5] [Identifying the conformal window by chiral phase boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30669)
*Jan Philipp Klinger,Reinhold Kaiser,Owe Philipsen,Jonas Schaible*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD模拟研究多味QCD的共形窗口边界，发现N_f=7时热致手征相变在连续极限手征极限下仍存在，而N_f=8时真空保持手征对称，支持N_f^* ∈ (7,8]。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分QCD-like理论与质量形变共形理论的关键在于热致手征相变是否在连续极限的手征极限下持续存在；需确定临界味数N_f^*以界定共形窗口。

Method: 采用非改进staggered费米子，在裸格点参数空间(N_τ, β, am, N_f)中系统绘制手征相图，对比N_f=7与N_f=8情形，并检验八味体系伪临界曲面是否符合共形超缩放律。

Result: N_f=7时热手征相变在a→0、m→0极限下持续存在；N_f=8时质量为零的连续极限理论在真空中保持手征对称；N_f=8的伪临界曲面满足共形超缩放关系。

Conclusion: 八味QCD位于共形窗口内，七味QCD则处于共形窗口外，故临界味数满足7 < N_f^* ≤ 8。

Abstract: Once the number of massless fermion flavours reaches a critical value $N_f^*$, many-flavour QCD enters the conformal window, where chiral symmetry remains unbroken in the vacuum. At non-zero quark mass, chiral symmetry is explicitly broken, and thermal chiral crossovers can also occur for $N_f\geq N_f^*$. Their presence alone therefore does not distinguish a QCD-like theory from a mass-deformed conformal theory. They differ, however, in whether a thermal transition persists in the continuum chiral limit. To address this, we map out the chiral phase boundaries of unimproved staggered fermions in the bare lattice parameter space $(N_\tau,\beta,am,N_f)$, comparing $N_f=7$ and $N_f=8$. Our results indicate that the thermal chiral transition persists in the continuum chiral limit for $N_f=7$, whereas the massless continuum theory with $N_f=8$ remains chirally symmetric in the vacuum. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the pseudo-critical surface for eight flavours observed for finite quark masses is consistent with conformal hyperscaling, providing further evidence that eight-flavour QCD lies within the conformal window and suggesting $7<N_f^*\leq8$.

</details>


### [6] [Leading-order dynamical QED corrections to the thermodynamics of isospin-asymmetric QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31087)
*Gergely Endrődi,Ádám Fischer,Wei-Ping Huang,Gergely Markó*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在格点上计算了动力学光子对非零同位旋化学势下QCD热力学的领头阶影响，重点研究带电π介子凝聚相变的性质；通过在e=0处对自由能密度f作电磁耦合e的展开，并测量∂²f/∂e²|ₑ₌₀，发现该系数在相变点μ_I^c处发生显著跃变，表明动力学光子可能将原本的二阶相变驱动为一阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD在非零同位旋化学势μ_I下存在二阶带电π介子凝聚相变；但引入动力学光子（QED）后，因夸克电荷不等导致作用量复化（符号问题），无法直接模拟；需探究光子如何改变相变阶数这一基本问题。

Method: 采用微扰展开法：在e=0处对自由能密度f作泰勒展开，提取领头阶系数∂²f/∂e²|ₑ₌₀；该系数由光子传播子与电磁流-流关联函数卷积给出；使用新提出的平面源（planar source）方法，在已有(2+1)-味纯QCD格点组态上测量该关联函数，同时扫描μ_I和π源λ，并外推λ→0。

Result: 在物理夸克质量及单一格点间距下，∂²f/∂e²|ₑ₌₀在μ_I < μ_I^c时与μ_I近似无关，而在μ_I ≥ μ_I^c时急剧上升；该跃变行为支持动力学光子诱导相变阶数提升（二阶→一阶）的理论预期。

Conclusion: 动力学光子对同位旋化学势驱动的π凝聚相变具有定性影响；∂²f/∂e²|ₑ₌₀的非解析行为是光子诱发一阶相变的关键证据；本工作为强相互作用物质中电磁修正的格点研究提供了首个定量基准。

Abstract: We present a first lattice calculation of the leading-order effect of dynamical photons on the thermodynamics of QCD at nonzero isospin chemical potential $\mu_I$, across the onset of charged-pion condensation. In QCD this onset is a second-order transition, which dynamical photons are expected, on general grounds, to drive first order. However, a direct simulation is not possible since unequal quark charges render the QCD+QED action complex at nonzero $\mu_I$. We therefore expand the free energy density $f$ in the electromagnetic coupling $e$ around $e=0$, where the sign problem is absent. The leading-order expansion coefficient, $\partial^2 f/\partial e^2|_{e=0}$, is the convolution of the photon propagator with the electromagnetic current-current correlator, which we measure with a newly proposed planar source on existing (2+1)-flavor pure-QCD ensembles at nonzero $\mu_I$ and pionic source $\lambda$. At physical quark masses and a single lattice spacing, after extrapolating to vanishing $\lambda$, we find that $\partial^2 f/\partial e^2|_{e=0}$ is, within errors, independent of $\mu_I$ below the onset and rises sharply across it.

</details>

### [7] [Frobenius $2$-algebras and $R$-matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29898)
*Hibiki Shibuya,Yuji Terashima*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入Frobenius 2-代数（Frobenius代数的范畴化推广），证明每个Frobenius 2-代数在其中心自同态幺半范畴中诱导一个满足Yang–Baxter方程的R-矩阵，并通过三个例子（辫子幺半范畴中的一对对象、具可逆对极的Hopf代数、有限维Hopf代数Heisenberg双重的表示）验证该构造；特别地，在第三个例子中复现了Kashaev的普适R-矩阵构造，从而实现了其范畴化推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Frobenius代数的代数结构提升至2-范畴层面，以统一解释不同背景下R-矩阵的起源，并为Kashaev构造提供更深层的范畴论基础。

Method: 利用2-代数（即具有适当双模结构的2-对象）与中心化子范畴（Z(C)或End_C(X)）之间的对应，结合Frobenius条件在2-层次上的对偶性与迹结构，构造满足Yang–Baxter方程的自然变换R。

Result: 建立了Frobenius 2-代数 → End_C(X)中满足YBE的R-矩阵的函子性对应；三个具体实例验证了该框架的广泛适用性；在Heisenberg双重表示范畴中严格导出Kashaev的R-矩阵。

Conclusion: Frobenius 2-代数是生成辫子结构（即解Yang–Baxter方程的R-矩阵）的自然高阶代数框架，为量子群、拓扑量子场论及低维拓扑中的R-矩阵构造提供了统一的范畴论语言。

Abstract: In this paper, introducing Frobenius $2$-algebras, which are categorical generalizations of Frobenius algebras, we show that every Frobenius $2$-algebra determines an $R$-matrix satisfying the Yang-Baxter equation in the monoidal category of endomorphisms of its underlying object. Moreover, we illustrate this construction through three examples: a pair of objects in a braided monoidal category, a Hopf algebra with invertible antipode, and representations of the Heisenberg double of a finite-dimensional Hopf algebra. In particular, in the third example, we recover Kashaev's construction of the universal $R$-matrix. Thus, our construction provides a categorical generalization of Kashaev's construction.

</details>


### [8] [Reproducing kernel and matrix representation of the deformed quantum optical density operator of $\mathcal{R}(p,q)-$super-coherent states on $\mathcal{B}_0\oplus\mathcal{B}_1$ fermionic quantum algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30576)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广传统相干态到R(p,q)-变形超对称框架，建立具备完备数学结构（如分辨率恒等式、再生核、量子化映射）的超相干态理论，并探索其在非对易几何与量子信息中的潜在应用。

Method: 采用R(p,q)-导数、角度积分与Berezin积分，在分级超Fock空间的四个扇区（玻色、φ₁-费米、φ₂-费米、双费米）中构造密度算符的对角表示；定义扇区再生核并验证其厄米性、正定性与矩阵乘法性质；实施Berezin量子化并计算可观测量的期望值与不确定性。

Abstract: Written in Lagrangian mass coordinates, the spherically symmetric Euler-Poisson equations for a polytropic gas become a single second-order equation for the radius $r(t,m)$ of the shell enclosing mass $m$, with adiabatic index $\gamma>1$ and entropy profile $S(m)$. We determine all point symmetries of this equation. Apart from time translation there is at most one scaling, except at the critical index $\gamma=4/3$, where a scaling exists for every entropy profile; these scalings are the entropy-fixed stabilisers of the two-parameter scaling group of the non-isentropic system, and we give the exact symmetry defect when $\gamma\neq4/3$. Noether's theorem singles out the profiles $S=Km^{14-10\gamma}$, for which a scaling is variational and gives a conservation law beyond energy; otherwise the scalings yield virial identities. Using the Briot-Bouquet theorem we prove that the regular centre of self-similar and homologous collapse carries a convergent analytic expansion with free central amplitude, and we derive a Puiseux expansion at the stellar surface whose exponent is the polytropic index $1/(\gamma-1)$. In mass coordinates a density singularity is a zero of $r_m$ or of $r$; for the first kind we prove a Hopf-type fold law and an exact shell-crossing criterion for free-falling dust. Finally, linear perturbations of the critical Einstein-de Sitter collapse separate exactly into spherical Bessel profiles, with exponents solving $3\kappa^2+\kappa-2+\mu=0$. All identities were checked by computer algebra, and the numerical statements rest on convergence studies of a second-order Lagrangian scheme, including a collapse with a free surface.

We show that all physically admissible spherical photon orbits outside the event horizon are radially unstable in the black-hole regime, whereas stable spherical photon orbits can exist in horizonless configurations. The unstable photon orbits determine the shadow, which for an edge-on observer is smaller and distorted compared with the Kerr shadow. For timelike motion, we identify marginally stable spherical orbits and determine the innermost stable spherical orbit (ISSO). We further examine the null, weak, strong, and dominant energy conditions, finding violations in the strong-field region but satisfaction of all four conditions asymptotically and throughout the exterior of every black-hole configuration. Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamics, dynamical stability, and phenomenological implications of Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (EGB) black holes in Dark Dimension scenarios with one or two micron-sized extra dimensions. For $M_*\sim 10$ TeV, we identify distinct collider benchmarks: a steeper energy scaling for the production cross section and a suppressed decay multiplicity ($\langle N \rangle \sim 4$). These provide clear experimental signatures distinguishing 6D EGB gravity from classical General Relativity, mapping the transition toward the string scale.

Abstract: We present analytical expressions for the energy and angular momentum fluxes radiated to infinity by a (nonspinning) point mass in an eccentric-equatorial orbit about a Kerr black hole. The analytical expressions are then validated with numerical comparisons against high-precision sample results from an independent Teukolsky code and with a broader set of numerically interpolated values derived from a flux data set produced with the same code. For $a=0.9M$, the numerically evaluated 10PN fluxes match current interpolated flux grids ($\sim10^{-7}$) for $p\gtrsim20$ to $75$ as $e$ ranges from $0.1$ to $0.7$, where they could replace Teukolsky-code results.

Abstract: Overlapping gravitational-wave signals can produce strong degeneracies in which a multi-source waveform is accurately represented by a single effective source. We formulate this source-confusion problem geometrically by treating the family of single-source waveforms as an embedded manifold in the noise-weighted detector Hilbert space. We introduce the distance-to-manifold statistic, defined as the minimum residual obtained by fitting a waveform with the single-source model, and show that its squared value is directly related to the maximized likelihood difference between two-source and single-source hypotheses in the noiseless limit. For two sources with nearby parameters, perturbative minimization demonstrates that the effective single-source fit absorbs all leading tangent-space deformations. The first irreducible residual lies in the normal space of the waveform manifold and is governed by its second fundamental form. We validate our approach using a six-parameter frequency-domain inspiral model with a LISA-like time-delay-interferometry response. Direct optimization reproduces the predicted quartic scaling over the numerically resolved regime, while two-dimensional confusion-zone maps reveal strongly anisotropic distinguishability boundaries. These results establish extrinsic waveform-manifold curvature as a coordinate-independent diagnostic of local multi-source confusion and provide a practical framework for identifying parameter-space directions in which overlapping gravitational-wave sources are most difficult to resolve.

Abstract: This is the Scientific Added Value Chapter of the Einstein Telescope (ET) Geometry Comparison Report, commissioned from the ET Organization by the ET Board of Governmental Representatives. We compare three configurations: a single-site 10-km triangle ($\Delta$) and two 15-km L-shaped detectors (2L) at widely separated sites, either nearly aligned or misaligned. We use metrics representative of the ET science case: compact-binary populations, dense matter, cosmography, stochastic backgrounds, multimessenger astronomy, fundamental physics, and continuous and burst sources. The $\Delta$ localizes sources better than the aligned 2L and performs comparably to both 2L configurations for continuous and burst sources and at kilohertz frequencies. For distance and sky localization, the FIM cannot capture multimodality and long-range correlations, leading to larger discrepancies, which however do not affect the ranking. Adding Cosmic Explorer to the network improves the reach of all three ET configurations, especially in volume localization and characterization of high-redshift sources. Abstract: We investigate the geodesic structure and the stability of circular orbits around a Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a Kiselev anisotropic fluid, characterised by the equation-of-state parameter $\omega$ and the normalisation parameter $N$. In contrast, for $\omega\geq-1/3$ stable circular orbits exist at all radii beyond the ISCO, whose location varies with $N$. We also derive a closed-form expression for the squared coordinate-time Lyapunov exponent $(\lambda_t^T)^2$, valid for arbitrary $\omega$, whose sign changes exactly at the ISCO and OSCO, so that the Lyapunov criterion reproduces the boundaries of stable circular motion. Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) observed by LISA will probe the environments of massive black holes (BHs), including the dark matter (DM) overdensities expected to form around them. The DM is modeled as a spike grown adiabatically from an initial Hernquist profile, and the momentum transfer is obtained by integrating relativistic scattering and capture cross sections over the full, generically anisotropic phase space seen by the secondary. Near the innermost stable circular orbit of rapidly spinning BHs $(a\gtrsim 0.37)$, however, frame dragging forces bound DM particles to co-rotate, so that the secondary is overtaken by the surrounding medium: the friction weakens and eventually reverses sign where the DM density peaks. Abstract: Orbital eccentricity of binary neutron star systems is a key parameter to reveal their formation history. Abstract: Astrometric measurements have emerged as a viable probe of gravitational waves (GWs), complementing pulsar timing searches by providing overlapping and higher GW frequency coverage. Restoring the signature phase gives $\rho=0.098$, in agreement with the TOTEM and ATLAS measurements, and fills the zero without moving it; a two-component opacity reduces the excess at the dip from a factor of 15 to a factor of 2 without reproducing the dip-bump structure and the large-$|t|$ tail simultaneously; a two-channel Good-Walker eikonal on a Gaussian profile moves the minimum further away from the measured dip.

Abstract: The QCD axion is a dark matter (DM) candidate particle which can also solve the long-standing strong charge-parity problem. The Axion Dark Matter eXperiment (ADMX) searches for resonantly-enhanced conversion of axion DM into photons in a strong magnetic field. Previous versions of the experiment probed most QCD axion DM from 2.7 $\mu$eV to 4.2 $\mu$eV, with ongoing and planned runs poised to cover up to 8 $\mu$eV (2 GHz). We present the design of a haloscope with 18 cavities 1 m in length read out using individual low-noise amplifier chains and digital coherent signal combination called ADMX extended frequency range (EFR). We discuss details on magnet and cryogenic infrastructure, cavity design, cold electronics and efficiency of coherent signal combination. We use detailed cavity and electronics simulations, as well as diagnostic data from previous ADMX runs, to estimate frequency-dependent instantaneous scan rate. When paired with a comprehensive study of experiment downtime, a projection of final axion coupling sensitivity is produced. Under the stated assumptions, the projected sensitivity is projected to reach below the DFSZ axion benchmark model from 8 $\mu$eV to 16 $\mu$eV (2 GHz to 4 GHz) in 36 months.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单洛伦兹不变本征（LIF）末态相空间定义，用于ATLAS实验中H→4ℓ道的STXS截面测量，以消除有效场论（EFT）对衰变端接受度的依赖，使重建效率修正近似EFT无关，从而支持无模型依赖的EFT解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATLAS在H→ZZ*→4ℓ道中使用的传统STXS分析依赖于基于标准模型衰变运动学定义的衰变端本征接受度，该接受度在EFT框架下随Wilson系数变化，导致EFT解释需复杂且模型依赖的修正。

Method: 引入单洛伦兹不变本征（LIF）衰变区域，将EFT依赖的接受度吸收到信号参数化中；利用√s=13.6 TeV、165 fb⁻¹的MC模拟验证LIF接受度的生产无关性与EFT稳定性，并量化重建效率对Wilson系数的残余依赖。

Result: LIF接受度在SM下为A^LIF_SM = 0.8546，生产无关性达0.07%，STXS箱间稳定性达0.19%；<1%事例来自LIF外；重建效率在探测的Wilson系数范围内最大变化仅6%。

Conclusion: 现有STXS测量可直接重标至LIF相空间，实现EFT解释中无需EFT依赖的重建效率修正，提升理论解释的普适性与稳健性。

Abstract: ATLAS measurements of Higgs boson production-mode cross-sections and Simplified Template Cross Sections (STXS), $\sigma\cdot B$, in the $H\to ZZ^{*}\to 4\ell$ decay channel rely on selection factors that fold in a decay-side fiducial acceptance evaluated with Standard Model decay kinematics. In an effective-field-theory interpretation that acceptance depends on the Wilson coefficients themselves. We recast these production-side $\sigma\cdot B$ results into a single Lorentz-invariant fiducial (LIF) decay region defined by the $H\to 4\ell$ decay, absorbing the EFT-dependent fiducial acceptance into the signal parametrisation, while the remaining reconstruction-efficiency corrections are shown to be largely EFT-independent. Using the Higgs boson signal Monte Carlo simulation at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 165 fb$^{-1}$, the LIF acceptance in the Standard Model is found to be $A^{LIF}_{SM}=0.8546$, production-independent to 0.07% and stable across STXS bins to 0.19%. Less than 1% of the reconstructed signal events originate from outside the LIF region, and the reconstruction efficiency for events in the region changes by at most 6% over the probed ranges of the EFT Wilson coefficients, as in the fiducial phase space of the published analysis. Consequently, the existing inclusive STXS cross-section measurements can be rescaled to the corresponding LIF fiducial phase space, allowing for EFT interpretations without EFT-dependent corrections for the reconstruction efficiency.

</details>

Conclusion: 所提出的可微分代理框架为闪烁体探测器光学响应快速建模与读出结构协同设计提供了坚实基础，显著提升了光导几何优化的效率与物理保真度。

Abstract: Optimizing light-guide geometries for scintillator-based imaging detectors requires repeated photon-transport evaluations, making direct Monte Carlo simulation computationally costly. Differentiable surrogates can accelerate these evaluations and support detector readout co-design aimed at improving information extraction from particle interactions. In this work we describe a surrogate model for photon transport. We develop it starting from a modular, event-driven Monte Carlo model for the generation and transport of scintillation photons, and a reciprocal Monte Carlo construction (an adjoint model) of a spatially and temporally resolved optical response field. The latter evaluates the collectible light output and its temporal structure from a scintillator volume by launching weighted photon trajectories from the light-guide readout face. We apply this approach to a scintillator cell with two mirrored, curved, single-clad light guides. We employ the adjoint model to train a geometry-conditioned neural surrogate that predicts collection probabilities and conditional transport-time distributions across a constrained guide-design family. The surrogate is natively differentiable, lending itself to be an element of a differentiable pipeline for gradient-based optimization. A forward-reciprocal benchmark and validation on held-out geometries establish the accuracy of the trained model and identify its limitations. The developed tools provide a foundation for rapid optical-response studies and detector readout co-design.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对窄共振与非共振振幅干涉情形下无bin似然拟合的高效计算方法，通过从概率密度中减去单极点项以解析处理共振贡献，剩余平滑部分则在仅由探测器分辨率决定的网格上数值卷积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窄共振宽度远小于探测器分辨率时，直接对真值级概率密度与探测器响应做卷积计算代价过高，导致无bin似然拟合不可行。

Method: 对每个共振引入一个简单极点（simple-pole）项并从原始概率密度中减去；该极点项解析表征共振贡献；剩余平滑部分在适配分辨率的粗网格上进行数值卷积；方法适用于多维密度及仅定义在实轴上的密度。

Result: 在两个基准问题中验证了该方法具有高精度、显著降低计算成本（避免高分辨率网格）、且统计有效性（如参数估计偏差与覆盖概率）保持良好。

Conclusion: 极点减法是一种通用、鲁棒且可扩展的技术，适用于实验粒子物理中含极点概率密度的无bin似然分析，尤其适合存在窄共振干涉的场景。

Abstract: Unbinned likelihood fits to detector-level data become prohibitively expensive when narrow resonances interfere with a nonresonant amplitude. The convolution of the truth-level probability density with the detector resolution must then resolve the resonances, whose widths are much smaller than the resolution. We propose a simple approach that subtracts one simple-pole term per resonance from the probability density. This term captures the resonant contribution in closed form, and the smooth remainder is convolved numerically on a grid whose spacing is set by the resolution alone. The approach applies to multidimensional densities and to densities known only on the real axis. Using two benchmark problems, we quantify the accuracy, computational cost, and statistical impact of the approach. We emphasise that the subtraction is a general approach to the computational problems of unbinned likelihoods in the presence of poles in their probability density and that it is applicable to a wide variety of problems in experimental particle physics.

</details>

Result: 所得符号学生模型含577个运算符，在测试集上复现教师模型判别能力，并泛化至四个新物理基准场景；FPGA实现仅需9个时钟周期（42.8 ns），资源占用仅为0.81% LUTs和0.44% DSP slices。

Abstract: At the CERN Large Hadron Collider, anomaly detection techniques are becoming popular alternatives to traditional signature-aware new physics searches. For this approach to avoid any bias induced by the real-time event selection applied in the trigger system of ATLAS and CMS, it should be implemented before the first stage of selection, the Level-1 Trigger. This requires deciding to accept or reject each collision within a few microseconds, a latency budget that excludes the most expressive anomaly-detection models. Knowledge distillation has successfully addressed this issue by replacing a complex anomaly-detection model with a lighter student network. This work takes the compression one step further, distilling an anomaly detection model based on a normalising flow into an explicit mathematical formula. Using the SymbolNet symbolic-regression framework with adaptive pruning, the student is a learned functional form based on a chosen subset of the inputs, providing at once feature selection and compression through knowledge distillation. The final expression contains 577 operations and matches the teacher's discrimination on a test dataset, generalising to four benchmark new physics scenarios. Implemented on a Xilinx Virtex UltraScale+ VU13P FPGA, the symbolic student has a nine-cycle latency, corresponding to 42.8\,ns at the post-route maximum clock frequency, and uses 0.81\% of the device's LUTs and 0.44\% of its DSP slices.

</details>

Result: transverse区带电粒子密度ρ_ch与∑p_T密度ρ_ΣpT均在p_T^leading ≈ 2–5 GeV/c区间快速上升，p_T^leading > 5 GeV/c后进入平台区；该阈值显著高于此前7 TeV和13 TeV结果，表明UE起始动量尺度随√s升高而增大；PYTHIA 8 (Monash)高估低p_T^leading区，EPOS4整体更接近数据。

Conclusion: UE活动存在清晰的能量依赖性，其特征动量尺度随√s增大而提升，暗示软与硬过程耦合机制在更高能量下发生演化；当前主流模型仍难以同时描述低-中p_T^leading区的非微扰动力学与硬散射触发下的集体响应。

Abstract: This contribution reports measurements characterizing the underlying event at midrapidity in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV, based on LHC Run 3 data collected by ALICE. The charged-particle number and summed transverse-momentum densities are measured as a function of the leading-particle transverse momentum ($p_{\rm T}^{\rm leading}$) in the toward, transverse, and away regions, defined relative to the azimuthal direction of the leading charged particle. The particle densities in the transverse region are observed to increase steeply at low $p_{\rm T}^{\rm leading}$, followed by a plateau for $p_{\rm T}^{\rm leading}>5$ GeV/$c$. The results are compared with measurements at lower center-of-mass energies to illustrate the energy dependence of the underlying-event activity, and with predictions from PYTHIA 8 and EPOS4.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于规范化的 $L_{\mu} + L_{\tau}$ 对称性的scotogenic模型，其自发破缺两单位后残留物质宇称 $M_P$，从而稳定中性轻标量暗物质候选者，并兼容热退耦 relic 密度、电弱精度实验及轻子型 $Z'$ 探测约束。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能同时解释中微子质量起源与暗物质候选者的自洽scotogenic框架，且避免传统 $L_e - L_{\mu}$ 或 $B-L$ 模型中的实验冲突；引入 $L_{\mu}+L_{\tau}$ 规范对称性以自然导出残余离散对称性 $M_P$ 并抑制味改变中性流（FCNC）过程。

Method: 构造包含右手中微子、带电/中性标量单态及额外 $U(1)_{L_{\mu}+L_{\tau}}$ 规范场的扩展标准模型；通过双标量真空期望值实现 $U(1)_{L_{\mu}+L_{\tau}}$ 破缺并生成残余 $Z_2$ 物质宇称 $M_P$；计算 scotogenic 一圈图对中微子质量矩阵的贡献；结合热退耦暗物质丰度、LEP/SLD 电弱精度数据、$Z'$ 动力学混合 $\epsilon$ 约束及 LHC 轻子末态搜寻进行参数空间扫描。

Result: 确认 singlet-like CP-even 标量作为可行暗物质候选者，其质量在 $10~\\text{GeV} \\lesssim m_S \\lesssim 1~\\text{TeV}$ 区间可满足 Planck 测得的 $\Omega h^2 \\approx 0.12$；$Z'$ 质量 $M_{Z'} \\gtrsim 500~\\text{GeV}$ 且 $\epsilon \\lesssim 10^{-2}$ 可规避电弱精度实验限制；可见末态（如 $pp \\to Z' \\to \\mu^+\\mu^-$）与不可见末态（$pp \\to Z' \\to \\chi \\chi$）在不同耦合区域具有可观测信号。

Conclusion: 该模型首次将 gauged $L_{\mu}+L_{\tau}$ 对称性、scotogenic 机制与残余物质宇称 $M_P$ 统一，为中微子质量、暗物质及轻子味物理提供协同解释；其预测的 $Z'$ 与 singlet 标量暗物质在 HL-LHC 和未来 $e^+e^-$ 对撞机上具备可检验性。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce the first scotogenic model based on a gauged $L_{\mu} + L_{\tau}$ symmetry whose spontaneous breaking by two units leaves a remnant matter parity $M_P$. The resulting light active neutrinos are of Majorana nature and the lightest electrically neutral state mediating the scotogenic loop is stabilized by $M_P$ and can be identified as a weakly interacting massive particle dark matter candidate. We analyze the phenomenology of a predominantly singlet-like CP-even scalar dark matter candidate and the representative regions of parameter space compatible with the observed thermal relic abundance. The $Z'$ boson is compatible with a broad range of parameters relevant for dark matter. In particular, electroweak precision constraints do not lead to significant tensions for kinetic mixing values up to $\epsilon\sim 10^{-2}$. We discuss the implications of existing searches for leptophilic gauge bosons and the conditions under which visible and invisible collider signatures become relevant.

Conclusion: 粲夸克不改变手征恢复与退禁闭的平滑交叉本质，其热学效应可视为对理想夸克气体极限的修正，适用于高精度QCD相结构建模与重离子碰撞信号解读。

Abstract: Using the Polyakov-loop extended linear-sigma model (PLSM), we investigate strongly interacting matter at finite temperature and baryon density by comparing the three-flavor (u,d,s) and four-flavor (u,d,s,c) formulations on equal footing, with the aim of isolating the thermal contribution of the charm quark. The model describes chiral dynamics through explicit and spontaneous symmetry breaking and reproduces the vacuum spectra of scalar and pseudoscalar mesons. Confinement is incorporated through a logarithmic Polyakov-loop potential calibrated to the continuum lattice-QCD equation of state (EoS). Within the mean-field approximation, we calculate light-, strange-, and charm-quark condensates, the Polyakov loop, and bulk thermodynamic observables, including pressure, trace anomaly, and entropy density, over broad ranges of temperature and baryon chemical potential. We also determine the chiral and deconfinement pseudocritical boundaries and represent them as phase boundaries and three-dimensional order-parameter surfaces. After tuning the Polyakov sector to lattice-QCD pressure and trace-anomaly data, the resulting entropy density agrees with HotQCD 2+1(+1)-flavor results throughout the crossover region. At zero baryon density, the non-strange condensate melts at Tc = 210 MeV in SU(3) and 218 MeV in SU(4), with curvature parameters kappa=0.018 and 0.017. Because mc>>Tc, charm remains thermally suppressed across the crossover. After normalization to the corresponding Stefan-Boltzmann limits, the SU(3) and SU(4) EoS nearly coincide below T~2Tc. Charm mainly contributes through a higher ideal-gas limit, a gradual high-temperature tail, and the flavor ordering u,d->s->c. For all baryon chemical potentials accessible within mean-field theory, the hadron-parton transition remains a smooth crossover.

Abstract: We compute the quark and gluon gravitational form factors $A$, $J$ and $D$ of the nucleon in three-point QCD sum rules at next-to-leading order in $\alpha_s$ for the quark and gluon energy-momentum tensors (EMT), treating both at the same order in the $\overline{\rm MS}$ scheme. The nucleon EMT also tests the method, showing where the sum rules are reliable and why. Protected estimators of $D$ and of the trace form factor $\bar c$ require antisymmetric Borel moments, since none with equal Borel masses alone is protected. The $\mathcal{O}(\alpha_s)$ corrections reduce $A_{\rm tot}$ by $32$--$40\%$, $J_{\rm tot}$ by $22$--$27\%$ and $|D_{\rm tot}|$ by $21$--$27\%$, since the perturbative share of the two-point function is much larger. The total form factors agree with lattice QCD for $1\le Q^2\le2.5$~GeV$^2$. At $Q^2=1.5$~GeV$^2$, $A_{\rm tot}=0.337\pm0.113$, $J_{\rm tot}=0.206\pm0.100$ and $D_{\rm tot}=-0.206\pm0.121$. $D$ is the most reliable result: its correlator is mostly perturbative, and its quark and gluon parts agree with the lattice. In $A$ and $J$, a local gluon-condensate term that does not fall with $Q^2$ is the largest, and their gluon shares are well below the lattice, possibly because the gluon EMT couples at this order only through gluon exchange and the condensate. A working region exists only near $\beta=1$, and the constant part of transitions cannot be separated within the Borel windows. The individual conservation identities deviate at one point by two standard deviations, consistent with a missing finite term in the $g_{\mu\nu}$ structure, and $\bar c$ is therefore given at leading order with condensates.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate how independent information at finite chiral chemical potential $\mu_5$ constrains pseudocritical-temperature responses in the Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model under a magnetic field. Vacuum calibration alone leaves the coefficient $c_r$ of the additional interaction $\delta\Omega_r=c_r\mu_5^2(M-m_0)^2$ undetermined. We compare soft-cutoff (SC) and Pauli-Villars (PV) vacuum regularizations, each combined with the direct treatment (D) or the magnetic finite-difference scheme (MFDS). The reference SC and PV treatments exhibit opposite finite-$\mu_5$ responses already at $eB=0$. Matching the $eB=0$ scalar-susceptibility peak-temperature ratio predicted by unitarized chiral perturbation theory gives $c_{\mathrm{SC}}=-0.07112$ and $c_{\mathrm{PV}}=0.22479$. Holding these coefficients fixed at $eB=0.1,0.2,0.4$ GeV$^2$ reduces the four-treatment spread of the normalized finite-$\mu_5$-induced mass-inflection temperature change by factors of approximately $7.5$-$15.3$ at $\mu_5=0.15$ GeV and $18.1$-$32.6$ at $\mu_5=0.30$ GeV. For the distinct scalar-susceptibility peak-temperature ratio at $\mu_5=0.15$ GeV, the spread decreases by factors of approximately $74$, $27$, and $14$ at the same magnetic fields, respectively. The reduction in regularization-dependent finite-$\mu_5$ responses thus persists at the sampled nonzero magnetic fields without further matching.

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在$P(X)$类规范k-essence与k-chameleon模型中实现希格斯场经典化的可能性，发现由于屏蔽区域内强耦合失效，Vainshtein屏蔽无法建立，故该类模型不支持希格斯经典化；但作者强调此结论不构成对希格斯经典化的普适否定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电弱希格斯扇区中的小等级问题（little hierarchy problem），探索经典化机制作为非威尔逊紫外完备方案在希格斯物理中的适用性。

Method: 分析$P(X)$类规范k-essence与k-chameleon模型中Vainshtein屏蔽的可行性，考察其在尖锐局域源附近的动力学行为，特别关注屏蔽区域内的强耦合尺度是否被压低至失效。

Result: $P(X)$模型无法支持Vainshtein屏蔽，即使引入规范动能函数以协同chameleon屏蔽，仍因屏蔽区内强耦合提前发生而失败。

Conclusion: 该类模型不适合作为希格斯经典化的有效框架，但该否定结果仅限于所研究的具体模型族，不具普适排除意义。

Abstract: Classicalization is a non-Wilsonian framework in which an effective field theory self-completes above its strong-coupling scale by generating semiclassical objects that decay into many soft quanta, thereby realizing ultraviolet-infrared mixing. A key requirement of this mechanism is that the force carrier be lighter than the strong-coupling scale. A natural application would be to embed classicalization in the electroweak Higgs sector to address the little hierarchy problem. In this work, we investigate whether Higgs classicalization can occur within the $P(X)$ class of gauged k-essence and k-chameleon models. For classicalization to occur, the theory must support Vainshtein screening around a sharply localized source. Our results show that it fails to do so, even when one attempts to engineer a symbiosis with chameleon screening through a gauge kinetic function, owing to a strong-coupling breakdown inside the screening region. These $P(X)$ theories therefore fail to provide a suitable framework for Higgs classicalization. Nevertheless, we emphasize that our results, obtained for a specific class of models, should not be interpreted as a no-go theorem for Higgs classicalization.

</details>


### [95] [New analytic constraints for the soft anomalous dimension bootstrap at three loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31168)
*Einan Gardi,Luke Lippstreu,Andrew J. McLeod,Laura Walsh,Zehao Zhu,Enrico Zunino*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过研究费曼积分的幺正割与顺序不连续性之间的关系，推导出软反常维数的新约束条件，并利用这些约束在质量为零和单质量情况下实现了四极函数的三圈自举构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The soft anomalous dimension governs the universal long-distance singularities of gauge theory amplitudes. It exhibits a highly constrained analytic structure and has been shown to be amenable to bootstrap methods. In this paper we revisit the relation between unitarity cuts and sequential discontinuities of Feynman integrals and use it to derive new constraints on the soft anomalous dimension. At three loops, we show that certain sequential discontinuities in non-overlapping two-particle channels vanish for every web contributing to the quadrupole function that correlates four coloured partons. These constraints hold for arbitrary parton masses, and we explicitly verify them for the known massless and one-mass cases. We then demonstrate their constraining power by substantially strengthening the massless bootstrap and carrying out the first bootstrap construction of the one-mass quadrupole function. The new constraints are largely complementary to those arising from the absence of unphysical branch points, symmetries, and collinear and Regge limits. With their help, we determine each quadrupole function within the assumed function space up to a single coefficient, which we fix by matching to existing diagrammatic results. These results open the way to bootstrapping the three-loop soft anomalous dimension for amplitudes with several massive partons.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有氙实验在亚GeV暗物质-电子散射探测中受限于单电子本底过高，导致低质量区灵敏度严重下降；传统截断方法损失大量曝光量，且信噪比受S ≲ B限制。

Abstract: There is high interest in improving experimental sensitivity to sub-GeV dark matter (DM) that scatters with electrons, as compelling theoretical models seem within reach. At the lowest DM masses, most of the expected signal lies in the single-electron regime, but so do large backgrounds. Xenon experiments therefore either do not yet search there at all or, as in XENONnT, apply cuts so severe that only $\sim$$10^{-4}$ of the exposure is retained, with sensitivity set by the criterion $S \lesssim B$, where those are the signal and background counts. We advocate that xenon detectors instead utilize most of their dark-electron rate (i.e., the total raw electron hit rate) and perform an annual-modulation search, for which the sensitivity scales as $S \lesssim \sqrt{B}$. Modern DAQs already record this rate, continuously in triggerless systems such as the XENONnT and PandaX-4T DAQs, and within trigger windows in LZ, so no new hardware is required. Using measured dark-electron rates, we estimate the sensitivity of XENONnT, LZ, PandaX-4T, PandaX-xT, and XLZD. We find that dramatic gains in sensitivity are possible, motivating new analyses of present and future xenon data.

</details>

Abstract: Vector boson scattering is a powerful tool to test the mechanism of electroweak symmetry breaking.
