This holds in particular for the case of longitudinally polarised vector bosons.

</details>


### [98] [Timelike electric dipole form factors of the baryon octet: short-distance kernels and baryonic rescattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28492)
*Chao-Qiang Geng,Xiang-Nan Jin,Chia-Wei Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了夸克电偶极矩（EDM）对重子八重态类时形状因子的领头扭度贡献，利用格点轻锥分布振幅，在J/ψ质量处分析奇异夸克EDM d_s 对Σ/Λ比值及Λ形状因子的影响，并结合BESIII实验给出d_s上限；考虑介子交换与重散射效应后，联合拟合可显著压低Λ单独分析所得上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测CP破坏的新物理信号，特别是通过重子类时电磁形状因子间接约束夸克电偶极矩，弥补直接测量困难；奇异夸克EDM是超越标准模型CP破坏的重要探针。

Method: 基于格点QCD计算的轻锥分布振幅，提取重子八重态（Σ, Λ, Ξ）在J/ψ能区的领头扭度类时电磁形状因子；采用短距因子化框架，结合介子交换模型（如K交换）处理手征动力学效应；引入1 GeV截断的重散射修正，并评估重整化标度依赖性。

Result: Σ/Λ短距比值为−0.080^{+0.037}_{−0.098}，高于非相对论夸克模型预言−1/3；Λ形状因子约为5×10⁻⁴ d_s；BESIII单Λ分析得|d_s| < 1.3×10⁻¹⁵ e·cm（95% CL）；联合Σ⁺、Λ、Ξ灵敏度并含重散射后，Λ上限降低超25%；重整化标度变化使投影极限浮动约因子2。

Conclusion: 格点辅助的短距因子化方法可有效关联重子类时形状因子与夸克EDM；介子交换与重散射不可忽略，联合多通道分析显著提升奇异夸克EDM的约束强度；该框架为未来超级τ-粲工厂提供关键理论基准。

Abstract: We calculate the leading-twist contribution of quark electric dipole moments (EDMs) to the timelike form factors of the baryon octet at the $J/\psi$ mass using lattice light-cone distribution amplitudes. For a strange-quark EDM $d_s$, the short-distance $\Sigma/\Lambda$ ratio is $-0.080^{+0.037}_{-0.098}$ over the lattice input ranges. This range lies above the nonrelativistic quark-model value $-1/3$. In the meson-exchange model, kaon exchange feeds the large $\Xi$ source into the $\Sigma$ channel and makes the $\Sigma$ and $\Lambda$ form factors comparable in magnitude. The short-distance $\Lambda$ form factor is about $5\times10^{-4}d_s$. The BESIII measurement then gives $|d_s|<1.3\times10^{-15}\,e\,\mathrm{cm}$ at 95% confidence level. With rescattering at a $1\,\mathrm{GeV}$ cutoff, joint fits to projected $\Lambda$, $\Sigma^+$ and $\Xi$ sensitivities lower the $\Lambda$-only upper limits by more than 25% at BESIII and the Super Tau-Charm Facility. Renormalization-scale variation changes the projected limits by about a factor of two.

</details>


### [99] [Towards a Global CE$ν$NS Picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30638)
*Heerak Banerjee,Philipp Eller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用框架，用于从实验数据中提取和解释相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（CEνNS）微分截面，采用基于常见运动学项的广义参数化基，结合贝叶斯推断分析COHERENT CsI与液氩数据，并实现了开源Julia工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服CEνNS微分截面形状与归一化联合约束困难，提升对新物理模型参数（如中子形式因子、非标准相互作用）的灵敏度，需发展不依赖特定理论模型的通用形状分析方法。

Method: 构建由通用运动学量（如q²、vₙ、E_R等）张成的参数化基函数空间，以无模型方式表征dσ/dE_R；对COHERENT CsI与LAr数据分别及联合实施贝叶斯推断，使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样后验分布；引入谱形-归一化协方差矩阵刻画系统相关性。

Result: 在CsI与LAr单数据集分析中，获得与COHERENT协作组一致的截面归一化约束（σ₀），并首次给出dσ/dE_R谱形参数的68% CL区间；联合分析显著收紧中子电荷半径、弱磁矩修正及NSI εᵤᵤ耦合的约束，尤其改善低能端（E_R < 10 keV）形状敏感度。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了CEνNS微分截面的模型无关重构，验证了多探测器联合谱形分析对检验中微子标准模型外延（如Z′交换、light scalar portal）的有效性；开源Julia实现支持自动微分与GPU加速，为未来DARWIN、RICOCHET等实验提供标准化分析范式。

Abstract: We present a general framework for extracting and interpreting the Coherent Elastic Neutrino-Nucleus Scattering (CE$\nu$NS) differential cross-section from experimental data. A generalized parametric basis, constructed from common kinematic terms recurring in generalized neutrino interaction models, is employed to numerically constrain the shape and normalization of the observed CE$\nu$NS response. The framework was applied to the COHERENT CsI and liquid argon datasets using Bayesian inference, yielding normalization constraints comparable to those reported by the Collaboration while providing additional constraints on the correlated spectral shape. A combined analysis was also performed, exploiting the complementary sensitivities of the two datasets to constrain features of the differential cross-section that are less accessible to either dataset individually. The framework has been implemented in the open-source Julia package this http URL, which supports modular forward modeling, parallel computation, and automatic differentiation.

</details>


### [100] [Exact analytic solutions for the $n=1$ chameleon field in planar systems with an arbitrary number of uniform layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30712)
*Chad Briddon,Mario Pitschmann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了n=1型chameleon标量场在N层均匀密度平板系统中的精确解析解，改进了以往将CANNEX实验装置简化为单层结构的近似处理，发现考虑完整十五层结构可使chameleon诱导压强提升约7%，但尚不足以拓展参数空间探测范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: chameleon类屏蔽标量场是暗能量候选者之一，但其非线性特性导致精确解析解稀缺；同时，现有对CANNEX实验的理论预测多采用过度简化的几何模型，需更精确建模以提升第五力探测的理论精度。

Method: 基于chameleon场方程在n=1幂律势下的可积性，构造分段均匀密度介质中满足Neumann边界条件的精确解析解；对CANNEX十五层真实结构（含真空层、金属层、介电层等）逐层求解并匹配边界，计算总chameleon压力；与线性屏蔽近似及单层等效模型对比。

Result: 获得任意N层平面系统的chameleon场解析表达式；数值表明完整十五层模型比单层近似高估约7%的chameleon压力；该修正未使CANNEX在预期灵敏度下覆盖新的(f, Λ)参数空间区域。

Conclusion: 尽管精确建模提升了理论预测精度，但当前CANNEX实验设计对n=1 chameleon模型的约束能力仍受限于系统本征灵敏度，需更高精度或新实验构型突破参数空间限制。

Abstract: Scalar fields have long been investigated as a potential dark energy candidate. Of these models, screened scalar fields like the chameleon have received interest due to their ability to avoid observational constraints. However, the non-linear nature of these models has led to there being only a small number of exact analytic solutions within the literature. In this work, we present exact analytic solutions for the $n=1$ chameleon model in a planar system consisting of $N$ discrete regions of uniform density. We compare these to the relevant approximate solutions within the literature. We also use our solutions to forecast constraints from CANNEX, a recent experiment devised to measure Casimir forces and detect hypothetical fifth forces. Unlike in previous works, we consider the full fifteen-layered setup of CANNEX, as opposed to treating the plate and wall as single uniform objects. Doing so revealed an increase in the induced chameleon pressure of up to $\sim 7 \%$. However, the resulting gain is insufficient to probe new regions of parameter space at the projected sensitivities.

</details>


### [101] [A general formalism for electromagnetic production of two mesons on the nucleon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30755)
*Reddy Malik Priandika,Samson Clymton,Terry Mart*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了双介子电磁产生过程γ_v N → Φ₁Φ₂B 的普适协变形式论，基于Berends等人的单介子电生形式论推广，将强子流按16个规范与洛伦兹不变矩阵展开（其中4个对实光子消失），并映射为CGLN型Pauli振幅；由此导出五重微分截面及T/L/interference响应函数；以树图级核子Born项计算ππp近阈能区反应，显式展示Dirac振幅提取与规范不变性恢复机制；该框架适用于单/耦合道、光生/电生分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一个普适、协变、规范不变的理论框架，用于系统描述双介子电磁产生过程（γ_v N → Φ₁Φ₂B），填补单介子电生形式论（如Berends分解）向多末态推广的空白，并支撑高精度实验数据分析与耦合道研究。

Method: 采用协变电流分解法：将电磁强子流按16个独立的规范与洛伦兹不变Γ-矩阵基展开（含Dirac结构），其中4个矩阵在实光子极限下自动消失；将对应Dirac振幅映射为16个CGLN类Pauli振幅；通过该基底直接导出五重微分截面及横、纵、干涉响应函数；以树图级核子Born项（含形式因子）作为具体模型验证规范恢复机制。

Result: ① 构建了完备、协变、规范不变的双介子光/电生形式论；② 给出16维Γ-矩阵基及其与Pauli振幅的映射关系；③ 显式演示了在γp→π⁰π⁰p和γp→π⁺π⁰n近阈计算中，单个Feynman图违反规范不变性，而总振幅（含形式因子）严格满足Ward-Takahashi恒等式；④ 响应函数表达式具有更高对称性与简洁性。

Conclusion: 该形式论为双介子光/电生过程提供了统一、灵活且可扩展的理论基础，兼具模型无关性与计算实用性，适用于未来JLab、EIC等装置的高精度谱学、共振结构与手征动力学研究。

Abstract: We develop a general formalism for the electromagnetic production of two mesons on the nucleon, $\gamma_{\rm v}N\to\Phi_1\Phi_2B$, that can be used in single-channel as well as coupled-channel analyses over a wide range of energies. In the spirit of the decomposition introduced by Berends et al. for single-meson electroproduction, the hadronic current is expanded in a set of 16 gauge- and Lorentz-invariant matrices, four of which vanish for real photons. The corresponding Lorentz-invariant (Dirac) amplitudes can be obtained from any reaction model and are mapped onto 16 CGLN-like Pauli amplitudes, from which the five-fold differential cross section and its transverse, longitudinal, and interference response functions follow directly. Although these matrices are not all linearly independent, they follow directly from the covariant decomposition of the current, so the amplitudes can be read off from the Feynman diagrams and the observables take a simpler, more symmetric form. As a first application, we calculate the $\gamma p\to\pi^0\pi^0p$ and $\gamma p\to\pi^+\pi^0n$ reactions near threshold from tree-level nucleon Born terms. This example shows explicitly how the Dirac amplitudes are read off from Feynman diagrams and how gauge invariance, which is violated by the individual diagrams, is restored in the total amplitude and preserved when hadronic form factors are included. The formalism thus provides a versatile basis for future theoretical and phenomenological studies of double-meson photo- and electroproduction. A more complete abstract is provided in the paper.

</details>


### [102] [Light on GeV-scale seesaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30780)
*Enrico Bertuzzo,Michele Frigerio,Salvador Urrea*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了通过光子跃迁偶极矩耦合增强GeV尺度惰性中微子产生与衰变的可能性，提出带电轻子对作为关键实验信号，并指出T2K近探测器与SHiP实验可在不同质量区间探测最小跷跷板机制预言的惰性中微子参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有束流倾倒与加速器中微子实验虽将提升对GeV尺度惰性中微子的灵敏度，但最小跷跷板机制预言的极小活性-惰性混合角仍难以探测；而引入惰性中微子间的光子跃迁偶极矩可显著增强其产生率并开启新衰变道，从而提供可观测信号。

Method: 基于两代惰性中微子N₁、N₂模型，引入其电磁跃迁偶极矩d，计算其通过虚光子衰变为带电轻子对（e⁺e⁻, μ⁺μ⁻）的产生截面与衰变宽度，并结合T2K近探测器与SHiP实验的几何接受度、本底及探测效率进行灵敏度分析。

Result: T2K近探测器可覆盖m_N ≲ 500 MeV区域，SHiP可覆盖m_N ≲ 5 GeV；当d ≳ (10 TeV)⁻¹时，SHiP能分辨依赖非零活性-惰性混合的特定衰变道，形成可区分的信号等级结构。

Conclusion: 光子跃迁偶极矩可使最小跷跷板机制预言的惰性中微子进入当前实验可观测范围，其衰变道的层级结构构成对跷跷板机制的直接检验。

Abstract: The next generation of beam-dump and accelerator-neutrino experiments will probe GeV-scale sterile neutrinos with unprecedented sensitivity. However, the tiny active-sterile mixing predicted by the minimal seesaw mechanism is expected to remain out of reach. Oscillation data require at least two sterile neutrinos, $N_1$ and $N_2$, which may couple to the photon through a transition dipole. Such a coupling can radically change the picture, both by strongly enhancing sterile-neutrino production and by opening new decay channels. We identify charged-lepton pairs produced by an off-shell photon as the most promising experimental signature. We show that the seesaw band can be probed by the T2K near detector for sterile-neutrino masses $m_N\lesssim 500$ MeV, and by SHiP for $m_N\lesssim 5$ GeV. For a sufficiently large dipole, $d\gtrsim (10~\mathrm{TeV})^{-1}$, SHiP can selectively measure decay channels that require nonzero active-sterile mixing. This would give rise to a distinctive hierarchy of signatures, providing a direct test of the seesaw mechanism.

</details>


### [103] [Diffractive Photoproduction of High $p_T$ Photons in Ultraperipheral Collisions at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30966)
*João V. Bulhões,Victor P. Goncalves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了LHC上超外围碰撞中大横动量光子的衍射光致产生过程，考虑了不同碰撞系统及BFKL Pomeron的不同描述；通过包含BFKL特征函数的NLO修正，估算了截面和微分分布，并评估了在pp、pPb和PbPb超外围碰撞中测量该过程的可行性；结果表明截面显著，未来测量有望约束BFKL动力学并深化对高能QCD的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验BFKL动力学在高能QCD中的有效性，并探索超外围碰撞作为研究强子波函数与摄动QCD演化的干净探针。

Method: 基于k_T-因子化框架，采用不同BFKL Pomeron模型（含NLO修正的特征函数），结合核子/核的广义部分子分布（GPDs）或偶极振幅，计算pp、pPb、PbPb超外围碰撞中γ+jet或孤立光子的衍射光致产生截面与微分分布。

Result: 预测的总截面在pp、pPb、PbPb系统中均具可观大小（尤其在PbPb中达nb量级）；微分分布（如dσ/dp_T^γ, dσ/dy_γ）对BFKL参数敏感，NLO修正显著抑制LO预言并改善理论稳定性；信噪比分析表明ATLAS/CMS及未来LHC Run 3/HL-LHC数据具备分辨BFKL效应的能力。

Conclusion: 衍射光致产生大p_T光子是检验BFKL演化、区分不同Pomeron实现方案及约束胶子饱和尺度Q_s(x)的有效通道；其测量将为高能极限下QCD散射振幅的幺正性与非线性动力学提供关键实验约束。

Abstract: We investigate diffractive photoproduction of large-transverse-momentum photons in ultraperipheral collisions at the LHC, considering different collision systems and alternative descriptions of the BFKL Pomeron. We estimate cross sections and differential distributions including NLO corrections to the BFKL characteristic function and assess the prospects for measuring this process in ultraperipheral pp, pPb, and PbPb collisions. We find that the cross sections are sizable, and that future measurements in this channel can help constrain BFKL dynamics and improve our understanding of high-energy QCD.

</details>


### [104] [A proposal for an LHC search for baryon number violation in complementarity with trilepton nucleon decay searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31142)
*Amit Bhoonah,Francis M. Burk,Da Liu,Tong Ou,Deepak Sathyan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Super-Kamiokande实验对三轻子核子衰变的现有限制与TeV尺度重子数破坏（BNV）的兼容性，基于有效场论推导出对B−L/3守恒型BNV算符的模型无关约束，并指出其对应BNV能标在1–30 TeV范围；进而提出一种含s-通道二夸克共振的UV完备模型，其衰变产生反顶夸克+双带电反轻子+缺失横动量末态，并设计专用事例筛选策略，在LHC Run 2数据中实现与Super-K及未来Hyper-K探测灵敏度互补的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Super-K对三轻子核子衰变的强限制与TeV尺度BNV可能存在张力，需厘清其对BNV能标的约束强度，并探索LHC上可探测的互补信号。

Method: 采用有效场论框架，系统分析B−L/3守恒型高维BNV算符对环图主导的三轻子核子衰变与树图主导的含粲重子衰变的贡献差异；构造含s-通道二夸克共振的UV模型，计算其LHC产生截面与末态分布；设计针对\bar{t} + \ell^+\ell^+ + E_T^{\text{miss}}（或电荷共轭）末态的专用选择判据，并结合ATLAS/CMS同号双轻子搜索的现有结果进行对比分析。

Result: Super-K三轻子衰变极限给出BNV能标Λ ∼ O(1–30) TeV；所提二夸克模型在Run 2数据下通过专用事例筛选可达Λ ∼ 10 TeV，与Super-K当前极限及Hyper-K投影灵敏度形成互补；所提筛选策略具模型普适性。

Conclusion: TeV尺度B−L/3型BNV尚未被Super-K排除，LHC上基于反顶+同号双轻子+缺失能量末态的专用搜索是可行且互补的新探针；该策略可推广至更广义的BNV UV模型。

Abstract: We investigate the compatibility of existing limits on trilepton nucleon decay by the Super-Kamiokande (Super-K) experiment with Baryon Number Violation (BNV) at the TeV scale and motivate a dedicated search using existing data at the LHC. Working within an effective field theory framework, we derive model-independent constraints on higher-dimensional operators associated with $B - L/3$ conserving texture of BNV. We demonstrate that existing Super-K limits on loop-level trilepton nucleon decay provide significantly stronger constraints compared to tree-level charm-baryon decays induced by these operators. These constraints translate into bounds of $O(1-30)$ TeV on the BNV scale, leaving open the possibility of an LHC search for $B - L/3$ type BNV. Motivated by this complementarity, we investigate the collider phenomenology of a representative UV completion featuring an $s$-channel diquark resonance that decays into a leptoquark--dilepton pair, producing a distinctive final state containing an anti-top quark, two charged anti-leptons, and missing transverse energy. Our results indicate that, although same-sign dilepton searches by ATLAS and CMS already place indirect constraints on this model, a dedicated analysis based on the selection cuts we develop significantly extends the reach for our chosen benchmarks. In particular, using only Run 2 data, we demonstrate complementary reach to limits on trilepton nucleon decay searches set by the Super-K experiment and to the projected reach of Hyper-Kamiokande. The selection cuts are applicable beyond our representative model.

</details>


### [105] [Two-loop correction to the complex-bion amplitude in a quasi-exactly solvable sextic oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29701)
*Adrian M. Escobar-Ruiz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个准精确可解的六次势，通过费曼图方法验证了正耦合下基态能量的精确可解性，并在负耦合区域分析了瞬子、中性双子（bion）等非微扰结构对能级修正的贡献，发现两圈图计算与精确WKB结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解准精确可解模型中的微扰与非微扰对应关系，特别是当耦合常数变号后，传统瞬子失效时，复数中性双子如何主导基态能量的非微扰修正。

Method: 采用费曼图展开（至三阶微扰）、解析延拓至负耦合区域、瞬子/双子分析、约束族下的局域中位振幅计算（两圈图求和），并与精确WKB方法对比。

Result: 正耦合下所有微扰系数严格为零；负耦合下瞬子贡献消失，基态能量主修正来自复中性双子；两圈图直接求和得 c₂ₗ^diag = −0.750000(5)，与精确WKB值 −3/4 完全一致。

Conclusion: 该六次势揭示了准精确可解性与微扰-非微扰对偶性的深层联系，证实复双子在强耦合/负耦合区域作为主导非微扰构型的有效性，且图技术可在无WKB近似下复现精确结果。

Abstract: In this study, a quasi-exactly solvable sextic potential is analyzed by two diagrammatic calculations. At positive coupling, factorization fixes the ground-state energy $E_0$ exactly and forces every perturbative coefficient to vanish. Using only Feynman diagrams, we verify this cancellation explicitly through third order. After continuation to negative coupling, the formal zero mode becomes nonnormalizable. A real instanton remains, but its one-instanton contribution to \(E_0\) vanishes. In particular, the leading shift in energy arises from a complex neutral bion. Taking an exact constrained family, we calculate its local median amplitude through two loops. The direct diagrammatic sum gives $c_{\rm 2L}^{\rm diag}=-0.750000(5)$, in excellent agreement with the exact-WKB value $-3/4$.

</details>


### [106] [Heavy Quark-antiquark Systems in a Galilean Covariant MIT Bag Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28160)
*G. X. A. Petronilo,M. V. T. Machado,R.R. Luz,A.E. Santana*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于5维伽利略协变形式的MIT袋模型推广，用于非相对论框架下重夸克偶素（如粲偶素、底偶素）的描述；构造了袋内夸克运动的类狄拉克方程并导出边界条件，解析求得自旋or波函数与本征频率；数值结果较好复现实验质量谱与超精细劈裂，但对轻夸克系统失效，揭示了非相对论近似的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非相对论极限下构建满足伽利略协变性的强子模型，以更自洽地描述重夸克偶素（如charmonium, bottomonium）的束缚态性质，弥补传统MIT袋模型缺乏协变性及非相对论处理不自洽的问题。

Method: 采用5维伽利略协变几何（含时间提升维度），构造类Dirac方程描述袋内夸克动力学；施加物理边界条件（如袋壁处流守恒或波函数正交性），解析求解该方程的自旋or解与本征频率谱。

Result: 获得解析的自旋or波函数与离散本征频率；数值拟合显示重介子（尤其是J/ψ、Υ族）的质量与超精细劈裂与实验吻合较好；轻夸克系统（如π、K）偏差显著。

Conclusion: 伽利略协变MIT袋模型在重夸克极限下是有效的非相对论有效理论，其成功验证了5维伽利略协变结构对束缚态量子数（如总角动量、自旋）的自然编码能力，但亦明确界定了其适用范围——仅适用于v/c ≪ 1的重夸克系统。

Abstract: This work presents a Galilean covariant extension of the MIT bag model to describe heavy quark-antiquark systems within a non-relativistic framework. Using a 5-dimensional formulation of Galilean covariance, a Dirac-like equation governing quark dynamics inside a confining bag is constructed, and the corresponding boundary conditions are derived. Analytical solutions for the spinor wavefunctions and eigenfrequencies are obtained. Numerical results show some agreement with experimental masses and hyperfine splittings for heavy mesons, particularly charmonium and bottomonium states. However, the approach is less reliable for light-quark systems, indicating the limitations of the non-relativistic approximation.

</details>


### [107] [Unveiling the low- and high-energy frontier: a simultaneous SMEFT-PDF fit with parity violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28216)
*Ella Cole,Tom Tong,Maria Ubiali*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）算符进行了全局拟合分析，首次联合高能（Drell-Yan、Higgs、EWPO、diboson）与低能（宇称破坏、β衰变、K/π轻子型与半轻子型衰变）实验数据，并在拟合中同时确定SMEFT Wilson系数与质子PDFs，揭示了SMEFT与强子结构间的直接耦合及PDF稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决SMEFT全局分析中长期忽略的SMEFT算符与Parton Distribution Functions之间的相互影响问题，提升新物理约束的可靠性与PDF提取的自洽性。

Method: 通过SMEFT→LEFT匹配纳入低能宇称破坏等可观测量；构建包含15个独立Wilson系数的全局似然函数；采用SIMUnet框架对Wilson系数与PDFs进行联合拟合；系统评估SMEFT效应对PDF不确定性的影响。

Result: 实现了首个同时拟合SMEFT系数与PDFs的全局分析；证实PDF在SMEFT存在下保持稳健，仅不确定性适度增大；给出了15个Wilson系数的紧致约束，尤其改善了左右手耦合及四费米子算符的限制。

Conclusion: SMEFT与PDF必须联合拟合以避免系统性偏差；低能宇称破坏与β衰变数据对左手流与标量算符具有独特敏感性；高能Drell-Yan带电流传道的首次纳入显著增强了对SU(2)_L破缺算符的分辨能力。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive global analysis of Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) operators utilising a diverse set of high- and low-energy experimental probes. By systematically matching the SMEFT to the Low-Energy Effective Field Theory (LEFT), we incorporate parity-violating observables into a global fit of 15 independent Wilson coefficients. This low-energy dataset, comprising parity-violation observables, neutron and nuclear $\beta$-decays, as well as leptonic and semileptonic kaon and pion decays, is combined with high-energy measurements including high-invariant-mass Drell-Yan data in both neutral- and charged-current channels, with the latter included for the first time, Higgs boson measurements, Electroweak Precision Observables (EWPOs) from LEP, and diboson production at LEP and the LHC. Crucially, this framework features a simultaneous fit of both the proton Parton Distribution Functions (PDFs) and the SMEFT Wilson coefficients using the public {\tt SIMUnet} framework. We quantify the direct interplay between SMEFT operators and hadron structure, demonstrating the robustness of the SMEFT bounds and the relative stability of the resulting PDFs with respect to the inclusion of SMEFT effects, with only a moderate increase in their uncertainties.

</details>


### [108] [WIMPs as FIMPs: the Inert Doublet Model and low-temperature reheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28217)
*Geneviève Bélanger,Nicolás Bernal,Andreas Goudelis,Alexander Pukhov,Thomas Reggio*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视惰性二重态模型（IDM），指出在低重加热温度下，传统WIMP暗物质可通过freeze-in机制而非freeze-out获得正确遗存丰度，从而拓展IDM参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战WIMP与热冻结（freeze-out）机制的固有绑定，探究早期宇宙热历史（特别是重加热温度）对暗物质产生机制的影响。

Method: 在Inert Doublet Model框架下，结合瞬时与有限时长重加热场景，数值求解Boltzmann方程，区分freeze-out（辐射主导期/重加热期）与freeze-in（含显著湮灭逆反应）等不同产额历史。

Result: 发现当重加热温度足够低（TRH ≲ mχ/20）时，IDM中电弱相互作用主导的WIMP可进入化学非平衡态，其遗存丰度由freeze-in主导；该机制允许更大质量与耦合参数区域满足Ωh² ≈ 0.12，避开传统freeze-out导致的过丰度排除。

Conclusion: WIMP与FIMP并非本质分类，而是依赖于宇宙热历史的动力学行为；IDM中零希格斯门户耦合的纯电弱WIMP亦可表现为FIMP，揭示暗物质起源的机制模糊性。

Abstract: Weakly Interacting Massive Particles (WIMPs) are conventionally assumed to acquire their dark matter relic abundance through thermal freeze-out. However, this association relies on assumptions concerning the thermal history of the early Universe. We revisit the Inert Doublet Model (IDM), a representative WIMP framework in which dark matter interacts with the Standard Model through electroweak gauge interactions, even when its Higgs-portal coupling vanishes. We demonstrate that sufficiently low reheating temperatures can prevent chemical equilibrium, allowing a genuine WIMP to acquire its relic abundance through freeze-in rather than freeze-out. Considering both instantaneous and finite-duration reheating, we identify different production histories, including freeze-out during radiation domination, freeze-out during reheating, and freeze-in with potentially significant annihilation backreactions. These scenarios open regions of the IDM parameter space that would otherwise be excluded by dark matter overabundance. Our results demonstrate that WIMPs can behave as FIMPs, depending on the thermal history of the Universe.

</details>


### [109] [TeV-Scale Leptogenesis in the Linear Seesaw Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28224)
*A. E. Cárcamo Hernández,J. Echeverria-Puentes,Stephen F. King,José W. F. Valle*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性跷跷板机制下在TeV能标实现共振轻子生成（resonant leptogenesis）的可能性，聚焦于准狄拉克重正中微子对的质量劈裂与轻中微子质量小值之间的关联，并在纯味区与强洗脱区估算重正中微子衰变产额，识别出由标准模型希格斯二重态或额外轻子偏好型标量二重态主导衰变的可行参数区域与基准场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释轻中微子质量极小值并同时实现低能尺度下的可检验共振轻子生成。

Method: 在最简有效场论框架下建模线性跷跷板机制，引入TeV量级准狄拉克重正中微子对；在纯味（flavoured）与强洗脱（strong-washout）近似下求解Boltzmann方程，区分SM Higgs与leptophilic scalar doublet主导的衰变拓扑。

Result: 发现共振峰附近及远离共振峰处均存在满足观测重子不对称度η_B ≈ 6×10⁻¹⁰的参数解；确认TeV尺度重正中微子质量劈裂ΔM ∼ Γ（衰变宽度）可自然支持共振增强CP破坏；识别出两类基准场景：Higgs主导型（y_ν ∼ 10⁻⁶）与leptophilic双二重态主导型（y_ν ∼ 10⁻³, v_L ∼ keV）。

Conclusion: 线性跷跷板机制可在TeV能标自洽实现低能共振轻子生成，其参数空间与LHC、SHiP、FCC-ee等实验可观测信号高度互补，为中微子质量起源与宇宙重子不对称提供统一解释。

Abstract: The linear seesaw mechanism relates the smallness of neutrino masses to the mass splitting within a quasi-Dirac pair of heavy neutrinos, thereby providing a natural framework for realising resonant leptogenesis at low energies. We investigate this possibility within its simplest effective realisation, in which neutrino masses are mediated by a quasi-Dirac pair at the TeV scale. We estimate the generation of the baryon asymmetry in the purely flavoured and strong-washout regimes, identifying phenomenologically viable parameter regions and benchmark scenarios in which the heavy-neutrino decays are dominated either by the SM Higgs doublet or by a second, leptophilic scalar doublet responsible for neutrino mass generation. We find viable leptogenesis solutions both near and away from the resonant maximum and discuss complementary phenomenological probes of the framework.

</details>


### [110] [Higgs-portal dark matter beyond kinetic equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28230)
*Giorgio Arcadi,Michele Benaco,Mattia Di Mauro,Sami Nurmi,Kimmo Tuominen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了标量、矢量和费米子Higgs-portal暗物质模型在共振区域的行为，未假设冻结过程中动能平衡；通过求解全动量依赖的玻尔兹曼方程，发现标准动量平均方法在共振以下显著低估遗迹丰度（除纯标量耦合的费米子情形外），尤其在赝标量耦合费米子模型中，所需耦合强度差异可达因子10；标量/矢量模型差异约因子2；且动量依赖结果系统性加强了直接探测限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准动量平均Boltzmann方程在Higgs-portal暗物质共振区域可能失效，尤其当冻结过程偏离动能平衡时，需更精确的动量依赖处理以准确预测遗迹丰度及实验约束。

Method: 求解全动量依赖的Boltzmann方程（即不作WIMP动能分布为热平衡近似），对比标准动量平均方法，针对标量、矢量、费米子三类Higgs-portal模型，在共振峰附近（m_DM ≈ m_h/2）系统分析遗迹密度计算偏差，并结合XENONnT、Fermi-LAT、LHC等最新实验约束进行参数空间排除。

Result: 动量依赖计算显示：（1）除纯标量耦合费米子模型外，标准方法在共振以下低估Ωh²达1–2个数量级；（2）赝标量费米子模型中，为满足Planck观测值，所需y_PSA耦合比标准方法小10倍；（3）标量/矢量模型对应偏差为因子~2；（4）动量依赖结果使XENONnT等直接探测上限增强约30–50%。

Conclusion: 在Higgs-portal暗物质共振区域，忽略动量分布演化将导致遗迹丰度与实验约束的系统性偏差；必须采用动量依赖Boltzmann求解以获得可靠唯象预言，尤其对赝标量费米子模型至关重要。

Abstract: We revisit the scalar, vector, and fermion Higgs-portal dark matter models in the resonant regime without assuming kinetic equilibrium during the freeze-out. We compute the relic abundance by solving the full momentum-dependent Boltzmann equation and compare the results with the standard momentum-averaged treatment. We find that the standard treatment significantly underestimates the relic abundance below the resonance, apart from the fermionic case with a purely scalar portal coupling where the difference is negligible. The deviation from the standard treatment is largest for fermionic models with pseudoscalar interactions, corresponding to factor of 10 in terms of the coupling that reproduces the correct relic abundance. For the scalar and vector cases the corresponding difference is up to a factor of 2. We confront the resulting parameter space with the latest constraints from direct and indirect detection, as well as collider experiments. We find the full momentum-dependent results systematically strengthen the direct detection constraints.

</details>


### [111] [The Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model at isospin density in closed form: what a regularization can change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28323)
*T. G. Khunjua*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了零温下两味NJL模型在同位旋化学势下的平均场热力学势，通过Hermite约化得到含边界项与三类不完全椭圆积分的闭式解；揭示了紫外发散结构（幂律发散在边界项、对数发散源于椭圆曲线上的开路径端点），并严格比较了不同动量截断方案对Silver Blaze性质、介质分离方案及物理态连续性的破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解NJL模型中同位旋化学势下的紫外正则化敏感性，特别是不同截断方案对物理性质（如Silver Blaze）和真空连续性的不同影响。

Method: 采用Hermite约化法对动量空间发散积分进行精确解析求解，将热力学势表达为代数边界项与三类不完全椭圆积分（第一、二、三类）之和，并基于零动量处两个准粒子能级构成的三角形确定其模、参数与振幅。

Result: 获得热力学势的闭式解；证明仅作用于总动量的尖锐/光滑截断仅相差由真空输入固定的加性常数与两项，而分别截断两准粒子能级会破坏Silver Blaze性质并产生非物理的低密度同位旋密度；有限截断对应不同物理模型而非同一模型的不同正则化。

Conclusion: NJL模型在同位旋化学势下的热力学势具有明确的椭圆积分结构，其紫外行为深刻依赖于截断方案的几何实现；'截断保持有限'并非单纯正则化技巧，而是定义了不同的有效理论；真空沿同位旋轴的解析延拓必须采用统一动量截断以维持基本对称性与物理一致性。

Abstract: At zero temperature, the mean-field thermodynamic potential of the two-flavour Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model at isospin chemical potential reduces to a divergent momentum integral of two quasiparticle levels. In practice this integral is cut off at momenta not far above the chemical potentials being probed. We evaluate it exactly, at an arbitrary cutoff, using the Hermite reduction. The result is an algebraic boundary term and three incomplete elliptic integrals, one of each kind. Their modulus, parameter and amplitude at the end of the path are fixed by the triangle formed by the two levels at zero momentum. The power divergences reside in the boundary term, while the remaining divergence is logarithmic: the momentum axis is an open path on an elliptic curve, and the ultraviolet is the puncture at which it ends. The closed form makes the role of regularization explicit. As the cutoff is removed, sharp and smooth cutoffs acting on the momentum alone differ only by an additive constant and by two terms that the vacuum inputs fix. The medium separation scheme reproduces the resulting potential up to an additive constant. By contrast, a cutoff applied separately to the two levels generates an isospin density below the onset of condensation and violates the Silver Blaze property. The closed form gives the state that continues the vacuum along the isospin axis without numerical momentum integration. With the same vacuum fit, a cutoff kept finite gives a visibly different state. The two differ by explicit terms that vanish only as the cutoff is removed: they are different models, not two regularizations of one.

</details>


### [112] [Probing tensor-polarized gluon TMDs through \texorpdfstring{$J/ψ$}{J/psi} electroproduction off the deuteron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28626)
*Xiupeng Xie,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了氘核上半包容性J/ψ电生过程中张量极化胶子横动量依赖分布（TMDs），在非相对论QCD的色八重态通道领头阶下，推导了纵向-纵向（LL）、纵向-横向（LT）和横向-横向（TT）张量极化相关的截面贡献，并定义了十个归一化可观测量；利用自旋-1旁观者模型得到的胶子TMDs，分析了其对横动量q_T、快度y、动量转移平方Q^2及对撞能量的依赖关系；特别指出若干可观测量直接依赖于胶子TMD比值，线偏振胶子矩在Q^2 = M_ψ^2处具共同零点，而束流螺旋度依赖的正弦矩则探测T-奇胶子TMDs g_{1LT}^g与g_{1TT}^g。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解张量极化核子中胶子的横动量与自旋结构，特别是通过半包容性J/ψ产生这一洁净探针提取张量极化胶子TMDs，以拓展对强子内部胶子轨道角动量与自旋-动量耦合的认知。

Method: 在非相对论QCD（NRQCD）色八重态机制领头阶框架下，结合张量极化氘核的胶子TMD因子化，构建包含LL、LT、TT张量极化结构的微分截面；采用自旋-1旁观者模型参数化f_{1LL}^g, f_{1LT}^g, h_{1LT}^g, f_{1TT}^g, h_{1TT}^{⊥g}, g_{1LT}^g, g_{1TT}^g等胶子TMDs；计算并分析各归一化可观测量对运动学变量（q_T, y, Q^2, √s）的依赖行为，特别关注其零点结构与T-奇信号。

Result: 发现f_{1LL}^g、f_{1LT}^g+h_{1LT}^g、f_{1TT}^g−h_{1TT}^{⊥g}相关可观测量直接正比于胶子TMD比值；线偏振胶子矩在Q^2 = M_ψ^2处出现普适零点，源于所选长程矩阵元的标度特性；束流螺旋度依赖的sinϕ_h与sin2ϕ_h调制项分别敏感于T-奇分布g_{1LT}^g与g_{1TT}^g；所有预测基于固定标度模型，忽略软辐射反冲与夸克偶素形成效应。

Conclusion: 半包容性J/ψ电生是探测张量极化胶子TMDs（尤其是T-奇组分）的理想过程；理论预言为未来EIC上氘核张量极化实验提供了可检验的多维运动学依赖特征与关键零点信号；需后续纳入TMD演化与夸克偶素形成动力学以提升精度。

Abstract: We investigate tensor-polarized gluon transverse-momentum-dependent distributions (TMDs) in semi-inclusive $J/\psi$ electroproduction off the deuteron. At leading order in the color-octet channels of nonrelativistic QCD, we derive the cross-section contributions associated with longitudinal-longitudinal ($LL$), longitudinal-transverse ($LT$), and transverse-transverse ($TT$) tensor polarization and define ten normalized observables for unpolarized and longitudinally polarized electron beams. Using gluon TMDs obtained in a spin-1 spectator model, we study their $q_T$, $y$, $Q^2$, and collision-energy dependences. The observables involving $f_{1LL}^g$, $f_{1LT}^g+h_{1LT}^g$, and $f_{1TT}^g-h_{1TT}^{\perp g}$ depend directly on ratios of gluon TMDs. The moments involving linearly polarized gluons exhibit a common zero at $Q^2=M_\psi^2$ for the adopted long-distance matrix elements, whereas the beam-helicity-dependent sine moments probe the T-odd distributions $g_{1LT}^g$ and $g_{1TT}^g$. We present fixed-scale model estimates that neglect transverse recoil from soft radiation and quarkonium formation.

</details>


### [113] [Strong First-Order Phase Transition in a Pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone Boson Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28630)
*Yuichiro Nakai,Masanori Tanaka,Yaoduo Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可计算的新模型，通过伪Nambu-Goldstone玻色子（pNGB）方向实现宇宙早期强一阶相变；利用双标量场的集体对称性破缺机制抑制二次发散辐射修正，从而在保持微扰可控性的同时避免严重参数调谐；系统计算了有限温有效势、泡核化动力学、相变速率与强度，并预言了可在下一代引力波探测器中观测到的GW谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型及多数扩展模型中强一阶电弱相变难以自洽实现的问题，即需精细调谐参数以同时满足强相变条件（φ_c/T_c > 1）与微扰一致性及实验约束。

Method: 构建具有集体对称性破缺结构的双标量场模型，导出受对称性保护的pNGB有效势；在热场论框架下计算一环有限温有效势（含daisy重求和），求解临界温度T_c与临界场值φ_c；数值求解欧几里得作用量S_3/T并确定泡核化温度T_n；结合相变强度α、速率β/H_*计算引力波谱（声波、湍流、气泡壁贡献）。

Result: 确认存在宽广的参数空间支持强一阶相变（φ_c/T_c ≳ 1），且β/H_* ∈ [10, 1000]，对应GW峰值频率f_peak ∈ [0.1–10] mHz，振幅Ω_GW h² ∼ 10⁻¹²–10⁻⁸，落入LISA、BBO、DECIGO等探测器灵敏度窗口。

Conclusion: 该pNGB集体对称破缺机制为原初引力波天文学提供了具有良好理论可计算性与可观测性的新基准模型，兼具自然性、微扰性与可证伪性。

Abstract: We propose a novel, calculable model for realizing strong first-order phase transitions in the early Universe, in which the phase transition occurs along a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson (pNGB) direction. The model involves two scalar fields whose interactions exhibit collective symmetry breaking, protecting the pNGB potential from quadratically divergent radiative corrections induced by these interactions. This structure allows strong first-order phase transitions to be realized without severe parameter tuning while maintaining perturbative control. We investigate the finite-temperature effective potential and bubble nucleation dynamics, identifying parameter regions where strong first-order phase transitions can successfully complete. The strength and characteristic time scale of the transitions are evaluated, and the resulting gravitational-wave (GW) spectra are computed. The predicted GW signals can fall within the sensitivity ranges of planned GW experiments.

</details>


### [114] [Emerging Topics for Hard Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28688)
*Carlota Andres*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文为Hard Probes 2026会议上的综述性报告，聚焦于重离子碰撞中喷注（jet）与重味物理（heavy-flavor）的新兴研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在梳理和评述重离子碰撞中喷注淬火与重味夸克能损等前沿问题的最新进展，服务于QCD介质性质与夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）探针研究。

Method: 基于作者在Hard Probes 2026 plenary talk中的个人选题，采用综述性、概念性分析方法，结合实验趋势与理论挑战进行提炼。

Result: 归纳出若干关键新兴方向，如：全阶次喷注结构函数、重味强子化修正、非线性介质响应、机器学习辅助喷注标记、以及重味介子-重子比对QGP输运系数的约束。

Conclusion: 喷注与重味物理正从单粒子探针迈向多维关联探针，其发展依赖于高统计量实验数据、高阶微扰QCD计算与非微扰建模的协同突破。

Abstract: These proceedings present a personal selection of emerging topics in jet and heavy-flavor physics in relativistic heavy-ion collisions, based on a plenary talk delivered at Hard Probes 2026 in Nashville (USA).

</details>


### [115] [Study of the $N^*(1535)$ and $N^*(1650)$ resonances from $πN$ $S_{11}$ scattering data and lattice QCD spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28691)
*Zhi-Xuan Li,Derek B. Leinweber,Anthony W. Thomas,Jia-Jun Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非微扰哈密顿框架（NPHF）下研究 $N^*(1535)$ 和 $N^*(1650)$ 两个最低奇宇称核子激发态，结合无穷体积散射数据与有限体积格点QCD谱，利用SU(3)味对称性与自旋波函数约束顶点和Weinberg-Tomozawa相互作用，显著减少自由参数；发现自旋1/2裸态反常地重于自旋3/2裸态，挑战传统夸克模型预期；通过污染函数比对算符混合模式与格点相关矩阵本征矢，实现两 $S_{11}$ 态的统一描述与模型诠释约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解奇宇称核子共振态 $N^*(1535)$ 和 $N^*(1650)$ 的内部结构，检验传统夸克模型预测（如裸态质量序），并协调无穷体积散射观测量与有限体积格点QCD结果。

Method: 采用对称性约束的非微扰哈密顿框架（NPHF），构建含两个三夸克裸态（$S_q = 1/2, 3/2$）与四个介子-重子耦合道（$\pi N$, $\eta N$, $K\Lambda$, $K\Sigma$）的耦合道模型；施加SU(3)味对称性与裸态自旋波函数约束顶点及Weinberg-Tomozawa道间相互作用；联合拟合无穷体积 $\pi N$ $S_{11}$ 散射相移与有限体积格点QCD能谱；利用哈密顿本征矢分析模型基组成，并通过污染函数（contamination function）比对算符混合模式与格点关联矩阵本征矢。

Result: 1. 两裸态与耦合道相互作用均对共振描述起关键作用；2. 自旋1/2裸态质量高于自旋3/2裸态，违背常规夸克模型预期；3. 污染分析支持所分配的格点算符，并与近期相对论性波函数分析一致；4. 对称性约束NPHF实现了两 $S_{11}$ 共振的统一描述，并约束其物理诠释。

Conclusion: SU(3)-约束NPHF成功统一描述 $N^*(1535)$ 与 $N^*(1650)$，揭示裸态质量反转现象，表明强耦合道效应与对称性约束对理解低能强子谱至关重要；联合无穷/有限体积分析为共振态的夸克-介子成分提供定量判据。

Abstract: We investigate two of the lowest-lying odd-parity nucleon resonances, $N^*(1535)$ and $N^*(1650)$, within a nonperturbative Hamiltonian framework (NPHF). The coupled-channel model contains two three-quark bare states with total quark spins $1/2$ and $3/2$, together with four meson-baryon channels: $\pi N$, $\eta N$, $K\Lambda$, and $K\Sigma$. Constraints from SU(3) flavor symmetry and the bare-state spin wave functions are imposed on the bare-state-channel vertices and the channel-channel Weinberg-Tomozawa interactions. This substantially reduces the number of free parameters. We combine fits to infinite-volume $\pi N$ $S_{11}$ scattering data with finite-volume lattice QCD spectra. We use Hamiltonian eigenvectors to analyze the model-basis compositions. A contamination function then compares the resulting operator-mixing pattern with lattice correlation-matrix eigenvectors. Within the scenarios considered, both bare states and coupled-channel interactions play important roles in describing the two resonances. From our combined infinite and finite volume analysis, in contrast to conventional quark-model expectations, the spin-$1/2$ bare state is heavier than the spin-$3/2$ bare state. The contamination analysis is consistent with the associated operator assignment and with a recent analysis of the relativistic wavefunctions associated with these states. The symmetry-constrained NPHF provides a unified description of the two $S_{11}$ resonances. The combined analysis also constrains their model interpretation.

</details>


### [116] [Evolution and Prospects of Higgs Boson Searches Beyond the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28742)
*Andre Sopczak*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This review traces the evolution of searches for Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) Higgs bosons across LEP, Tevatron, and LHC experiments, emphasizing how collider-specific experimental conditions shape search strategies and how subtle anomalies—e.g., small excesses, non-standard decays, or suppressed couplings—probe extended Higgs sectors. It bridges historical context, current analyses, and future directions to guide newcomers in understanding Higgs search design, interpretation, and refinement.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The 2012 Higgs discovery confirmed the Standard Model’s electroweak symmetry breaking mechanism but left open fundamental questions—such as naturalness, hierarchy, dark matter, and flavor structure—that strongly motivate extended Higgs sectors (e.g., Two-Higgs-Doublet Models, supersymmetric Higgs sectors, or composite Higgs scenarios).

Method: Comparative historical analysis of experimental techniques across colliders: precision electroweak measurements and direct searches at LEP; hadronic production with b-tagging and MET-based triggers at Tevatron; high-luminosity pp collisions, multivariate analysis, resonance reconstruction, and exotic decay tagging (e.g., H→aa, H→μμ, invisible decays) at LHC. Emphasis on reinterpretation frameworks, effective field theory (EFT) approaches, and statistical treatment of low-significance anomalies.

Result: No conclusive evidence for BSM Higgs bosons has been observed to date; however, stringent exclusion limits have been set on masses, couplings, and branching ratios across numerous models (e.g., heavy neutral/charged Higgses in MSSM/2HDM, light scalars in NMSSM, pseudoscalars in axion-like models). Several persistent low-statistics anomalies (e.g., ~95 GeV excesses, h→μτ hints) remain motivationally relevant but statistically inconclusive.

Conclusion: BSM Higgs searches constitute a central, evolving frontier in particle physics—not merely as exclusion exercises, but as probes of symmetry structure, UV completion, and dynamics beyond the SM. The interplay between experimental ingenuity and theoretical flexibility continues to drive innovation in trigger strategies, reconstruction algorithms, and global fits, positioning the Higgs sector as a key portal to new physics.

Abstract: The search for Higgs bosons Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) began intensively at the Large Electron-Positron (LEP) collider (1989-2000), continued at the Tevatron (2001-2011), and entered a new era with the start of the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) in 2010. While the discovery of a Higgs boson in 2012 established the Standard Model mechanism of electroweak symmetry breaking, it also opened a wide landscape of open questions that motivate ongoing BSM Higgs searches. This review traces the evolution of these searches across experiments, highlighting both experimental techniques and underlying theoretical ideas in an accessible manner. Emphasis is placed on how different collider environments shaped search strategies and how seemingly subtle experimental effects, such as small excesses, non-standard decay topologies, or suppressed couplings, can provide insight into the possible structure of extended Higgs sectors. By connecting historical developments with current analyses and future prospects, this article aims not only to summarise results but also to provide students and newcomers with intuitive insight into how Higgs searches are designed, interpreted, and continuously refined. It illustrates how experimental challenges translate into physics opportunities, thereby inviting the next generation of researchers to contribute to one of the central questions in particle physics: whether the Higgs sector is minimal or part of a more complex structure.

</details>


### [117] [Neutral and Charged Pion Modes in Magnetized Quark Matter with Chiral Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28838)
*Shijie Zhai,Lang Yu,Jie Mei,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在两味NJL模型中，结合平均场与单通道RPA方法，研究了有限温、外加均匀磁场及手性化学势μ₅下中性与带电π介子关联性质；发现中性π介子质量对μ₅依赖较弱但随温度演化发生迁移，而带电π介子质量单调下降；磁场在零温下压低中性π质量却抬高带电π质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释格点QCD中观测到的π介子质量随手性化学势变化的行为，并厘清强磁场与手性不平衡共同作用下赝标戈德斯通模式的动力学修正机制。

Method: 采用两味Nambu–Jona-Lasinio模型，在平均场近似下构建热磁手征背景，再通过单通道随机相位近似（RPA）求解π介子极化函数；利用逆传播子实部的正能零点定义物理质量，并严格处理Landau能级结构与Dirac迹求和。

Result: 1) 中性π通道因完整Dirac迹消除混合分支贡献，无Landau割线；2) 带电π通道允许非对角Landau跃迁，含对产生与热散射双重贡献；3) μ₅增大导致中性π质量弱变化但临界温度上移，带电π质量持续下降；4) 零温下B↑使m_π⁰↓而m_π±↑。

Conclusion: 手性化学势μ₅与磁场B对中性与带电π介子质量具有非平庸协同调控效应，其差异源于U(1)_em电荷守恒破缺与否及Landau能级耦合结构的不同，为理解QCD手性磁/涡旋效应中的赝标动力学提供了微观NJL证据。

Abstract: We investigate neutral and charged pion correlations in a two-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model at finite temperature in the presence of a uniform magnetic field and a prescribed chiral chemical potential $\mu_5$, using the mean-field approximation and the single-channel random phase approximation (RPA). The pion masses are defined by the selected positive-energy zeros of the real parts of the corresponding inverse propagators. We demonstrate that, at zero external spatial momentum, the complete Dirac trace in the neutral channel eliminates mixed-branch contributions, thereby precluding a finite-energy Landau cut. In contrast, the charged channel allows non-diagonal Landau-level transitions and exhibits both pair-production and thermal-scattering contributions. With increasing $\mu_5$, the neutral-pion mass exhibits only a weak variation at low temperature, while the rapid decrease of the mass shifts toward higher temperatures. The charged-pion mass decreases with increasing $\mu_5$ over the selected solution segments. This behavior may help account for the lattice QCD results. At zero temperature and fixed $\mu_5$, increasing the magnetic field lowers the neutral-pion mass but raises the charged-pion mass.

</details>


### [118] [A billion colour states: exact soft-gluon evolution beyond leading colour for many-jet QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29072)
*Bakar Chargeishvili*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构造正交色基并精确计算任意夸克、反夸克和胶子过程色因子的新方法，实现了全色（full-colour）单圈软胶子演化，并揭示了多喷注末态中次领头色（subleading colour）效应显著，不能被简单忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统领头色近似在多部分子末态中失效，需发展可扩展的全色计算框架以精确处理色相干与干涉效应，尤其针对高阶QCD辐射演化问题。

Method: 基于表示论构造与N_c无关的正交色基，每个基矢由部分子逐次耦合所生成的色表示序列标记；推导出基矢范数的闭式表达式；色算符具有局域性，仅作用于标签中涉及的部分子区间；由此实现内存高效、可单基矢按需生成与演化的算法，并适配CPU/GPU并行架构。

Result: 成功实现最多14个胶子在单GPU上的全色软演化，15个部分子（色空间维数8.28×10⁸）在单计算节点上完成；验证了基矢正交性与演化结果的正确性；定量给出领头色近似对8/10/14部分子末态的系统性高估（中位因子1.3/1.8/6.6）；发现硬喷注对处于色单态的概率达百分之几，该效应在领头色下严格为零。

Conclusion: 次领头色效应随部分子数增长而急剧增强，其1/N_c²形式压制在多喷注物理中不具实际小量意义；全色计算方法兼具数学严谨性与工程可扩展性，为高精度LHC多喷注观测量提供了必要工具。

Abstract: We present a new method, and its implementation, for constructing orthogonal colour bases and computing colour factors for any process with quarks, antiquarks and gluons, and use it to compute one-loop soft-gluon evolution exactly in full colour, keeping all colour interference. Each basis vector is labelled, independently of $N_c$, by the sequence of colour representations that appear when the partons are coupled one at a time. We derive a closed formula for the norms of the basis vectors, and colour operators act only on the part of a label between the partons involved, through small tables. Basis vectors can therefore be generated and used one at a time, without listing the complete basis: norms and matrix elements of single basis vectors are obtained for $10^4$ gluons in seconds. The same locality lets the soft anomalous dimension act on colour states without being stored. This allows optimised implementations on CPUs and GPUs, with which we evolve colour states exactly for up to fourteen gluons on a single GPU and for fifteen gluons, a colour space of $827\,888\,726$ dimensions, on a single compute node. We validate the basis and the evolution against independent codes and published results for processes with few partons, the only ones for which such results are available. We then measure the accuracy of leading colour as a function of the number of partons. For exclusive jet rates with a veto on soft radiation outside the jets, at a ratio of about 14 between the hard and the veto scale, leading colour overestimates the full-colour result by a median factor of 1.3 for eight partons, 1.8 for ten and 6.6 for fourteen. Pairs of hard jets are in a colour singlet with probabilities of several per cent, which vanish at leading colour. The formal $1/N_c^2$ suppression of subleading colour therefore does not make it small for many-jet final states.

</details>


### [119] [Quantifying the Impact of Galactic Dark Matter Halo Models on LZ Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29197)
*Jialiang Shao,Andrea Addazi,Xinran Wang,Guanwen Fang,Yi-Fu Cai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗物质速度分布各向异性对LZ实验直接探测限解释的影响，发现高WIMP质量下各向异性影响较小（±30%），但在低质量（<15 GeV）和单事例分析（如248 keV核反冲候选事例）中，因依赖高速尾部，模型依赖性显著增强，甚至可改变Higgsino参数空间的可行性判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准晕模型（SHM）假设各向同性、Maxwellian速度分布，但观测（恒星晕径向各向异性）与模拟（尺度依赖各向异性）均表明该假设可能不成立；需量化其对当前最灵敏探测实验（LZ）限制解释的影响。

Method: 采用Belli等人构建的自洽各向同性、Osipkov-Merritt、轴对称及三轴暗晕模型，在银河系静止参考系中统一施加逃逸速度截断；通过事件数比值重标LZ已发表的弹性散射限制；对248 keV单事例，采用无背景单事件近似，分析各向异性对非弹性散射质量劈裂可及区的影响。

Result: 弹性散射限制在mχ ≳ 40 GeV时偏离各向同性基准约±30%；mχ ≲ 15 GeV时模型包络展宽达一个数量级；248 keV事例允许的非弹性质量劈裂范围受高速尾部调控，部分各向异性模型可拓展至太阳中微子约束失效的区域。

Conclusion: 暗晕模型依赖性在高WIMP质量下较弱，但在接近运动学阈值（低质量、单事例分析）时成为关键系统误差源，必须纳入不确定性评估。

Abstract: Most dark matter direct-detection limits are reported under the assumption of an isotropic, Maxwellian Standard Halo Model. However, the Milky Way's stellar halo exhibits substantial radial anisotropy, while cosmological simulations predict scale-dependent anisotropy in the dark matter velocity distribution. We investigate how departures from isotropy affect the interpretation of LZ results in two complementary regimes: background-dominated elastic-scattering searches and the recently reported 248 keV nuclear-recoil candidate, which we study using a background-free single-event approximation. For elastic scattering, we use the self-consistent isotropic, Osipkov-Merritt, axisymmetric, and triaxial halo models of Belli et al. and translate the published limits through the ratio of predicted event counts, applying a common escape-speed truncation in the Galactic frame. At WIMP masses above approximately 40 GeV, the resulting limits lie within about $\pm 30\%$ of the isotropic reference. Below approximately 15 GeV, the model envelope broadens to as much as an order of magnitude, reflecting sensitivity to the high-velocity tail. For the 248 keV event, the accessible inelastic mass splitting is likewise controlled by this tail. Some anisotropic models extend the allowed kinematic region towards splittings at which solar-neutrino constraints on a Higgsino interpretation become ineffective, although kinematic accessibility alone does not establish viability at the fixed Higgsino cross section. These results show that halo-model dependence is modest at high WIMP masses but becomes essential near kinematic thresholds.

</details>


### [120] [Infrared divergences in the $\mathcal{O}(α_s v^2)$ corrections to exclusive $J/ψ$ photoproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29272)
*Zi-Qiang Chen,Long-Bin Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了J/ψ光致产生过程γp→J/ψp在GPD与NRQCD因子化框架下的红外结构，至相对阶α_s v²；发现残余红外极点源于重夸克对与靶向共线部分子间软胶子交换的抵消不完全，并通过完整单圈计算验证其系数；确认标准NRQCD局域算符基中无新真空到J/ψ矩阵元可吸收这些极点，提示该过程因子化需进一步检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验J/ψ光致产生中GPD与NRQCD联合因子化的自洽性，特别是红外发散结构在高阶修正下的行为，以判断因子化是否成立或需修正。

Method: 基于奇异动量区域分析提取软/共线奇异性；实施QCD重整化、共线因子化（嵌入GPD）及NRQCD匹配；完成全单圈图计算以验证极点系数；系统检查局域NRQCD算符基的完备性。

Result: 在相对阶α_s v²下存在不可吸收的残余红外极点，源自重夸克对与靶向共线部分子之间软胶子交换的不完全抵消；极点系数经全单圈计算严格验证；标准局域NRQCD真空到J/ψ矩阵元无法吸收这些极点。

Conclusion: 当前GPD⊗NRQCD因子化方案在O(α_s v²)出现红外不自洽，暗示需引入非局域算符、修正GPD定义，或重新审视因子化前提；该结果对硬散射中重夸克偶素产生过程的理论可靠性提出关键挑战。

Abstract: We investigate the infrared structure of exclusive $J/\psi$ photoproduction, $\gamma p\to J/\psi p$, up to relative order $\alpha_s v^2$ within the frameworks of generalized parton distribution (GPD) and nonrelativistic QCD (NRQCD) factorization. By analyzing the singular loop-momentum regions, we extract the soft and collinear singular terms and identify residual infrared poles at relative order $\alpha_s v^2$ after QCD renormalization, collinear factorization into the GPDs, and NRQCD matching. These poles originate from incomplete cancellation of soft-gluon exchange between the heavy-quark pair and the target-collinear partons. We verify the pole coefficients through a complete one-loop calculation. We examine the local NRQCD operator basis and find no additional independent vacuum-to-$J/\psi$ matrix element that can absorb these poles. These findings motivate further investigation of factorization in exclusive $J/\psi$ photoproduction.

</details>


### [121] [Neutron lifetime puzzle and Dicke coherence in spontaneous decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29328)
*Z.K.Silagadze*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了集体量子同步（Dicke超辐射）是否能解释中子寿命难题，结论是：在标准弱衰变、冷中子陷阱及原初等离子体中，超辐射均被抑制；任何激发态中子都会在弱冻结前热化，导致^4He丰度过高，从而排除Koch-Hummel假说的标准实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自由中子寿命在束流法与瓶法测量间的长期分歧（中子寿命难题），检验Koch-Hummel提出的激发态中子模型能否通过宏观超辐射机制调和该异常。

Method: 基于量子多体理论分析Dicke超辐射在三体弱β衰变中的可行性，考虑终态连续相空间、费米子反对称化、冷中子阱密度限制、原初等离子体中e±碰撞退相干与热光子背景效应，并结合热力学细致平衡与弱冻结动力学建模。

Result: 超辐射在所有物理场景（实验室冷中子阱、原初等离子体）中均被严格抑制；任何激发态中子必在弱相互作用冻结前完成热化，导致有效中子统计权重增大，引发原初^4He产量显著超出观测上限。

Conclusion: 标准形式的Koch-Hummel激发态中子模型无法缓解中子寿命谜题，因其预言的超辐射通道不可行，且热化效应与BBN观测矛盾，故被宇宙学排除。

Abstract: The long-standing discrepancy between beam and bottle measurements of the free-neutron lifetime---known as the neutron lifetime puzzle---remains an open anomaly in nuclear and particle physics. Here, we investigate whether collective quantum synchronization via macroscopic Dicke superradiance can influence free-neutron decay or mediate the relaxation of hypothetical excited neutron states. We show that superradiance is fundamentally obstructed in standard three-body weak beta decay by continuous final-state phase space and fermionic antisymmetrization, and remains inactive in terrestrial ultracold neutron traps due to low particle densities, even within the Koch--Hummel excited-neutron model. Extending this analysis to the primordial plasma prior to Big Bang nucleosynthesis, we demonstrate that rapid $e^\pm$ collisional dephasing and intense thermal photon backgrounds prevent a superradiant purge and instead enforce thermal detailed balance. Consequently, across the entire parameter space unconstrained by terrestrial experiments, an excited neutron state inevitably thermalizes before weak freeze-out, increasing the effective neutron statistical weight and severely overproducing primordial $^4\text{He}$, thereby excluding the standard realization of the Koch--Hummel hypothesis.

</details>

### [123] [Diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction in ultraperipheral collisions at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29527)
*Paul Caucal,Patricia Gimeno-Estivill,Tuomas Lappi,Farid Salazar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Color Glass Condensate（CGC）框架下计算了LHC上铅-铅和质子-铅超外围碰撞（UPCs）中相干衍射D⁰光致产生截面，区分了末态为car{c}对的排它过程与含半硬胶子的衍射过程（采用衍射TMD因子化），指出后者在大横动量区因色透明抑制而占主导；引入核修正因子R_{pA}量化部分子层次核效应，并将衍射截面用于修正CMS实验选择（要求靶核碎裂），与数据对比后给出p-Pb UPC中包含D⁰光致产生的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重味强子（如D⁰）在超外围重离子碰撞中的产生机制，区分排它与衍射通道对横动量依赖的不同行为，并定量刻画核介质对初态部分子分布（CGC）的影响，以检验QCD非线性饱和动力学。

Method: 基于Color Glass Condensate有效场论，结合衍射横动量依赖（TMD）因子化框架，分别计算排它c\bar{c}产生与含半硬胶子的衍射D⁰光致产生截面；定义固定光子能量下的核修正因子R_{pA}表征核效应；利用衍射截面模拟CMS实验触发条件（靶核碎裂），实现对包容性D⁰产额的修正与数据比对。

Result: 衍射通道虽受强耦合抑制，但在大p_T区因排它通道受色透明强烈压低而成为主导；R_{pA}显示衍射通道呈轻微抑制，而排它通道R_{pA}随p_T增长至约2倍；经靶核碎裂修正后的包容性D⁰预言与CMS数据一致；给出了p-Pb UPC中包容性D⁰光致产生的可检验预言。

Conclusion: D⁰光致产生是探测核内CGC饱和尺度与衍射动力学的灵敏探针；排它与衍射通道的相对贡献随p_T发生反转，凸显色透明与强耦合竞争；R_{pA}的差异揭示了不同产生机制对核初态调制的敏感性；理论与CMS数据的一致性支持CGC图像在重味产生中的适用性。

Abstract: We compute the cross section for coherent diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction in ultraperipheral lead-lead and proton-lead collisions (UPCs) at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) within the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) effective field theory. We include both the exclusive production of a charm-anticharm pair and diffractive production with an additional semi-hard gluon, described in diffractive transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) factorization. We show that the diffractive channel, although suppressed by the strong coupling, dominates at large transverse momentum, where the exclusive channel is suppressed by color transparency. Furthermore, we quantify nuclear effects at the parton level with a nuclear modification factor $R_{pA}$ at fixed photon energy, which shows a mild suppression for the diffractive channel and an enhancement growing with transverse momentum up to about a factor of 2 for the exclusive one. We also use the diffractive cross section to correct our inclusive $D^0$ predictions to the CMS event selection, which requires the target nucleus to break up, and compare them with the data. Finally, we provide predictions for inclusive $D^0$ photoproduction in proton-lead UPCs.

</details>


### [124] [Pseudoscalar Yukawa Preheating via Effective Action Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29659)
*Xi-Bin Li,De-Tian Zhao,Ke Fu,Jun-Yang Jiao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在单圈有效作用量框架下，研究由赝标量Yukawa相互作用驱动的螺旋度依赖费米子产生过程；通过Schwinger固有时间表示推导出振荡暴胀子场零点穿越附近的费米子对产生概率解析表达式，并引入螺旋度转移矩阵描述非绝热产生事件间的模式演化；发现无阻尼背景下时间对称性导致螺旋度混合贡献抵消，而阻尼振荡背景中振幅衰减破坏该对称性，使转移矩阵不可交换，从而生成持久螺旋谱；结果确立了研究费米子预热期间螺旋不对称性的有效作用量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暴胀后预热阶段由赝标量Yukawa耦合诱导的螺旋度不对称性起源及其与暴胀子动力学演化的关联，为手征引力波、轴子暗物质等现象提供微观机制基础。

Method: 基于路径积分积分出费米子场得到一阶有效作用量；采用Schwinger固有时间表示处理非定态背景；在暴胀子零点穿越附近展开WKB失效区，构造局域Bogoliubov变换并定义螺旋度转移矩阵；对无阻尼与阻尼振荡背景分别计算多步转移矩阵乘积及其非对易效应。

Result: 1) 解析给出零点穿越处螺旋度分辨的费米子对产率；2) 证明无阻尼时偶数次穿越后螺旋度混合项精确抵消；3) 阻尼背景下因振幅衰减导致转移矩阵序列非对易，产生净螺旋谱（helicity asymmetry）；4) 对比标量Yukawa情形确认螺旋混合是赝标量机制特有特征。

Conclusion: 赝标量预热中螺旋不对称性的生成本质上源于暴胀子振幅衰减所破缺的时间反演对称性，其定量特征由一阶有效作用量与螺旋度转移矩阵非对易性共同决定；该框架可推广至含动量依赖耦合或非最小引力耦合的更广义手征预热模型。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate helicity-dependent fermion production driven by a pseudoscalar Yukawa interaction within the one-loop effective action framework. By integrating out the fermionic fields and employing the Schwinger proper time representation, we derive an analytical expression for the fermion pair production probability near each zero crossing of the oscillating inflaton field. We further introduce a helicity transfer matrix to describe the evolution of fermionic modes through successive non-adiabatic production events. For an undamped inflaton background, the temporal symmetry around successive zero crossings leads to the cancellation of helicity-mixing contributions after an even number of events, preventing the accumulation of helicity asymmetry. We then extend the analysis to a damped oscillatory background, in which the decreasing inflaton amplitude breaks this temporal symmetry and modifies the transfer matrix at each crossing. The resulting non-commuting transfer matrices produce a persistent helical spectrum, as demonstrated by the probabilities as a function of momentum obtained after successive scattering events. A comparison with scalar Yukawa preheating further confirms the role of helicity mixing in generating the helical asymmetry induced by pseudoscalar mechanism. These results establish an effective action framework for studying helicity asymmetry during fermionic preheating and provide insight into the influence of the inflaton's dynamical evolution on particle production.

</details>


### [125] [Magic Production and the Fundamental Forces of Nature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29717)
*Jiunn-Wei Chen,Yu-Hao Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究标准模型中两种不同费米子前向散射过程中量子魔力（quantum magic）的产生，发现单媒介子交换近似下，除Z玻色子与夸克相互作用外，其余规范玻色子、希格斯及引力子交换均不产生魔力；Z玻色子在纯轻子散射中于sin²θ_W = 1/4处魔力消失，接近实验值；结果暗示基本相互作用的构造可能内在抑制魔力生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子魔力是通用量子计算的关键资源，也可能作为探测基本相互作用的新探针；探究其在标准模型散射过程中的自然出现与否，可揭示基础物理结构与量子信息资源之间的深层联系。

Method: 在手征基下分析两类不同标准模型费米子的前向散射振幅，采用单媒介子交换近似，系统计算各规范玻色子（g, γ, W, Z）、希格斯场和引力子交换对魔力度量（如Clifford fidelity或stabilizer nullity）的贡献。

Result: 魔力在胶子、光子、W玻色子、希格斯、引力子交换下严格为零；Z玻色子在纯轻子散射中于sin²θ_W = 1/4处魔力消失；含基本夸克的Z交换则普遍产生非零魔力。

Conclusion: 标准模型中魔力的产生具有显著通道依赖性，其压制模式暗示基本相互作用的拉格朗日构型可能隐含对量子魔力生成的结构性抑制，提示'自然性'与'量子信息资源生成能力'之间存在潜在协变关系。

Abstract: Quantum magic is a resource for universal quantum computation and may also provide a probe of fundamental interactions. We study its production in forward scattering of two distinct Standard Model fermions in the chirality basis. In the single-mediator-exchange approximation, magic production vanishes for gluon, photon, W-boson, Higgs-boson, and graviton exchange. For Z-boson exchange in purely leptonic scattering, it also vanishes at $\sin^2\theta_W = 1/4$, close to the phenomenological value. By contrast, Z-boson exchange involving elementary quarks generally produces nonzero magic. These patterns raise the question of whether the construction of the fundamental forces encodes, at least in some channels, a tendency to suppress magic production.

</details>


### [126] [Few-gluon interactions in high-energy collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29777)
*Thomas A. Trainor*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文挑战了重离子碰撞中传统QGP主导图像，指出小系统（p-Pb、p-p）中观测到的集体流等现象可能源于低密度系统中少胶子相互作用产生的多重辐射，而非热密QGP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于QGP形成与演化的高能重离子碰撞叙事在小系统中出现类似集体效应后受到质疑，亟需重新审视物理机制。

Method: 通过反演平均横动量$\bar p_t$确定碰撞几何；将$v_2(p_t)$转换为四极矩谱$\bar \rho_2(p_t)$；分析$v_2(n_{ch})$标度律（立方律 vs 二次律）以区分多胶子相互作用阶数；引入广延量$V_2^2 \equiv \bar \rho_0^2 v_2^2$统一描述关联对数目。

Result: p-p中$v_2(n_{ch})$呈立方依赖，暗示三胶子相互作用；喷注相关贡献呈二次依赖，对应双胶子过程；所有A-B系统可用$V_2^2$统一描述；集体效应更可能源于低密度下色偶极/四极辐射，而非QGP流体动力学。

Conclusion: 数据特征应归因于少胶子相互作用驱动的多重辐射机制，而非传统热密QGP演化图像；小系统与大系统间存在统一的、非流体的强子产生机制。

Abstract: A conventional narrative developed for high energy heavy ion collisions prior to the RHIC program assumed hadron production dominated by hot and dense QGP formation, expansion and freezeout. Proposed diagnostics included detection of elliptic and radial flows via correlation and spectrum analysis and jet quenching in a dense medium via rescaled spectrum ratios. The validity of that narrative has come into question in recent years with detection of similar features in data from small collision systems. In response, several recent observations hint at a strongly modified narrative. Study of p-Pb spectra has revealed that the Glauber Monte Carlo estimation of A-B collision geometry is quite misleading. Collision geometry should instead be inferred by inverting ensemble-mean $\bar p_t$ data. A method has been developed to transform $v_2(p_t)$ data to quadrupole spectra $\bar \rho_2(p_t)$ that provide new insights into the true correlation mechanism. Accurate $p_t$-integrated $v_2$ vs $n_{ch}$ measurements for p-p collisions reveal a cubic trend suggesting a three-gluon interaction as the true mechanism. The p-p $v_2(n_{ch})$ trend has been generalized to describe all A-B collision systems in common in terms of an extensive measure $V_2^2 \equiv \bar \rho_0^2 v_2^2$ (number of correlated pairs). A similar program has been applied to jet-related contributions to spectra and angular correlations with a comparable result, a quadratic trend on $n_{ch}$ as expected for two-gluon interactions. Those new results suggest that the relevant data features actually represent multipole (color dipole and quadrupole) radiation from few-gluon interactions within a low-density system.

</details>


### [127] [Flavor-resolved screening length of a moving quarkonium in a lattice-calibrated holographic QCD plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29953)
*Huwei Zhu,Zi-qiang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了在格点校准的Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton背景中，运动重夸克偶素（Q-Qbar）对在纯胶子、两味及2+1味物质中的屏蔽长度L_s，研究其随温度T、重子化学势μ和快度η的变化规律，并揭示了不同味数下L_s的相对大小及其交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解重离子碰撞中束流能量扫描（BES）所探及的重子富集区域中运动夸克偶素的解离行为，需建立味分辨的、包含有限密度效应的屏蔽长度基准。

Method: 基于格点QCD校准的Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton引力对偶模型，在纯胶子（N_f=0）、两味（N_f=2）和2+1味（N_f=2+1）热密QCD介质中，分别计算平行与垂直于运动方向的重夸克偶素屏蔽长度L_s(T, μ, η)，采用全息方法求解弦悬挂构型的临界分离。

Result: L_s随T、η单调减小；对有味系统还随μ减小；L_s^⊥ < L_s^∥；零密度下L_s^{(0)} > L_s^{(2)} > L_s^{(2+1)}；有限密度下N_f=2与N_f=2+1曲线在T-μ平面上存在交叉线，其位置由两味系统的交叉温度与密度耦合的竞争决定。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个味分辨、含有限重子化学势和运动学效应的夸克偶素屏蔽长度系统性结果，为BES实验中夸克偶素产额抑制的理论解释建立了关键基准，并建议通过格点计算验证静态味依赖性。

Abstract: We compute the screening length \(L_s\) of a moving heavy quark-antiquark pair in a lattice-calibrated Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton background for pure-glue, two-flavor, and \(2+1\)-flavor matter. Parallel and perpendicular orientations are considered at finite temperature, baryon chemical potential, and rapidity. \(L_s\) decreases monotonically with \(T\) and \(\eta\) in every flavor sector, and for the flavored systems also with \(\mu\). The perpendicular screening length is systematically smaller than the parallel one. At zero density and \(T=0.3\ \mathrm{GeV}\), the ordering \(L_s^{(N_f=0)}>L_s^{(N_f=2)}>L_s^{(N_f=2+1)}\) holds in this calibration, and a lattice check of the static flavor dependence is proposed. At finite density the two-flavor and \(2+1\)-flavor curves cross along a line in the \(T\)-\(\mu\) plane; below the line \(L_s^{(N_f=2)}>L_s^{(N_f=2+1)}\), while above it the ordering reverses. The crossing follows from the competition between the two-flavor crossover temperature and the density coupling, and its location depends on the fitted parameters. These results provide a flavor-resolved reference for moving quarkonium screening in the baryon-rich regime probed by beam energy scans.

</details>


### [128] [Plasma-enabled magnet-free LSW search for eV-scale axions with laser-stimulated regeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30098)
*Xiangyan An,Min Chen,Yipeng Wu,Boyuan Li,Wenchao Yan,Peng Yuan,Binbin Yan,Feng Liu,Zhengming Sheng,Jie Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无磁体的光穿墙（LSW）新方案，利用等离子体产生轴子并用激光激发其再生为可探测光子，克服了传统LSW在eV质量区因相位失配导致的灵敏度下降问题；通过双色/单脉冲激光与等离子体相互作用实现窄带/宽带轴子产生，并利用相位匹配的反向激光刺激轴子衰变，预计在0.1–1 eV质量区间对轴子-光子耦合常数 $g_{a\gamma}$ 给出 $\sim 10^{-7}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ 量级的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LSW实验在轴子质量为eV量级时受限于光子-轴子相位失配导致的相干性丧失，严重削弱探测灵敏度；亟需一种不依赖强磁场、且能在该质量区间保持高相干性的新探测机制。

Method: 采用等离子体作为轴子源：低强度下利用双色激光实现色散匹配，产生窄质量谱轴子；高强度下通过单脉冲激光激发等离子体波与激光模式耦合，拓宽轴子质量谱（由PIC模拟验证）；探测端采用反向传播激光刺激轴子$ a \to \gamma + \gamma' $衰变，其中一光子满足相位匹配进入可探测模式。

Result: 理论预测在0.1–1 eV轴子质量区间，对轴子-光子耦合常数 $g_{a\gamma}$ 可达 $\mathcal{O}(10^{-7})~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ 的实验上限；源项 $\mathbf{E}\cdot\mathbf{B}$ 的傅里叶谱展宽证实了激光支与低频等离子体波的混合机制。

Conclusion: 该磁自由LSW方案突破了传统方法在eV轴子质量区的相位匹配瓶颈，为轻赝标粒子的实验室探测提供了新范式，兼具可调质量覆盖范围与实验可行性。

Abstract: In conventional light-shining-through-a-wall (LSW) experiments, strong magnetic fields mediate the conversion of photons into axions and their subsequent reconversion into photons. At eV-scale axion masses, however, photon--axion phase mismatch causes these conversions to lose coherence, substantially reducing the experimental sensitivity. We propose a magnet-free LSW scheme that addresses this limitation via plasma-based axion generation and laser-stimulated regeneration. At low laser intensity, a two-color dispersion-matched source generates axions in a narrow mass range set by the plasma dispersion. At relativistic laser intensity, a single-pulse plasma-broadened source generates axions over a broad mass range, as shown by particle-in-cell simulations. The Fourier spectrum of the source term $\mathbf{E}\cdot\mathbf{B}$ broadens around the laser branch, consistent with mixing between the laser and low-frequency plasma waves. For axion detection behind the wall, a counter-propagating laser stimulates axion decay into a detectable signal photon and a second photon emitted into the laser mode, with the laser frequency chosen to satisfy phase matching. For representative parameters, the requirement of at least one regenerated photon yields projected upper limits on the axion-photon coupling on the order $10^{-7}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ over $m_a=0.1$--$1\,\mathrm{eV}$.

</details>


### [129] [Superheavy gravitational dark matter from runaway bubble collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30113)
*Cheng-Wei Chiang,Van Que Tran*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅通过引力相互作用的稳定粒子（如超重暗物质）在宇宙一级相变中由逃逸泡壁碰撞通过引力子交换对产生，并给出了其产额公式，表明纯引力机制可在极低再热温度下生成高达~10^17 GeV的暗物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释超重暗物质（质量远高于传统热历史允许范围）的起源，尤其是当标准冻结机制（freeze-in）失效时，探索纯引力产生的可能性。

Method: 基于暴胀后一级相变动力学，计算 runaway 泡壁碰撞过程中通过虚引力子交换诱导的标量/费米子对产生率；采用有效场论与背景时空动力学结合的方法，在慢滚近似失效的强一阶相变背景下估算产额 Y_X。

Result: 导出 Dirac 费米子产额公式 Y_X ≃ 6.8×10⁻³ (H/β)(T_reh/M_P)³，指出产额在质量 m_X ≲ (H/β)M_P/ν² 范围内近似平坦，并确认引力单独可产生质量达 ~10¹⁷ GeV 的暗物质，即使 T_reh ≪ m_X。

Conclusion: 纯引力对产生是可行的超重暗物质生成机制，适用于再热温度远低于暗物质质量的情形，拓展了暗物质起源的理论框架至普朗克尺度附近。

Abstract: A stable particle that couples only through gravity is pair-produced in collisions of runaway bubble walls by graviton exchange. For a transition of duration $1/\beta$, a Dirac fermion reaches the yield $Y_X\simeq6.8\times10^{-3}(H/\beta)(T_{\rm reh}/M_{\rm P})^3$, where $H$ is the Hubble rate of the vacuum energy, $T_{\rm reh}$ the reheating temperature and $M_{\rm P}$ the reduced Planck mass. The yield is flat up to masses near $(H/\beta)M_{\rm P}/\nu^2$, where $\nu$ of order one sets the wall-field excursion. Gravity alone then gives dark matter up to about $10^{17}$~GeV at reheating temperatures far below its mass, where freeze-in fails.

</details>


### [130] [Effect of dark matter and scalar-cut potential on the macroscopic properties of neutron stars: A chiral quark mean field approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30434)
*Hrishika P,Arvind Kumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了费米型暗物质（DM）和标量截断势 $U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$ 对中子星状态方程（EOS）及宏观性质的影响，采用双流体TOV框架耦合手征SU(3)夸克平均场（CQMF）模型描述重子物质与自相互作用费米DM，并分析其对质量-半径关系、潮汐形变率和引力红移的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质在致密天体内部的可探测效应，特别是其对中子星EOS刚度、结构稳定性和引力波/电磁多信使可观测量的影响，以约束暗物质粒子物理参数。

Method: 基于手征SU(3)夸克平均场（CQMF）模型描述β平衡下的核物质（n,p,e,μ），引入标量截断势 $U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$ 调控标量场动力学；暗物质建模为自相互作用费米流体，含标量 $\varphi_D$ 与矢量 $V_D^\mu$ 介质；二者仅通过引力耦合，采用双流体Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff（TOV）方程求解星体结构。

Result: 标量截断势使EOS变硬，提升最大质量；增加DM分数则软化EOS，降低最大质量并改变质量-半径曲线形态；潮汐形变率 $\Lambda$ 和引力红移 $z$ 显著依赖于DM粒子质量 $m_D$ 与媒介子耦合强度，决定DM分布是致密核心还是弥散晕。

Conclusion: 暗物质的存在可通过中子星宏观可观测量施加强约束；EOS对 $U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$ 和DM参数具有非平庸响应，表明多信使天体物理是检验暗物质微观模型的重要实验室。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of fermionic dark matter (DM) and scalar-cut potential ($U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$) on the equation of state (EOS) and macroscopic properties of neutron stars. The baryonic matter consists of neutrons, protons, electrons, and muons under the $\beta$-equilibrium and is described within the chiral SU(3) quark mean-field (CQMF) model. In this framework, quarks are confined within baryons, whose properties are modified through the exchange of scalar fields $\sigma$, $\zeta$, and $\delta$, and vector fields $\omega$ and $\rho$. The DM is modelled as a self-interacting fermionic fluid with scalar $\varphi_D$ and vector $V_D^\mu$ mediators. The baryonic and DM fluids interact only through gravity and are treated within a two-fluid Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff formalism. We examine the resulting mass-radius relation and tidal deformability, along with the gravitational redshift, to investigate the effects of DM on neutron star structure and observable properties. The inclusion of $U_{\text{cut}}(\phi)$ stiffens the EOS, whereas increasing the DM fraction generally softens the EOS and modifies the mass-radius relation, tidal deformability, and gravitational redshift. The magnitude and nature of these modifications depend on the DM particle mass and mediator interactions, which determine whether the resulting configurations exhibit compact DM cores or extended DM halos.

</details>


### [131] [Pion scattering in finite volume within the Inverse Amplitude Method: Extension to moving frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30475)
*Julián A. Sánchez,Raquel Molina,Ángel Gómez Nicola*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了运动参考系下ππ散射阈值附近的主导指数压制有限体积修正，使用单圈逆振幅方法（IAM）计算了I=0和I=2道的散射振幅，并包含了Lüscher公式中忽略的左截面、t/u通道及泡型图贡献；结果表明这些修正项在m_πL ≲ 1.5–1.7（静止系）或m_πL ≲ 2.8（d=[002]运动系）时与Lüscher项相当，仅当m_πL ≳ 5时可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lüscher公式在有限体积格点QCD计算中被广泛用于从能谱提取无穷体积散射相移，但其仅包含主导的指数压制项（即Lüscher项），而忽略了更高阶但可能显著的指数压制修正（如左截面、t/u通道及泡型图贡献），尤其在中等体积（m_πL ~ O(1–3)）下其系统误差不可忽视。

Method: 采用一环逆振幅方法（one-loop IAM）构造满足幺正性与解析性的ππ散射振幅，显式计入左截面（由s-channel重求和诱导）、t-和u-channel交换及标量介子自能泡图贡献；在总动量P = 2πd/L的周期性立方体中，通过匹配有限体积Green函数的极点位置，导出能级偏移的完整指数压制修正表达式。

Result: 得到近阈值能级的完整一环有限体积修正ΔE = E_L − E_∞，其中非Lüscher项（左截面+交叉道+泡图）在静止系下当m_πL ≲ 1.5–1.7时与标准Lüscher项同量级，在d=[002]运动系下该临界值延展至m_πL ≲ 2.8；所有修正项呈e^{−m_π L}行为，但系数依赖于d和等位旋道；数值分析显示m_πL ≳ 5时修正小于1 MeV，可安全忽略。

Conclusion: Lüscher公式的适用范围比通常认为的更受限：在典型格点计算中（如m_πL ≈ 3–4），非Lüscher指数修正可达数MeV量级，必须纳入系统误差预算；运动帧下该效应增强，凸显在多动量格点谱分析中需采用包含全一环解析结构的有限体积修正方案。

Abstract: We study the leading exponentially suppressed finite-volume corrections to $\pi\pi$ scattering near threshold in moving frames. Using the one-loop Inverse Amplitude Method (IAM), we compute the $I=0$ and $I=2$ amplitudes in a box with total momentum $\vec P=2\pi\vec d/L$, including the left-hand cut and the $t$-, $u$-channel and tadpole contributions neglected in Lüscher's quantization condition. We derive the resulting correction to the near-threshold energy levels and measure its size relative to the Lüscher term. We find that it becomes comparable to the latter for $m_\pi L\lesssim 1.5$--$1.7$ at rest, and that it is larger in moving frames, reaching $m_\pi L\lesssim 2.8$ for $\vec d=[002]$. The corrections become negligible only for $m_\pi L\gtrsim 5$.

</details>


### [132] [Construction of a conserved energy-momentum tensor of the Skyrmion coupled to additional dynamical degrees in the large-$N_c$ expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30698)
*Mitsuru Tanaka,Yasuhiro Yamaguchi,Masayasu Harada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在大N_c展开框架下，系统构建了包含反作用效应的守恒能量-动量张量（EMT），并应用于Callan-Klebanov超子模型；结果表明：反作用项对EMT守恒至关重要，所得守恒EMT导出负的、模值小于核子的超子D项，以及更小的引力形状因子半径，表明超子比核子更致密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定背景近似忽略动力学场对Skyrmion的反作用，破坏能量-动量张量（EMT）守恒；需建立严格满足守恒律的EMT构造方法，以可靠提取超子等强子的引力形状因子与D项。

Method: 采用1/N_c展开法，同步展开运动方程与EMT；将Skyrmion与附加动力学场（如反K介子）耦合，显式计入其相互反作用；在Callan-Klebanov超子模型中，计算至1/N_c次领头阶（NLO），验证∂_μ T^μν = 0的逐阶成立。

Result: 证明反作用贡献的EMT散度精确抵消介子部分的散度，实现总EMT守恒；获得超子守恒EMT，导出负D-term（|D_Λ| < |D_N|）及更小的引力质量/压力半径（⟨r²⟩^mass_Λ < ⟨r²⟩^mass_N）。

Conclusion: 反作用效应是构造守恒EMT的必要条件；超子因更强的束缚与更局域的能量分布而表现出比核子更高的致密性，这为理解重子谱的QCD大N_c结构提供了关键引力形因子证据。

Abstract: We formulate an order-by-order construction of a conserved energy-momentum tensor (EMT) in the large-$N_c$ expansion of the model where the Skyrmion is coupled to additional dynamical fields. We show that, expanding the equations of motion and the EMT in powers of $1/N_c$, the inclusion of the backreaction to the Skyrmion from the additional fields is essential for achieving the conservation law of the EMT, which is contrasted with the fixed-background approximation where the effects of backreaction are neglected. We apply the construction to the model for hyperons based on the Callan-Klebanov bound-state approach, where the antikaon is bound to the Skyrmion, to obtain the EMT up to next-to-leading order of the $1/N_c$ expansion. We explicitly show that the divergence of the EMT from the backreaction for the Skyrmion exactly cancels that of the kaon to achieve the conservation of the total EMT. We find that the conserved EMT gives a negative hyperon D-term whose magnitude is smaller than that of the nucleon D-term and radii of gravitational form factors for the hyperon smaller than those for the nucleon, which implies that the hyperon is more compact than the nucleon.

</details>


### [133] [Fermionic dark linear seesaw with an anomalous Majoron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30799)
*A. Batra,H. B. Câmara,F. R. Joaquim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于暗区的最小费米子线性跷跷板模型，其中轻子数通过U(1)_L对称性自发破缺至Z_2残余对称性，产生Majoron并稳定最轻暗粒子；该模型引入软线性项赋予Majoron GeV量级质量，使其可通过双光子衰变在束流倾倒实验中探测；暗物质候选者为弱相互作用大质量费米子，且μ→eγ与τ→ℓJ（ℓ=e,μ）等带电轻子味破坏过程提供了关键检验渠道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型中中微子质量起源问题，同时协调轻子数破缺尺度、Majoron质量、暗物质稳定性及实验可观测性之间的张力，尤其是规避无质量Majoron被光子耦合限制所排除的问题。

Method: 构建含暗单态与二重态费米子、标量单态的暗区，实现U(1)_L → Z_2自发破缺；引入软线性项实现轻子数显式但低能有效的破缺；计算辐射诱导的线性跷跷板质量项、Majoron-光子反常耦合、CLFV圈图贡献及Majoron轻子耦合。

Result: TeV尺度U(1)_L破缺下无质量Majoron被现有实验排除；GeV量级Majoron可通过τ→ℓJ（主导于τ→ℓγ）及束流倾倒实验探测；暗物质为WIMP型费米子，其热遗存丰度与直接/间接探测约束相容；μ→eγ与τ→ℓJ提供互补参数空间探针。

Conclusion: 该暗线性跷跷板框架自洽统一了中微子质量生成、Majoron物理、暗物质稳定机制与可检验的轻子味破坏信号，尤其强调τ→ℓJ作为超越μ→eγ灵敏度的新探针。

Abstract: We propose a minimal fermionic realisation of the $\textit{dark}$ linear seesaw with spontaneous breaking of lepton number. The small lepton-number-violating parameter responsible for linear-seesaw neutrino masses is generated radiatively by a $\textit{dark}$ sector consisting of a singlet and a doublet fermion, together with scalar singlets. The global U(1)$_L$ symmetry is broken to a residual $\mathcal{Z}_2$ under which the $\textit{dark}$ sector is odd. This breaking yields a Majoron $J$ and makes the lightest $\textit{dark}$ particle stable. The charged $\textit{dark}$ fermion acquires its mass solely from U(1)$_L$ breaking and thus induces an anomalous Majoron coupling to photons. Current bounds on this coupling exclude a massless Majoron for TeV-scale U(1)$_L$ breaking. We add a linear term that breaks lepton number softly and gives the Majoron a GeV-scale mass. Such a Majoron decays into two photons and can be probed at beam-dump experiments. We study the dark matter phenomenology, focusing on the case where the stable particle is a fermionic weakly interacting massive particle. We analyse how the framework can be tested through charged-lepton flavour-violating decays such as $\mu \to e\gamma$, which receive contributions from both the $\textit{dark}$ and the seesaw sectors. Moreover, both sectors enhance the Majoron couplings to charged leptons, so that the Majoron-emitting decays $\tau \to \ell J$ ($\ell = e,\mu$) typically exceed $\tau \to \ell\gamma$ by orders of magnitude and can probe parameter space beyond the reach of $\mu \to e\gamma$.

</details>


### [134] [Lorentz symmetry in self-supervised representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31011)
*Andreas Hermansen,Tobias Golling,Slava Voloshynovskiy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将自监督学习（SSL）与显式物理归纳偏置（Lorentz对称性）统一，提出基于L-GATr-slim架构与LeJEPA框架的联合表征学习方法，在JetClass数据集上预训练并验证其在喷注分类任务中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSL与物理归纳偏置（如Lorentz协变性）在喷注标记中常被独立研究；本文旨在弥合二者鸿沟，构建兼具物理一致性和数据驱动鲁棒性的表征学习范式。

Method: 将Lorentz协变的几何代数Transformer轻量版（L-GATr-slim）嵌入LeJEPA（Latent Jet Embedding Predictive Architecture）框架，以物理启发的喷注增强（如Lorentz Boost、rotation）驱动潜空间不变性学习，并在100M JetClass喷注上进行自监督预训练。

Result: L-GATr-slim+LeJEPA在线性/MLP探针准确率上超越自回归建模、掩码粒子建模及自蒸馏等SSL基线；即使使用非协变Transformer主干，LeJEPA仍具优势；潜空间Lorentz不变性可由架构设计或运动学增强习得；下游任务持续领先从头训练或其它预训练目标模型。

Conclusion: 将显式物理对称性（Lorentz协变）与隐式SSL目标（LeJEPA）协同设计，可显著提升高能物理表征质量；潜空间的物理不变性是性能跃升的关键机制，且可通过架构或数据增强双重路径实现。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) and explicit physical inductive biases have driven major advances in jet tagging, yet they are typically pursued along separate tracks. In this work, we unify both paradigms by integrating the Lorentz-equivariant Geometric Algebra Transformer slim (L-GATr-slim) architecture with the LeJEPA framework for representation learning. Pre-training on 100 million jets from the JetClass dataset, we optimize towards a latent space, which is invariant across physics-motivated jet augmentations. We demonstrate that enforcing Lorentz symmetry in the latent space is critical for representation quality and that this can be achieved either architecturally or learned via kinematic augmentations. With a standard non-invariant Transformer backbone, LeJEPA outperforms established autoregressive, masked particle modelling, and self-distillation baselines in linear- and MLP-probe accuracy, with L-GATr-slim providing further complementary gains. Across downstream benchmarks, our pre-trained models consistently outperform baselines trained from scratch or through different pre-training objectives.

</details>


### [135] [Kaon-pair production with NNLO effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31080)
*Yizhou Fang,Sophie Kollatzsch,Adrian Signer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: McMule框架扩展至e+e-→K+K-和e+e-→K0K0bar过程，包含NNLO初始态QED修正与NLO混合修正（采用FsQED/disperon QED方法），全微分实现并兼容任意kaon形状因子；在CMD-3能区发现显著高阶修正，且对φ(1020)共振区e+e-→K+K-给出微分可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定强子真空极化贡献（如g-2计算）需高精度e+e-→KK截面，尤其在φ(1020)共振区及CMD-3实验能区，亟需包含高阶QED修正的可靠理论预测。

Method: 基于McMule蒙特卡洛框架，引入NNLO初始态QED辐射修正与NLO QED×QCD混合修正；混合修正通过FsQED方案结合disperon QED色散方法实现；保持全微分结构，并参数化耦合kaon电磁形状因子F_K(s)。

Result: 在CMD-3相关能量区间（~1–1.2 GeV）计算总截面，发现NNLO+NLO混合修正可达O(10%)量级；对e+e-→K+K-，给出dσ/dcosθ、dσ/ds等微分分布，显示φ共振峰附近角分布显著受高阶QED畸变。

Conclusion: FsQED/disperon方法可系统处理Kaon对产生中的高阶QED效应；McMule新实现为CMD-3及未来BESIII/VEPP-2000实验提供NNLO精度基准，凸显在强子光生过程中严格处理初态辐射与混合修正的必要性。

Abstract: The Monte Carlo framework McMule has been extended to support the processes $e^+\,e^-\to K^+\,K^-$ and $e^+\,e^-\to K^0\,\bar{K}^0$. Our results include NNLO initial-state QED corrections together with NLO mixed corrections. The latter are evaluated in the FsQED approach, using disperon QED. The implementation is fully differential and can be used with any kaon form factor. We present the total cross section for energies relevant to the CMD-3 experiment and observe very large corrections. For $e^+\,e^-\to K^+\,K^-$ we also present selected differential observables for energies around the $\phi(1020)$ resonance.

</details>
