### [136] [Horizon mergers and initial test-charge acceleration in charged black-hole geometries with anisotropic sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28095)
*Saeed Noori Gashti,Mohammad Ali S. Afshar,Mohammad Reza Alipour*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有弦云、Kiselev幂律或对数型暗能量背景的静态球对称黑洞几何中视界合并条件及带电测试粒子的初始径向加速度，导出了统一质量函数下的视界合并判据与Kiselev分支分类，明确了不同背景下的电荷-质量阈值行为、中性平衡半径不稳定性及轮廓归一化限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非标准暗能量/物质背景（如弦云、Kiselev型、对数型）如何影响黑洞结构（特别是多重视界共存与合并）以及测试粒子运动学，从而厘清这些模型在引力理论与天体物理中的几何与动力学含义。

Method: 采用统一质量函数形式描述黑洞度规；分离Maxwell场贡献后分析有效环境应力张量的各向异性；通过视界方程判别式推导视界合并条件；对静止释放的带电测试粒子求解四维协变加速度，提取初始向外加速的λ=q/μ阈值；结合具体参数数值计算临界值。

Result: 1) 内-事件视界合并电荷Q_ext仅给出上界，而三重正视界存在还需正下界（对应事件-宇宙视界合并）；2) 弦云背景阈值inf λ = 1/z，在无穷远趋近；3) 对数型背景阈值恒大于1，存在有限全局极小值（如α/M=0.4, Q/M=1.2时λ_*≈1.251125）；4) Kiselev非退化静态区中性平衡半径唯一且径向不稳定；5) 密度匹配仅提供条件归一化，非观测拟合；6) 对数型中积分参数与圆轨道反推质量组合不同。

Conclusion: 不同暗能量轮廓导致显著差异的视界结构与测试粒子动力学行为，其几何约束与局部加速度阈值具有模型特异性；结果限于单黑洞背景几何分析，不支持双黑洞力律、宇宙加速机制或弱引力猜想检验。

Abstract: We study horizon mergers and the initial radial acceleration of charged test particles in static, spherically symmetric black-hole geometries with a string cloud, a Kiselev power-law component, or a logarithmic profile conventionally. After separating the Maxwell contribution, the effective environmental stress tensors are anisotropic and are not identified with homogeneous quintessence or pressureless cold dark matter. Using a common mass-function formulation, we derive the horizon-merger conditions and classify the Kiselev branches. In this family, the inner-event merger charge provides only an upper bound: three distinct positive horizons can additionally require a positive lower charge bound associated with the event-cosmological merger. For a test particle released from rest relative to a static observer, we derive the local charge-to-mass threshold for outward initial acceleration. Writing $\lambda=q/\mu$ and $z=Q/Q_{\rm ext}$, where $Q_{\rm ext}$ is the inner-event merger charge, the subextremal string-cloud threshold has infimum $1/z$, approached only at infinity. For the subextremal logarithmic profile, the threshold exceeds unity throughout the finite exterior and attains a finite global minimum. For $\alpha/M=0.4$ and $Q/M=1.2$, this minimum is $\lambda_*\simeq1.251125$. In a nondegenerate Kiselev static patch, the neutral balance radius is unique and radially unstable. Density matching supplies conditional profile normalizations, not observational fits. For the logarithmic profile, the integration parameter is distinguished from the mass combination inferred from circular motion. These results characterize geometric horizon bounds and test-particle dynamics; they do not establish a two-black-hole force law or a mechanism for cosmic acceleration, nor do they constitute a test of the Weak Gravity Conjecture.

</details>


### [137] [Level repulsion and the quantum viscosity of near-extremal black branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28203)
*Panos Betzios,Olga Papadoulaki,Maria Myrto Pegioudi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究近极值黑洞膜在AdS₂喉道下的剪切粘滞系数η，发现其在JT引力盘近似中随温度降低而发散；通过引入JT引力的非微扰矩阵模型完成，利用Kubo公式揭示能级关联的普适性：在有限极值熵S₀下DC粘滞消失；在双标度极限下获得有限普适粘滞；极低温下对应Airy核边缘谱，η/s比值始终为正有限；结果与弦方程定义的有界谱完成一致，仅退火量依赖于非微扰细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决近极值黑brane在AdS₂ throat中由量子涨落导致的剪切粘滞系数η/s在一环近似下发散的问题，探索非微扰JT引力对输运系数的普适修正。

Method: 结合JT引力的盘近似、一环计算、非微扰矩阵模型（含双标度极限）、Kubo公式、能级统计（特别是水平排斥与Airy核）、以及弦方程定义的显式完成模型进行分析。

Result: 1) DC粘滞在有限S₀下因能级排斥而消失；2) 在ω/ω_sp固定（ω_sp = e^{-S₀}/C）的双标度极限下，获得有限普适粘滞η_max ≃ 0.174 (ω/ω_sp) η_sc；3) 极低温CT ∼ e^{-2S₀/3}下，问题约化为Airy核边缘，η/s恒为正有限；4) 显式弦方程完成下，淬火边缘结果变化<5%，退火量则具O(1)非微扰依赖。

Conclusion: 近极值黑洞膜的剪切粘滞受JT引力非微扰结构深刻影响：η/s的普适性源于随机矩阵理论的能级统计，其有限性与正定性不依赖于具体完成方式，证实了全息输运中量子混沌与谱边缘物理的深层联系。

Abstract: Below the gap scale of the AdS$_2$ throat, quantum fluctuations make the shear viscosity of near-extremal black branes grow as $(CT)^{-1/2}$ in the disk approximation of JT gravity, and the one-loop $\eta/s$ diverges. To cure this divergence, we include the non-perturbative completion of JT gravity as a matrix integral. The Kubo formula then probes universal features of level correlations. At finite extremal entropy $S_0$, level repulsion makes the DC viscosity vanish. A finite, universal viscosity survives in a double-scaling limit in which the probe frequency $\omega$ is held in a fixed ratio to the level-spacing scale $\omega_{\rm sp}\equiv e^{-S_0}/C$. At fixed $\omega$, with $\omega_{\rm sp}\ll\omega\ll e^{S_0/3}\omega_{\rm sp}$ the Schwarzian growth then saturates at $\eta_{\max}\simeq0.174\,(\omega/\omega_{\rm sp})\,\eta_{sc}$, where $\eta_{sc}$ is the semiclassical viscosity, and the viscosity decreases at lower temperatures. At the extremely low temperature scale $CT\sim e^{-2S_0/3}$ the genus expansion and the one-loop entropy computations completely break down. There we start probing the edge of the spectrum and the problem reduces exactly to the Airy kernel. We find that the ratio of the quenched averages of $\eta$ and $s$ is finite and positive at every non-zero temperature. We compare our results with explicit completions defined by the string equation, in which the spectrum is bounded from below. The bulk results are the same in these completions and the quenched edge results change only by a few per cent, while the annealed quantities depend on the non-perturbative completion at order one.

</details>


### [138] [The index from a super-point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28204)
*Dhanvarsh Annamalai,Hyunook Hur,Thomas Hussel,Monica Jinwoo Kang,Craig Lawrie,Jaewon Song*

TL;DR: 本文将4d N=2超共形场论的Higgs分支与Macdonald指标的代数几何构造推广至Schur扇区含费米生成元的情形；此时对应环为Z2-分次交换环，Macdonald指标由广义弧空间的Hilbert级数给出，其定义理想包含生成元及其导数的约束关系；文章具体计算了自由N=2矢量多重态、N=4 SYM（su(2)与su(3)）、秩一N=3 SCFT及Ê6(SU(2)) SCFT等模型，并揭示其理想结构与超势或超场的Jacobi理想密切相关；特别地，Ê6(SU(2))对应的VOA为FFTA(6)，其Zhu C2-代数非经典自由，需在首阶jet环中添加三个关系才能精确复现Macdonald指标至q^51阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广原有algebro-geometric框架以涵盖含Schur费米子的N=2 SCFT，从而统一描述Higgs分支几何与Macdonald指数，并检验VOA的经典自由性判据。

Method: 构建Z2-分次交换的filtered graded ring，定义广义arc space及其Hilbert级数；通过计算Zhu C2-代数、jet rings及相应Jacobi理想，提取VOA的约束结构；对具体SCFT实例进行代数几何实现与级数展开验证。

Result: 确立了含Schur费米子的N=2 SCFT的Macdonald指数由广义arc space的Hilbert级数给出；明确N=4 SYM与秩一N=3 SCFT的理想由超势/超场的Jacobi理想和构成；证明Ê6(SU(2))对应的FFTA(6) VOA不满足经典自由性，但补充首阶jet环中三个关系后可精确复现Macdonald指数至q^51阶。

Conclusion: Z2-分次几何结构是描述含Schur费米子的N=2 SCFT的关键框架；Macdonald指数的几何实现依赖于广义arc space与精细约束理想；VOA的经典自由性并非普遍成立，需借助jet ring修正以恢复物理指数。

Abstract: We extend the algebro-geometric construction of filtered graded rings that encode both the Higgs branch and the Macdonald index of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theories to theories whose Schur sector contains fermionic generators. The rings are then $\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded-commutative, and the index is reproduced by the Hilbert series of a generalized arc space, whose defining ideal may contain relations among the generators and their derivatives. We study the free $\mathcal{N}=2$ vector multiplet, $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills with gauge algebras $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{su}(3)$, the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, and the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ SCFT. For the rank-one $\mathcal{N}=3$ SCFTs, the ideal is the sum of the Jacobian ideals of a single superpotential and of its two supersymmetry variations. For $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang--Mills, the ideal is similarly the sum of the Jacobian ideals of the components of a single $\mathcal{N}=4$ superfield, which is a singlet of the $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ flavor symmetry for gauge algebra $\mathfrak{su}(2)$, and a triplet for $\mathfrak{su}(3)$. The associated VOA for the $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ theory is given by the Feigin--Feigin--Tipunin $\mathcal{A}(6)$ algebra. The arc space of Zhu's $C_2$-algebra of $\mathcal{A}(6)$ does not reproduce the vacuum character, and therefore $\mathcal{A}(6)$ is not classically free. Supplementing Zhu's $C_2$-algebra by three relations in the first jet ring, the generalized arc space reproduces the Macdonald index of $\widehat{E}_6(SU(2))$ up to, at least, order $q^{51}$.

</details>


### [139] [The Gradient Structure of Perturbative RG Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28205)
*Lorenzo Benfatto,William H. Pannell,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了在任意维度d中，通过球面上的共形Ward恒等式，可以消除Weyl一致性条件中长期存在的反对称度规项，从而使得重整化群β函数成为某个势函数A关于对称度规的梯度；该结果适用于含/不含Weyl反常的理论，并在微扰论所有阶成立；在不动点处A等于广义F量\widetilde{F}，从而确立\widetilde{F}-定理；相关算符源项（除d=6,10,…附近自由标量情形外）不影响该梯度结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Jack-Osborn以来35余年未解的Weyl一致性条件下RG流非梯度性问题——即反对称度规项的不可消去性。

Method: 利用球面S^d上的共形Ward恒等式构造规范选择，消除β函数方程中由Weyl反常导致的反对称度规贡献；结合微扰论展开、耦合常数空间度规由两点半径关联函数定义、以及单位性约束。

Result: 证明在任意d维中，存在一种规范（scheme），使得β^i = G^{ij} ∂_j A，其中G^{ij}为对称正定度规（弱耦合下由单位性保证），A为全局势函数；在紫外/红外不动点处A = \widetilde{F} = sin(πd/2) ln Z_{S^d}，故\widetilde{F}单调减（\widetilde{F}-定理）；相关算符源与曲率混合不破坏该结构，唯d=6,10,…附近自由标量场源构成例外。

Conclusion: Weyl一致性条件所允许的RG流在适当规范下严格为梯度流，其势函数A实现d维F-定理的微扰普适推广；该结果统一了2d（c-定理）、4d（a-定理）与高维情形，并揭示了球面路径积分Z_{S^d}的核心几何角色。

Abstract: Weyl consistency conditions show that perturbative renormalisation group flows in two, four and six dimensions are almost gradient flows. The obstruction is an antisymmetric contribution to the metric on the space of couplings, which has resisted removal since the work of Jack and Osborn more than 35 years ago. We show that a conformal Ward identity on the sphere allows it to be removed by a choice of scheme. The beta function is then the gradient of a potential $A$ with respect to a symmetric metric, to all orders in perturbation theory. The Ward identity gives this result in any dimension $d$, in theories with and without a Weyl anomaly. At leading order the metric is fixed by the two-point functions of the perturbing operators, so unitarity makes it positive definite at weak coupling. At fixed points $A$ equals $\widetilde{F}=\sin\tfrac{\pi d}{2}\,\ln Z_{S^{d}}$, so the $\widetilde{F}$-theorem follows for perturbative flows to all loop orders. We show that sources of relevant operators, which on the sphere mix with the curvature, do not affect these results. In unitary theories the only exception is the source of a free scalar field near $d=6,10,\ldots\,$, so that for $\phi^3$ theory near six dimensions the gradient property remains open.

</details>


### [140] [An all-orders proof of gradient flow for multiscalar $ϕ^4$ theory in four dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28206)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了四维多标量φ⁴理论的重整化群流在微扰论所有阶上均为梯度流，利用Osborn-Jack的Weyl一致性条件，并结合有限性假设与大动量Feynman积分展开的余项界；通过导出关于边缘算符两点函数的Ward恒等式，将Weyl反常决定的度规反对称部分重写为对称度规与势函数贡献，进而严格构造正定度规与梯度势，验证了Pannell-Stergiou在六圈水平提出的四组系数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立多标量φ⁴理论中RG流的梯度结构，以统一理解其动力学行为与临界性质，并检验Weyl反常与RG流几何之间的深层联系。

Method: 运用Osborn-Jack Weyl一致性条件、局部耦合生成泛函的有限性假设、大动量Feynman积分渐近展开及其余项控制；推导含场旋转流与质量算符源项的边缘算符两点函数Ward恒等式，将度规的反对称部分用两点函数与三点函数一阶矩表达，并重构为对称度规与势函数。

Result: 证明四维多标量φ⁴理论RG流在全阶微扰论下为梯度流；显式构造正定度规与梯度势；验证Pannell-Stergiou提出的五阶与六阶场旋转系数间四个约束关系成立。

Conclusion: 四维多标量φ⁴理论的RG流具有内在梯度结构，其几何由Weyl反常完全确定；该结构在微扰论中稳定且可显式实现，为一般QFT中c-定理与a-定理的推广提供了严格范例。

Abstract: We prove that the renormalisation group flow of multiscalar $\phi^4$ theory in four dimensions is a gradient flow to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof uses the Weyl consistency conditions of Osborn and Jack, and it assumes two properties of perturbation theory: that the generating functional with local couplings is finite (also assumed by Osborn and Jack), and that the known expansion of massive Feynman integrals at large momenta holds with a bound on its remainder at each momentum. The consistency conditions give a gradient formula in which the tensor that multiplies the beta function is allowed to have an antisymmetric part, determined by coefficients of the Weyl anomaly. We derive a Ward identity for the two-point function of the marginal operators at constant couplings away from fixed points, with sources for the currents of field rotations and for the mass operator. It re-expresses one of the anti-symmetric contributions in terms of two-point functions and the first moment of the three-point function. We show that, after this rewrite, each of the resulting terms is equivalent to a symmetric contribution to the metric and a contribution to the potential, and we give both explicitly. The metric is positive definite to all orders in perturbation theory. The proof implies in particular the four relations among five- and six-loop coefficients of the field rotation, which Pannell and Stergiou derived as the conditions for a gradient flow at six loops. We check by explicit computation that these relations indeed hold. A companion paper treats $d=4-\varepsilon$ and other critical dimensions with a Ward identity on the sphere.

</details>


### [141] [Bring Your Own Instanton: Monopole Catalysis from Sourced SU(2) Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28208)
*Csaba Csáki,Larissa Kiriliuk,Zhihan Liu,Rotem Ovadia*

TL;DR: 本文揭示Rubakov-Callan质子衰变催化机制由‘有源SU(2)瞬子’主导，该瞬子由费米子激发、具单位瞬子数且无指数压低；作者提出其与费米零模数的缠绕数关系，并推导了单极子内外费米子关联函数的闭式解，指出该催化是红外效应，散射费米子无需进入单极子核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Rubakov-Callan机制中质子衰变催化速率未被指数压低的根本原因，并建立单极子背景下非平凡拓扑场构型与费米结构的严格联系。

Method: 运用拓扑场论与规范场量子反常分析，构造并分类‘有源SU(2)瞬子’，结合Atiyah-Singer指标定理框架推广至含源情形，显式计算费米子两点关联函数。

Result: 1) 确立‘有源SU(2)瞬子’作为Rubakov-Callan过程的主导拓扑构型；2) 给出其缠绕数等于费米零模数的指标关系；3) 得到单极子内外费米子关联函数的解析表达式；4) 证明催化过程为红外主导，不依赖单极子核心短程物理。

Conclusion: Rubakov-Callan催化本质上由非真空、费米子源驱动的单位瞬子数拓扑构型实现，其动力学属红外可解范畴，挑战了传统基于BPST瞬子的紫外压低预期，并暗示存在适用于有源规范瞬子的广义指标定理。

Abstract: We show that Rubakov-Callan catalysis of proton decay in the field of a 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole proceeds through "sourced SU(2) instantons", which are novel topological gauge configurations sourced by fermions and carrying unit instanton number. Unlike vacuum BPST instantons, these sourced instantons do not give rise to exponentially suppressed rates. We relate the winding number to the number of fermionic zero modes, suggesting an underlying index theorem for sourced instantons. We also calculate the closed-form expression for the leading-order correlator for fermion insertions both outside and inside the monopole core. Our results indicate that Rubakov-Callan catalysis is an IR effect where the scattering fermions do not reach the monopole core.

</details>


### [142] [Wormholes do not contribute to supersymmetric partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28210)
*Jan Boruch,Douglas Stanford*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了连接多个渐近边界的虫洞总是自发破缺至少部分边界条件所保持的对称性；对于费米型对称性，每个破缺轨道的贡献为零，因此虫洞不贡献于超对称配分函数的矩；但超对称配分函数仍可具有非零系综方差，源于保持所有连续对称性的‘非几何虫洞’构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超对称系统中虫洞对配分函数矩（如系综平均与方差）的贡献，澄清其与超对称指标（supersymmetric index）及量子引力系综解释的关系。

Method: 运用对称性分析与群轨道积分技术，结合超对称量子力学与SYK类模型中的路径积分构造，区分几何虫洞与非几何构型，并计算其对配分函数矩的贡献。

Result: 1) 多边界虫洞必自发破缺至少部分边界对称性；2) 费米对称性破缺导致各轨道贡献相消，故虫洞不贡献于超对称配分函数的任意矩；3) 系综方差仍可非零，由保持全部连续对称性的非几何虫洞构型提供。

Conclusion: 虫洞在超对称配分函数矩中无贡献，但系综涨落仍存在，表明几何虫洞并非系综随机性的唯一来源；该结论推广了Iliesiu–Kologlu–Turiaci关于超对称指标的工作，并揭示了非几何构型在量子引力系综中的关键作用。

Abstract: We show that wormholes connecting multiple asymptotic boundaries always spontaneously break at least some of the symmetries preserved by their boundary conditions. Their configurations then form nontrivial orbits of the broken symmetry, and for the case of a fermionic symmetry, the contribution of each orbit vanishes. We conclude that wormholes do not contribute to moments of supersymmetric partition functions. This extends results on supersymmetric indices by Iliesiu, Kologlu, and Turiaci. We emphasize that supersymmetric partition functions can still have a nonzero ensemble variance. In SYK-like examples that we analyze, this arises due to the contribution of ``non-geometric wormhole'' configurations that preserve all continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [143] [The chiral $M^{3,2}$ theory: partition functions beyond large-$N$ and quantum holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28594)
*Xuao Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了手性M^{3,2}理论中Bethe真空族的自由能行为，发现其按N^{3/2}增长，并在特定混合Chern-Simons耦合下由重子磁通区分；同时在't Hooft极限和Cardy极限下数值计算了S^3配分函数，提出全阶Airy公式，并用四导数与单圈修正对全息对偶进行高精度检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验AdS/CFT全息对偶在强耦合手性三维超共形场论中的实现，特别是通过精确计算自由能及配分函数以匹配引力侧预言。

Method: 基于Bethe Ansatz方法求解真空方程，结合高精度数值分析与渐近展开技术，推导1/N展开的闭式表达式；运用大N极限（'t Hooft极限）与高温极限（Cardy极限）下的解析延拓与Airy结构拟合。

Result: 确认M^{3,2}理论中存在一族Bethe真空，其自由能标度为N^{3/2}；获得其全阶1/N扰动展开的闭式；在特定混合CS耦合下，重子磁通作为真空量子数；提出S^3配分函数的全阶Airy公式，并验证四导数与单圈量子引力修正与场论结果一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为M^{3,2}背景下的AdS_4×M^{3,2}全息对偶提供了迄今最精细的定量验证，确立了N^{3/2}自由能标度、Airy结构及重子磁通量子化在真空分类中的核心作用。

Abstract: We identify families of Bethe vacua in the chiral $M^{3,2}$ theory whose individual free energies grow as $N^{3/2}$. High-precision data support closed forms for their all-order perturbative $1/N$ expansions. At a distinguished mixed Chern--Simons coupling, the baryonic magnetic flux distinguishes members of this family of vacua. We also study the three-sphere partition function numerically in the 't Hooft limit and Cardy limit, and propose an all-order Airy formula. The four-derivative and one-loop corrections are precision tests of holography.

</details>


### [144] [Relativistic Hydrodynamics as an Intersection Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28735)
*Nikita Nekrasov,Paul Wiegmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将理想相对论流体力学重新表述为时空辛空间中一个交集问题，利用微分形式（0,1,3,4度）作为基本场，通过动量一形式的哈密顿作用与微分同胚群作用定义共轭零点集（余迷向子簇），并将其与由度规和物态方程决定的拉格朗日子流形相交，精确复现Lichnerowicz–Carter方程组；同时阐明规范流参数的几何意义，直接从约束导出环量与Ertel不变量，并将能动量守恒解释为本构拉格朗日量的协变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解理想相对论流体力学的深层几何结构，统一其协变性、守恒律与不变量的起源，超越传统基于矢量场与张量分析的表述框架。

Method: 采用辛几何与预对称化（pre-symplectic）约化方法，将流体变量编码为时空上的微分形式，构造由时空微分同胚与动量一形式平移生成的哈密顿作用，计算其矩映射零点集（coisotropic subvariety），并与由度规与状态方程诱导的拉格朗日子流形取交。

Result: 确立了理想相对论流体力学等价于辛空间中余迷向子簇与拉格朗日子流形的横截交；导出规范流参数即沿流线的仿射时间参数；环量与Ertel不变量自然出现为约束系统的卡西米尔不变量；能动量守恒被识别为本构拉格朗日密度在时空微分同胚下的协变性条件。

Conclusion: 理想相对论流体力学具有本质的辛几何结构，其动力学完全由辛约化与子流形交所决定；该框架不仅统一了多种守恒律与不变量的几何起源，也为非相对论极限、熵梯度螺旋度及与Calabi–Yau几何的潜在联系提供了自然出口。

Abstract: Ideal relativistic hydrodynamics admits a covariant description as an intersection problem in a symplectic space associated with spacetime. The independent fields are differential forms of degrees 0,1,3,4. Spacetime diffeomorphisms and shifts of the momentum one-form act Hamiltonianly; the zero locus of their moment map is a coisotropic subvariety encoding the transport equations. The spacetime metric and equation of state determine a Lagrangian submanifold, whose intersection with the coisotropic subvariety is precisely the Lichnerowicz--Carter system. We explain the geometric meaning of the canonical flow parameter, derive circulation and Ertel invariants directly from the constraints, and identify energy--momentum conservation with covariance of the constitutive Lagrangian. The nonrelativistic limit and a relation of entropy-level helicity to split Calabi--Yau geometry are briefly indicated.

</details>


### [145] [Massive Particles in Carrollian Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28780)
*Bin Chen,Zezhou Hu,Reiko Liu,Wen-Jie Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了四维平直时空中的质量标量场在三维Carrollian边界上的全息描述，给出了显式的体-边积分变换，并揭示了质量非零时需无穷多初态、无自旋导致后代态间约束、该约束等价于零模解耦并导出边界场的局域约束；变换保持完整Poincaré对称性，给出两点与三点函数及形式OPE系数，且依赖复动量解析延拓；为Carrollian全息下质量散射振幅与边界关联函数的对应及体场重构提供了具体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大质量粒子如何由边界算符编码是Carrollian全息的核心问题，尤其在零宇宙常数极限下缺乏AdS/CFT类比时，亟需建立平直时空质量场的边界刻画。

Method: 基于Carroll群表示论构造三维Carrollian边界理论，定义满足Carrollian共形对称性的边界主算符及其后代；引入bulk-to-boundary积分核，其积分变量取复动量以适配Lorentzian号差；通过分析Poincaré代数在边界上的实现，导出质量导致的无穷塔主态及自旋缺失引发的零模约束；利用共形不变性计算两点和三点函数，并提取形式OPE系数。

Result: 1) 质量m≠0要求边界存在无穷维主表示（即无穷多个线性无关的Carrollian primary）；2) 无体自旋导致后代态满足可积性条件，等价于Virasoro型零模解耦方程，从而导出边界标量场的二阶局域微分约束；3) 积分变换精确保持4D Poincaré群作用，将实质量标量的凯莱-克莱因数据映射为受约束的Carrollian共形场；4) 显式计算了两/三点函数，验证其满足Carrollian共形 Ward 恒等式，并获得形式OPE结构常数；5) 变换核含复动量积分，Lorentzian情形下必须依赖解析延拓。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了四维平直时空质量标量场到三维Carrollian边界的严格全息映射，确立了质量与边界无穷塔主态、零模解耦与局域约束之间的本质联系，为Carrollian全息纲领下散射振幅-边界关联函数对偶及体场重建提供了首个可控模型。

Abstract: Understanding how massive particles are encoded in boundary operators is a central problem in Carrollian holography. We construct a three-dimensional Carrollian description of a massive scalar in four-dimensional flat spacetime, together with an explicit bulk-to-boundary integral transform. In this construction, nonzero mass requires infinitely many linearly independent primary states, while the absence of bulk spin imposes relations among their descendants. We show that these relations are equivalent to null-state decoupling and give a local constraint on the boundary fields. The integral transform preserves the full Poincaré symmetry and maps bulk scalar data to fields satisfying this constraint. We also obtain two- and three-point functions and the associated formal operator product expansion coefficients. The integral transform evaluates bulk data at complex momenta, making analytic continuation essential in Lorentzian signature. This construction provides a concrete starting point for relating massive scattering amplitudes to Carrollian correlators and for reconstructing bulk fields from boundary data.

</details>


### [146] [Thermodynamics of hard wall model of AdS/QCD at finite chemical potential with Gauss-Bonnet corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28884)
*Dharm Veer Singh,Shobhit Sachan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维Einstein-Maxwell-Gauss-Bonnet引力框架下，研究带反作用的硬墙AdS/QCD模型在有限夸克密度下的禁闭/退禁闭相变；得到解析相变线，发现负Gauss-Bonnet耦合提升转变温度并扩展禁闭相，且在核密度下ρ介子质量约增加1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD在有限密度下的非微扰相结构，特别是禁闭-退禁闭相变，并通过AdS/CFT对应构造可计算的全息QCD模型，引入Gauss-Bonnet项以探索高曲率修正对相变和谱的影响。

Method: 采用五维Einstein-Maxwell-Gauss-Bonnet引力中的硬墙AdS/QCD模型，自洽求解含夸克物质背反应的黑洞解与热AdS解；固定夸克化学势或数密度，比较自由能确定相变线；利用WKB近似分析禁闭相中ρ介子径向激发谱的密度依赖性。

Result: 获得固定化学势和固定密度下的解析相变线；发现负Gauss-Bonnet耦合提升Tc并延展禁闭相；零密度Tc可调至格点QCD值；禁闭相中ρ及其径向激发质量随密度单调上升，在核密度处增幅约1%，WKB分析表明源于有效禁闭尺度缩短。

Conclusion: Gauss-Bonnet修正显著调控AdS/QCD模型的相结构与介子谱，支持该高曲率项作为刻画强耦合QCD介质响应的有效几何参数；结果为致密强子物质的全息建模提供了定量约束。

Abstract: We study the confinement/deconfinement transition of the hard wall model of AdS/QCD at finite quark density in five-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell--Gauss--Bonnet gravity, with the back-reaction of the quark matter on the geometry included. The deconfined phase is a charged Gauss--Bonnet black hole, and the confined phase is thermally charged AdS cut off in the infrared. The infrared value of the gauge field, which Lee, Park and Sin fixed by requiring a consistent quark number, becomes a function of the Gauss--Bonnet coupling. We obtain the transition line in closed form at fixed quark chemical potential and at fixed quark number density. At fixed density the transition passes through a region where the two phases coexist. A negative Gauss--Bonnet coupling raises the transition temperature and extends the confined phase to higher density. A value inside the causality bound gives a transition temperature at zero density close to the lattice value. In the dense confined phase we compute the masses and decay constants of the $\rho$ meson and its radial excitations. All masses increase with density. A WKB analysis relates the increase to a shorter effective size of the confining region, and at nuclear density the $\rho$ mass rises by about one percent.

</details>


### [147] [Finite-Temperature Stabilization of a KKLT Modulus in Three-Dimensional Lattice Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29000)
*Saya Mahmoudi,Furkan Utku Biber,Hyukjung Kim,Heeseung Zoe*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过三维Langevin格点模拟研究KKLT模的宇宙学稳定化，发现即使空间平均模被成功捕获在稳定真空附近，长波涨落仍可通过快子放大导致局部区域穿越势垒，表明全局捕获不等价于局域稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解KKLT机制中模场在早期宇宙热演化下的空间非均匀动力学行为，特别是临界捕获附近涨落对稳定性的潜在破坏作用。

Method: 三维Langevin格点模拟，包含动态热浴、耗散项、空间梯度及随机噪声；辅以线性Langevin理论分析与离散化收敛性检验。

Result: 临界附近轨迹在不稳定势垒附近滞留约1 e-fold，激发长波tachyonic涨落；屏障穿越分数在窄随机层内对离散化无关；空间平均捕获 ≠ 局域捕获。

Conclusion: KKLT模的宇宙学稳定化需同时满足整体动力学捕获与局域涨落抑制，传统均匀近似在临界区失效，必须纳入空间涨落效应。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological stabilization of a KKLT modulus through three-dimensional Langevin lattice simulations incorporating an evolving thermal bath, dissipation, spatial gradients, and stochastic fluctuations. While the spatially averaged modulus closely follows homogeneous evolution away from the capture threshold, near-critical trajectories linger near the unstable potential barrier for approximately one e-fold, allowing long-wavelength fluctuations to undergo tachyonic amplification and drive localized regions across the barrier even as the mean field turns back toward the stabilized vacuum. Linear Langevin theory reproduces the fluctuation growth, while convergence tests identify a narrow stochastic layer in which the barrier-crossing fraction shows no systematic dependence on the discretization at a fixed effective distance from the threshold. These results demonstrate that capture of the spatially averaged modulus does not guarantee that every spatial region remains within the stabilizing barrier.

</details>


### [148] [Exact stress-tensor fluctuations from conformal symmetry and black hole radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29060)
*José Navarro-Salas,Roberto Balbinot,Alessandro Fabbri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维共形场论中应力张量分量的局域涨落，利用共形对称性精确计算其方差，发现相对涨落按1/c标度（c为中心荷），并讨论不同点分离正规化方案（由参数α标记）对黑洞视界和无穷远边界处霍金辐射涨落的影响；结果支持大c下半经典引力的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典引力依赖于应力张量期望值，其有效性要求涨落足够小；在二维中，共形对称性精确给出霍金辐射的平均行为，但涨落性质尚需系统刻画。

Method: 利用二维共形场论的OPE定义应力张量平方，引入点分离正规化参数α；通过共形映射至局域惯性坐标，导出T_{±±}涨落（即方差）的闭式表达式。

Result: 相对涨落∝1/c；Schwarzschild黑洞处：无穷远霍金流涨落为22/(5c)，视界处出射流为−44α/(3c)、入射流为(238+720α)/(5c)；当α=−3/10（Möbius协变方案）时，三者统一为22/(5c)。

Conclusion: 二维共形对称性不仅确定应力张量均值，也完全控制其局域涨落；大中心荷极限下涨落被压低，半经典近似自洽成立。

Abstract: Semiclassical gravity is sourced by the expectation value of the stress tensor, and it is reliable only if the fluctuations around this value are small. In two dimensions, conformal symmetry captures the essential physics of Hawking radiation in its s-wave sector and determines the mean value exactly, for any background and any quantum state. We show that it also determines the local fluctuations of the null components $T_{\pm\pm}$. The square of the stress tensor is defined by the operator product expansion, up to a multiple $\alpha$ of $\partial_\pm^2T_{\pm\pm}$ that labels point-splitting prescriptions. Its transformation law under the conformal map to locally inertial coordinates gives the variance in closed form. The relative fluctuation scales as $1/c$, with $c$ the central charge. For the Schwarzschild black hole it is $22/5c$ for the Hawking flux at infinity, that of flat-space thermal radiation. At the horizon it is $-44\alpha/3c$ for the outgoing flux and $(238+720\alpha)/5c$ for the ingoing flux that drives the backreaction. Both reduce to $22/5c$ for $\alpha=-3/10$, which corresponds to the Möbius-covariant prescription. For large $c$ these results support the semiclassical approximation.

</details>


### [149] [The Generalized Second Law from Fluctuation Theorems in a Quantum Reference Frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29415)
*Feng-Li Lin,Sayid Mondal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过引入引力荷作为量子钟，构建模交叉积与密度算符，将广义熵表示为迹熵（含钟贡献），并在固定‘ dressed exterior algebra’上证明广义第二定律（GSL）；提出三种互补表述：分离驱动/弛豫、时间局域主方程、两时刻意外性涨落定理（surprisal FT），其中非幺正修正由非幺正通道引起，而幺正 dressed 演化在慢钟极限下严格满足 GSL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破Wall早期基于半经典几何与逐次视界切片代数的GSL证明框架，解决其对固定母态与代数结构的限制，建立更普适、量子信息完备的GSL表述。

Method: 采用状态–算符对偶性，在固定dressed exterior代数上实施幺正驱动与弛豫；引入引力荷作为量子钟构造模交叉积；利用量子信道涨落定理（FT）、相对熵收缩、物理过程第一定律及模功等工具，发展三类动力学表述。

Result: 证明了在慢钟极限下GSL严格成立；给出有限钟宽修正的定量估计；识别出非幺正通道导致的非幺正修正项；确立幺正dressed演化是GSL成立的充分（非必要）条件；建立GSL与受控开放量子系统热力学的对应。

Conclusion: GSL可被严格表述为开放黑洞外区量子信道的涨落定理，其成立不依赖于半经典几何假设或特定代数嵌套结构，而根植于模理论、量子信息与引力荷时间参数化的统一框架。

Abstract: We prove the generalized second law (GSL) for black-hole exteriors undergoing unitary driving and relaxation on a fixed semifinite algebra. Wall's influential proof assumes semiclassical geometry and restricts fixed parent states to algebras of successive horizon cuts. We instead evolve the state on a fixed dressed exterior algebra. With complete characteristic data, the same semiclassical exterior supports both descriptions, analogous to the Heisenberg and Schrödinger pictures through state--operator duality. Using a gravitational charge as a quantum clock, we construct a modular crossed product with density operators whose trace entropy reproduces generalized entropy up to clock contributions. Our approach parallels thermodynamics in regulated field theory and formulates the GSL through a quantum channel fluctuation theorem (FT) for the open exterior. We develop three complementary formulations under specified dynamical assumptions. Separated unitary driving and relaxation combine modular work, the physical-process first law relating horizon absorption to area gain, and relative-entropy contraction through data processing. A time-local description accommodates simultaneous unitary driving and dissipation. A two-time surprisal FT treats the complete channel, identifies the nonunital correction, and yields a GSL bound for unital dressed evolution without resolving intermediate dynamics. Relaxation that does not mix distinct dressed-charge sectors and preserves the full dressed reference state is unital. Unitality is sufficient for this FT proof but is not necessary for the GSL along a particular evolution. The entropy bounds establish the GSL in a controlled slow-clock limit and quantify finite-width clock corrections.

</details>


### [150] [Symmetry resolved spread complexity for BMN matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29425)
*Dibakar Roychowdhury*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究BMN矩阵模型在SO(6)标量扇区U(1)全局对称性下的对称性分解传播复杂度，对比低温禁闭相与高温去禁闭相中电荷分辨的Lanczos系数、返回振幅与早期复杂度演化，并建立其与全息对偶几何（LM液滴 vs. 带电D0-膜黑洞）的对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子混沌与复杂度在规范/引力对偶中的对称性分解行为，特别是守恒荷如何影响热场双重态的动力学与信息 scrambling。

Method: 基于电荷分解的热场双重态（cTFD），计算各固定电荷扇区的返回振幅、谱矩、Lanczos三对角化系数；结合低能有效理论（位移谐振子）与大N连续谱近似（热高斯谱密度），分析传播复杂度的时间演化。

Result: 低温相：Lanczos系数呈线性对角+√n离对角增长，复杂度不等分，存在正的复杂度涨落下界ΔC(t)≥0；高温相：a_n^{(q)}恒定、b_n^{(q)}∝√n，复杂度以电荷依赖方差σ_E²(q,T)主导二次启动；全息上分别对应LM液滴中相干弦涨落与带电D0黑洞视界附近的耗散型scrambling。

Conclusion: 对称性分解传播复杂度可作为探测量子相变与全息几何结构的精细探针，揭示守恒荷在量子混沌动力学中的非平庸组织作用。

Abstract: We investigate the symmetry-resolved spread complexity of the Berenstein-Maldacena-Nastase (BMN) matrix model under a global $U(1)$ symmetry acting within the transverse $SO(6)$ scalar sector. Decomposing the time-evolved charged thermofield double (cTFD) state into orthogonal fixed-charge sectors, we examine the return amplitude, spectral moments, tridiagonal Lanczos coefficients, and early-time spread complexity across the low-temperature confined ($\beta\mu \gg 1$) and finite-temperature deconfined ($T \gg \mu$) phases. In the low-temperature gapped regime, effective sector dynamics map onto decoupled, displaced harmonic oscillators with a Casimir-like zero-point offset, yielding factorized moments, linear drift in diagonal Lanczos coefficients $a_n^{(q)}$, and sub-linear $\sqrt{n}$ off-diagonal growth with thermally suppressed initial hopping. Charge-dependent Boltzmann weights, vacuum shifts, and degeneracies break complexity equipartition, while inter-sector coherence and charge dispersion enforce an early-time positivity bound $\Delta C(t) = C_{\text{total}}(t) - \bar{C}(t) \ge 0$. In the \emph{finite}-temperature continuum phase, modeling the planar large-$N$ spectral density as a thermal Gaussian gives constant diagonal coefficients $a_n^{(q)} = \bar{E}_q$, universal square-root scaling $b_n^{(q)} = \sigma_E \sqrt{n}$, and a quadratic spread launch $\mathcal{C}^{(q)}(t) = \sigma_E^2(q,T)\,t^2 + \mathcal{O}(t^4)$ governed by the backreacted sector energy variance. Holographically, these regimes distinguish coherent string fluctuations in horizonless Lin-Maldacena (LM) droplet geometries from dissipative scrambling near charged ten-dimensional black D0-brane horizons.

</details>


### [151] [Scattering amplitudes in AdS$_5\times$ S$^5$ from the pure spinor superstring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29622)
*Luis F. Alday,Thiago Fleury,Tobias Hansen,Lucas N. S. Martins,Dennis Zavaleta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了在AdS₅×S⁵背景中纯自旋器超弦的BRST不变零模积分方案，解决了该背景下振幅计算的核心障碍，并首次直接通过世界面计算了半BPS超引力态的二点与三点振幅，其归一化三点函数精确复现了平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS₅×S⁵背景中，纯自旋器形式论的零模积分缺乏BRST不变且可计算的处方，阻碍了高精度超弦振幅（尤其是相关函数）的世界面计算。

Method: 构造BRST不变的零模测度，引入AdS特有的贡献（区别于平直空间），并等价表述为picture-changing算子作用或非极小正则化方案；应用于任意外线态、任意多重数的亏格零振幅。

Result: 成功实现半BPS超引力态的二点与三点世界面振幅计算；归一化三点函数与N=4 SYM平面极限下由强耦合AdS/CFT预言的结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 该BRST不变零模积分方案是AdS₅×S⁵中纯自旋器超弦振幅计算的关键突破，为高阶圈图与非BPS过程提供了严格基础，并强化了AdS/CFT对应在世界面层面的自洽性。

Abstract: We formulate a BRST-invariant prescription for the integration of zero modes in the pure spinor superstring on $AdS_5\times S^5$. The measure includes contributions absent in flat space and admits a description in terms of picture changing operators or a non-minimal regulator. The prescription is valid for genus-zero amplitudes with arbitrary multiplicity and external states and removes a central obstacle to amplitude computations in this background. We use it to perform the first direct worldsheet computation of two and three-point amplitudes of half-BPS supergravity states. The properly normalised three-point amplitudes exactly reproduce the results of planar ${\mathcal N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory.

</details>


### [152] [Quadratic-in-spin scattering of compact bodies at fourth post-Minkowskian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29670)
*Taushif Ahmed,Syed Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了任意速度和质量比下，自旋对齐致密天体散射角的四阶后闵可夫斯基（4PM）结果，包含自旋平方项、自旋诱导四极矩、辐射反作用及曲率平方型自旋潮汐耦合；通过三圈世界线积分得到以多对数函数与椭圆积分为闭式表达的结果；红外极点精确相消，确定尾部对数项；导出新的后牛顿系数与一般四极矩下的三阶角动量损失；该结果修正了黑洞散射的重求和预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画强场高速自旋致密双星系统的散射动力学，为引力波建模与散射振幅-世界线对应提供高精度基准，尤其关注自旋平方效应与辐射耗散的协同贡献。

Method: 基于世界线有效作用量框架，结合三圈费曼图拓扑与参数积分技术，系统计算含自旋平方、自旋诱导四极矩、辐射反应及R²型自旋-潮汐耦合的世界线散射振幅；利用红外一致性约束固定尾部对数项；采用多对数与椭圆积分闭式表达散射角。

Result: 获得4PM散射角的解析闭式解（含Li₂, Li₃, K, E等特殊函数），红外极点完全抵消，确定尾部对数系数；导出首个含一般四极矩的三阶角动量辐射损失公式；提取新的3PN阶自旋平方相关系数；量化其对黑洞散射重求和轨迹的影响。

Conclusion: 自旋平方与辐射反作用的联合效应不可忽略，其在4PM阶引入关键修正，显著影响散射角的重求和行为与角动量辐射谱，为未来EFT-散射振幅交叉验证及数值相对论标定提供新基准。

Abstract: We present the fourth-post-Minkowskian scattering angle of aligned spinning compact bodies through quadratic spin at arbitrary velocity and mass ratio. We include spin-induced quadrupoles and radiation reaction, supplemented by curvature-squared spin-tidal terms. Three-loop worldline integrals yield closed forms in polylogarithms and elliptic integrals. Infrared poles cancel exactly, fixing the tail logarithm. We obtain a new post-Newtonian coefficient and third-order angular-momentum loss for general quadrupoles. The new input shifts resummed predictions for black-hole scattering.

</details>


### [153] [A note on Holographic Renormalization for Polarized IKKT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29677)
*Matteo Guglielmetti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了与极化IKKT矩阵模型真空对偶的IIB超引力解的壳上作用量的边界抵消项，基于变分原理和渐近运动方程决定的离壳涨落衰减条件；在固定D1-膜电荷系综下，超对称鞍点的重整化壳上作用量重现了矩阵模型结果，该系综通过离壳对偶RR二形式势实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使IIB超引力中与极化IKKT矩阵模型真空对偶的解具有良定义的变分原理，并实现壳上作用量的紫外有限性与全息匹配。

Method: 基于渐近运动方程确定离壳涨落的边界衰减行为，构造满足Dirichlet边界条件的边界抵消项；通过离壳对偶化RR二形式势以实现固定D1-膜电荷的系综。

Result: 得到了良定义的、有限的重整化壳上作用量；在超对称鞍点上，其值精确复现极化IKKT矩阵模型在固定D1-膜电荷系综下的自由能（或有效作用量）。

Conclusion: 证实了该类AdS/CMT型全息对应中边界抵消项的构造一致性，并确立了D1-膜电荷作为全息共轭变量的关键角色，强化了矩阵模型与超引力之间的非微扰对应。

Abstract: We construct a boundary counterterm for the on-shell action of type IIB supergravity solutions dual to vacua of the polarized IKKT matrix model. The construction is based on the variational principle, with off-shell fluctuations whose fall-off conditions are based on the asymptotic equations of motion. The renormalized on-shell action of the supersymmetric saddles reproduces the matrix- model result once we pass to the ensemble of fixed D1-brane charges, which we implement off-shell by dualizing the RR two-form potential.

</details>


### [154] [Infrared finite scattering theory: Non-relativistic Coulomb scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29691)
*Kartik Prabhu,Shivam Upadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了库仑势下的非相对论量子散射问题，通过Dollard修正框架消除了Born级数中因长程相互作用导致的红外发散；发展了一种全阶抵消库仑发散的微扰理论，并在二阶Born近似下显式验证；进一步将该方法推广至海森堡绘景，引入含时正则变换消除渐近相空间中的对数修正，构建了适配修正渐近动力学的LSZ约化形式，为处理相对论性量子场论中长程相互作用与无质量场引起的红外发散提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决库仑势下传统S矩阵微扰计算中因长程相互作用导致的红外发散问题，并为推广至相对论性量子场论（尤其是含无质量场和红外发散的情形）建立严格、可微扰的散射理论框架。

Method: 采用Dollard渐近哈密顿量H_D替代自由哈密顿量H_0以定义散射算符；发展基于Dollard算符的全阶微扰展开，显式证明库仑发散在各阶自动抵消；引入时间依赖正则变换重构渐近相空间变量，消除库仑对数相位修正；在海森堡绘景中多途径构造修正LSZ S矩阵，并建立其与Dollard散射算符的等价性。

Result: 1) 构造出全阶抵消库仑红外发散的Dollard微扰散射振幅，二阶计算复现精确分布意义下的库仑振幅（含前向散射）；2) 实现从薛定谔绘景到海森堡绘景的严格过渡；3) 提出修正的LSZ约化公式，适配Dollard型渐近动力学；4) 建立了连接非相对论Dollard理论与相对论红外结构的桥梁。

Conclusion: Dollard散射理论可系统地纳入微扰框架，其核心思想——通过修正渐近哈密顿量/相空间来吸收长程效应——可自然推广至量子场论，为构建处理软光子、软胶子等导致的红外发散的LSZ型散射理论提供普适范式。

Abstract: We revisit non-relativistic quantum-mechanical scattering in a Coulomb potential. The long-range nature of the interaction leads to divergent integrals in conventional S-matrix calculations using the Born series. Dollard showed that a well-defined scattering operator can be constructed by replacing the free Hamiltonian $ H_0 $ with an appropriate asymptotic Hamiltonian $ H_{\rm D} $. However, explicit perturbative calculations within Dollard's framework have remained scarce. We develop a perturbative formulation of the Dollard scattering operator in which the Coulomb divergences cancel to all orders. Perturbatively, we demonstrate this cancellation explicitly through second Born order and show that the resulting amplitude reproduces the known exact distributional Coulomb amplitude, including its forward-scattering contribution. With the aim of generalizing our analysis to quantum field theory, we reformulate the problem in the Heisenberg picture. We construct a new set of canonical variables through a time-dependent canonical transformation that removes the logarithmic Coulomb correction from the asymptotic phase-space coordinates. Using these variables, we construct the corresponding Heisenberg S-matrix through several complementary approaches. This leads to a formulation of the Lehmann--Symanzik--Zimmermann (LSZ) reduction formalism adapted to the modified asymptotic dynamics, which we relate to the Dollard scattering operator. This construction provides a framework for extending the LSZ formalism to infrared divergences in relativistic quantum field theories with long-range interactions and massless fields.

</details>


### [155] [Bounds on entanglement fluctuations at holographic interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29699)
*Pallab Basu,Vishnu Jejjala*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息共形界面中纠缠涨落与熵的约束关系，证明了有效中心荷、容量和左右中心荷之间的不等式，并揭示了引力响应的结构及Janus喉对容量发散的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在固定熵条件下纠缠涨落的最小可能值，特别是在全息共形界面背景下，理解量子信息与引力响应之间的深层联系。

Method: 基于具标量势的全息共形界面模型，结合背景Einstein方程与耦合引力响应分析，推导熵、容量与能量传输之间的不等式约束。

Result: 证明了 $c_\text{eff}\leq c_\text{cap}\leq c_\text{eff}^2/c_{LR}$；发现引力响应由输运项减去正标量贡献构成，且该减法受Einstein方程限制；指出长Janus喉可在有限熵系数下实现无界容量，同时保持标量响应有能隙。

Conclusion: 纠缠容量受全息中心荷与能流性质严格约束；几何结构（如Janus喉）可解耦容量与熵的线性关系，暗示新型量子信息受限机制。

Abstract: How small can entanglement fluctuations be at fixed entropy? For a class of holographic conformal interfaces supported by a scalar with a potential, we prove $c_\text{eff}\leq c_\text{cap}\leq c_\text{eff}^2/c_{LR}$, relating entropy, capacity, and energy transmission. The coupled gravitational response is a transport term minus a positive scalar contribution; the background Einstein equation bounds that subtraction. A long Janus throat allows unbounded capacity at a finite entropy coefficient while the scalar response remains gapped.

</details>


### [156] [Lie Algebra Cohomology and Seiberg-Witten Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29713)
*Ahsan Z. Khan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了纯N=2超对称规范理论（SU(2)规范群）中微扰全纯-拓扑局域算符代数，证明其为由中心荷为零的Virasoro元X与权为3的奇素元Y生成的泊松顶点代数，且满足X²=0；等价地，计算了相对李代数上同调H*(sl₂[z,w]/(w²), sl₂)及SL₂/PGL₂相容Satake范畴中单位对象的自Ext群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证作者先前关于N=2纯规范理论中全纯-拓扑算符代数结构的猜想，并建立其与相对李代数上同调及Satake范畴Ext群之间的精确对应。

Method: 运用构型空间上的因子化结构、深度滤过论证，以及对系数代数C[z,w]/(w²)的两类形变。

Result: 确认该算符代数为具有中心荷零的Virasoro生成元X与权三奇素元Y的泊松顶点代数，约束仅为X²=0；同时给出相对上同调与Satake范畴中Ext群的具体计算结果。

Conclusion: 纯N=2 SU(2)规范理论的微扰全纯-拓扑局域算符代数完全由上述PVA结构刻画，且与几何/表示论中的相对上同调及Satake范畴不变量严格等价。

Abstract: We compute the algebra of perturbative holomorphic-topological local operators of pure $\mathcal N=2$ gauge theory with gauge group $SU(2)$, proving a conjecture of the author: it is the Poisson vertex algebra generated by a Virasoro element $X$ of central charge zero and an odd primary $Y$ of weight three, subject only to $X^2=0$. Equivalently, we compute the relative Lie algebra cohomology $H^*(\mathfrak{sl}_2[z,w]/(w^2),\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ and the self-Ext groups of the unit in the coherent Satake category for $SL_2$ and $PGL_2$. The proof uses factorization on configuration spaces of points on the affine line, a depth argument, and two deformations of the coefficient algebra $\mathbb C[z,w]/(w^2)$.

</details>


### [157] [Boundary spectroscopy of hidden near-horizon reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30092)
*Yiling Chen,Jiayue Yang,Zhen Zhong,Robert B. Mann,Jie Jiang,Ming Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转BTZ黑洞外部的标量场在近视界处共转被动壁的精确边界响应，发现其频谱由Airy滤波器给出，正能量条件排除了增长模；边界响应可识别壁的恒定阻抗和位置，且二者正交耦合，导致边界表现出反射性而非完全吸收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究经典黑洞视界完全吸收性质的可能偏差，以及这些偏差如何在边界上被观测到。

Method: 基于旋转Ba\~nados-Teitelboim-Zanelli（BTZ）时空中的标量场理论，推导近视界共转被动壁的精确边界响应，结合Airy函数分析其频谱特性，并施加正能量约束以排除不稳定模。

Result: 边界响应由Airy滤波器刻画；响应在任意频带内唯一确定壁的恒定阻抗与位置，二者以谱函数加权正交出现；边界表现为反射性，而完美吸收行为呈指数隐藏。

Conclusion: 经典黑洞视界虽在体空间中表现为完全吸收，但其微小偏差可通过边界响应以非指数方式显现，揭示了全息意义上边界对近视界物理的敏感探测能力。

Abstract: A classical black hole horizon absorbs completely, and any deviation would be hidden in the bulk. For a scalar field in a rotating Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli exterior, we derive an exact boundary response of a corotating passive wall near the horizon. An Airy filter gives its spectrum, and positive energy excludes growing modes. On any frequency band the response identifies a constant wall impedance and the wall position, which enter orthogonally with weights determined by the spectral function. The boundary thus sees a reflecting wall, whereas a perfect absorber is exponentially hidden.

</details>


### [158] [Charged spin-$\frac32$ in a chiral Feshbach--Villars model: hyperbolic propagation, Landau-level instability, and exceptional points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30225)
*Haryanto M. Siahaan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在$(\frac{3}{2},0)\oplus(0,\frac{3}{2})$表示下、含自由旋磁比$g$的Pauli耦合二阶波动方程，其Feshbach–Villars形式为16分量拟厄米哈密顿系统；发现自旋-$j$ Landau谱实性的临界条件为$|g|j \leq 1$，并在$g=2, j=3/2$时揭示零能阈值处出现二阶例外点及正定守恒二次型的丧失；引入高斯阻尼静电势后，在最低两态自旋-$3/2$流形上观察到非实本征值分支，其出现依赖于磁场强度与扰动幅度；结果区分了谱不稳定性与双曲性丧失，但未确立物理费米子量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建高自旋（特别是$\frac{3}{2}$）相对论性波方程的严格谱理论框架，检验其在强电磁场中本征值实性、稳定性与物理可诠释性之间的关系，尤其关注伪厄米性、例外点结构与守恒流存在性之间的联系。

Method: 采用Feshbach–Villars分裂将二阶Pauli耦合方程转化为16分量伪厄米哈密顿系统；利用旋量表示与协变特征分析研究Landau谱；通过微扰理论（一阶非简并/简并）处理静电势扰动；结合例外点（EP2）判据与正定守恒二次型的存在性判定谱稳定性。

Result: 1) 自旋-$j$ Landau谱保持实值当且仅当$|g|j \leq 1$；2) $g=2, j=3/2$时，$|qB|=m^2/2$处发生零能简并，每个手性块出现二阶例外点（EP2），且正定守恒二次型消失；3) 加入高斯阻尼静电势后，在$\frac{m^2}{6}<|qB|<\frac{m^2}{2}$区间内，对足够小非零扰动，最低自旋-$\frac{3}{2}$两态流形上出现非实本征值分支，唯在特定势阱宽度下一级劈裂消失；4) $g=2$自旋-$\frac{1}{2}$极限满足Dirac谱实性检验，而$g=1/j$时该静电扰动不产生一阶虚部修正。

Conclusion: 谱实性、例外点结构与守恒流存在性三者紧密耦合：$|g|j > 1$导致谱失稳，EP2标志着经典可逆性与量子概率诠释的双重失效；但谱不稳定性本身不等价于动力学双曲性丧失；该模型尚不能导出满足正则量子化要求的物理费米子场。

Abstract: We study a Pauli-coupled second-order wave equation on $(\frac32,0)\oplus(0,\frac32)$ with a free gyromagnetic ratio $g$. Its Feshbach--Villars form is a sixteen-component pseudo-Hermitian Hamiltonian system with lightlike covariant characteristics in smooth electromagnetic backgrounds. The solution space is chirality doubled; a covariant physical single-species reduction is not assumed. The exact spin-$j$ Landau spectrum remains real at every field strength precisely when $|g|j\le1$. At $g=2$, the critical spin-$\frac32$ channel reaches zero at $|qB|=m^2/2$, where each chiral block develops a second-order exceptional point. This threshold also marks the loss of a time-independent positive-definite conserved quadratic form for the channel. We also include a bounded, Gaussian-damped harmonic electrostatic potential and its associated electric field. The complete first-order problem on a degenerate manifold reduces to $V\pm iC$, with $V$ and $C$ the projected potential and spin--electric coupling. On the lowest two-state spin-$\frac32$ manifold, nonreal eigenvalue branches arise for $m^2/6<|qB|<m^2/2$ at sufficiently small nonzero perturbation amplitude, except at a special well range where the first-order splitting vanishes. The spin-$\frac12$, $g=2$ limit satisfies the Dirac spectral-reality check, whereas $g=1/j$ has no imaginary first-order correction for this perturbation. These results separate spectral instability from loss of hyperbolicity; they do not establish a physical fermionic quantization.

</details>


### [159] [Matter-decorated trumpets in double-scaled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30296)
*Matteo Beccaria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在sine-dilaton引力及其边界对偶DSSYK模型中，首次构造了携带标量物质场（维度Δ）的、超越JT极限的matter-decorated双喇叭（double trumpet）虫洞振幅；通过ETH两矩阵模型定义物质修饰虫洞，从带物质插入的端世界膜（EOW brane）迹出发构建物质修饰喇叭，并严格证明其按弦数（chords through the neck）分扇区精确拼接为虫洞，权重为正且含弦的扭转与绕颈闭合圈贡献；喇叭解以Rogers多项式的积分变换给出，权重亦得闭式解；在JT极限与经典极限下均正确还原已知结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对物质修饰虫洞（matter-decorated wormhole）的胶合构造仅适用于Jackiw-Teitelboim（JT）极限，而无法推广至更一般的sine-dilaton引力；本文旨在突破该限制，在其边界对偶DSSYK框架下建立普适的、非JT的胶合方案，从而深化对全息虫洞、量子引力中物质-几何耦合机制的理解。

Method: 采用double-scaled SYK（DSSYK）作为sine-dilaton引力的边界对偶，基于ETH two-matrix model（含Gaussian matter）定义matter-decorated wormhole；将matter-decorated trumpet定义为end-of-the-world brane trace with matter insertion；利用chord diagram expansion按穿过虫洞颈（neck）的弦（chord）数目分扇区展开；通过组合分析与正交多项式理论（特别是Rogers polynomials）导出喇叭振幅与胶合权重的闭式表达；验证其在JT极限与经典极限下的还原性。

Result: 1) 首次给出sine-dilaton引力中matter-decorated double trumpet的完整非JT胶合构造；2) matter-decorated trumpet振幅以Rogers多项式的Mellin-Barnes型积分变换显式给出；3) 胶合权重为严格正定，编码弦的twist与winding及绕颈闭合圈的拓扑贡献；4) 在JT极限下，喇叭与权重各自独立还原为已知JT结果（而非仅其乘积）；5) 经典极限下，每个喇叭退化为纯引力sine-dilaton trumpet乘以物质测地线在边界的贡献因子。

Conclusion: 本文建立了sine-dilaton引力中物质修饰虫洞的首个超越JT极限的严格胶合构造，揭示了DSSYK中chord diagram结构与bulk几何之间的精细对应；结果表明，matter-gravity entanglement在非微扰水平上由正权重组合规则与正交多项式结构统一刻画，支持DSSYK作为sine-dilaton全息对偶的完备性，并为高维AdS/CFT中wormhole–matter耦合提供了可计算范式。

Abstract: In sine-dilaton gravity, the double trumpet, the wormhole amplitude without matter, is obtained by gluing two trumpets along a closed geodesic of discrete length and summing over this length. When the wormhole carries the propagator of a matter field of dimension $\Delta$, joining the two asymptotic boundaries and crossing the neck, such a gluing is known only in the Jackiw--Teitelboim (JT) limit. We construct the gluing beyond JT in double-scaled SYK (DSSYK), the proposed boundary dual of sine-dilaton gravity, whose disk amplitudes it reproduces. In this context, we adopt the definition of the matter-decorated wormhole in terms of the ETH two-matrix model with Gaussian matter. We build matter-decorated trumpets from an end-of-the-world brane trace with a matter insertion, and prove that the wormhole is glued from them exactly, sector by sector in the number of chords through the neck, with a positive weight that accounts for the twists and windings of these chords and for the closed loops around the neck. Trumpets and weight are obtained in closed form, the former as transforms of Rogers polynomials, and we discuss the uniqueness of the construction. In the JT limit, both reproduce the known gluing separately, and not only in combination. In the classical limit, each trumpet takes the form expected in the bulk: a pure-gravity sine-dilaton trumpet dressed by the factor that a probe matter geodesic contributes at its boundary.

</details>


### [160] [Rotating Black Holes from Scaling Transformations in Horava Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30337)
*Deniz O. Devecioglu,Mu-In Park*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了低能Hořava引力中改变解物理参数的尺度变换，提出了一种简单尺度变换，可从已知相对论解生成旋转黑洞解；具体构造了'Doran-like'和'Kerr-Schild-like'两类旋转解，二者均为horizon-penetrating，但null horizon非Killing horizon；奇点结构不同，但均保护因果性（chronology protection）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建低能Hořava引力中物理上合理的旋转黑洞解，以检验该理论对广义相对论黑洞结构的修正及其因果性保持能力。

Method: 利用尺度变换（scaling transformation）将广义相对论中的已知轴对称解（如Kerr解）映射至低能Hořava引力框架下，并在Doran与Kerr-Schild坐标系中分别构造新解，分析其horizon结构、Killing性质及奇点与因果性。

Result: 得到了两类新的旋转黑洞解：Doran-like与Kerr-Schild-like；二者均为horizon-penetrating，null horizons不满足Killing条件；奇点结构不同，但均无闭合类时曲线（CTC），满足chronology protection。

Conclusion: 低能Hořava引力可通过尺度变换继承相对论黑洞的部分几何结构，但破坏Killing horizon性质；其旋转解虽偏离爱因斯坦引力预言，仍保持基本因果结构完整性。

Abstract: We study the scaling transformations that change the physical parameters of solutions in low-energy Horava gravity. In particular, we present a simple scaling transformation that generates rotating black-hole solutions in low-energy Horava gravity from known relativistic solutions. As explicit examples, we obtain "Doran-like" and "Kerr-Schild-like" rotating solutions in Horava gravity. We find that both solutions are horizon-penetrating, but their null horizons are not Killing horizons. We also find that, although their singularity structures differ from each other, chronology is protected in both solutions, as in the previously obtained Kerr-like solutions in Boyer-Lindquist coordinates.

</details>


### [161] [Darboux transformation for the ASDYM equations: Solitons and instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30433)
*Shangshuai Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用反自对偶杨-米尔斯方程的单重Darboux变换，将杨-米尔斯拉格朗日密度表示为τ-函数形式；对指数型孤子解，证明τ-函数可由复Grassmann流形Gr(N,M;ℂ)中任意点参数化，并构造相应规范场；对ADHM构型的瞬子，将ADHM构造重新诠释为单重Darboux变换，复现Osborn公式并导出对应联络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立反自对偶杨-米尔斯方程解的统一几何描述框架，连接Darboux变换、τ-函数方法与经典瞬子构造（如ADHM），揭示其内在代数结构与可积性本质。

Method: 应用一重Darboux变换于反自对偶Yang--Mills方程；利用τ-函数表达拉格朗日密度；结合Grassmannian参数化处理孤子解；将ADHM数据嵌入Darboux框架以重构瞬子联络。

Result: 1) 杨-米尔斯拉格朗日密度被显式写为τ-函数的微分多项式；2) 指数孤子对应的τ-函数实现Gr(N,M;ℂ)参数化；3) ADHM构造被等价表述为一重Darboux变换，从而导出Osborn公式及规范联络显式表达式。

Conclusion: Darboux变换为反自对偶杨-米尔斯方程提供了统一的可积系统视角，τ-函数与Grassmann几何共同刻画了孤子与瞬子的模空间结构，ADHM构造本质上是一类特殊Darboux解。

Abstract: Using a one-fold Darboux transformation for the anti-self-dual Yang--Mills equations, we express the Yang--Mills Lagrangian density in terms of $\tau$-functions. For solitons constructed from exponential functions, we show that the associated $\tau$-functions admit a parametrization by arbitrary points in the Grassmannian Gr(N,M;$\mathbb{C}$), and construct the corresponding Yang--Mills fields. For instantons arising from the Atiyah--Drinfeld--Hitchin--Manin (ADHM) construction, we reinterpret the ADHM construction in terms of a one-fold Darboux transformation, recover Osborn's formula for the Yang--Mills Lagrangian density, and obtain the corresponding gauge connection.

</details>


### [162] [Non-invertible Duality Defects in Four-Dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_N$ Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30489)
*Satoshi Yamaguchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维Z_N格点规范理论中的对偶性，构造了实现这些对偶性的拓扑界面，并在自对偶耦合处将其识别为非可逆对称性的对偶缺陷；同时分析了中心对称缺陷及其与对偶界面的交点，推导出若干交叉关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维格点规范理论中由对偶性诱导的非可逆对称性结构，及其与中心对称等传统拓扑缺陷的相互作用。

Method: 基于广义plaquette作用量构造Z_N格点规范理论；通过为每个基本构型赋权并验证其拓扑不变性，定义对偶性拓扑界面；利用缺陷代数与边界条件匹配分析缺陷交点及交叉关系。

Result: 构建了严格拓扑不变的对偶性界面；证明在自对偶点其为非可逆对称性（即duality defect）；导出中心对称缺陷与对偶界面之间的完整junction algebra及多个crossing relations。

Conclusion: 四维Z_N格点规范理论中的对偶性可由拓扑界面精确实现，且在自对偶点升华为非可逆对称性；中心对称与对偶缺陷的耦合结构揭示了高阶对称性范畴的丰富代数。

Abstract: We consider four-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_N$ lattice gauge theories with generalized plaquette actions. These theories are known to exhibit dualities. We construct topological interfaces implementing these dualities by assigning a weight to each building block and proving topological invariance. At self-dual couplings, these interfaces become duality defects representing non-invertible symmetries. We also study center symmetry defects and their junctions with the duality interfaces and derive several crossing relations.

</details>


### [163] [Dirichlet walls beyond JT gravity and their quantum group interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30567)
*Luca Griguolo,Jacopo Papalini,Lorenzo Russo,Domenico Seminara,Alex Tarana*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对sinh稀释子引力在有限狄利克雷边界条件下的正则量子化进行了研究，发现其波函数由q-超几何函数描述，并与U_q(sl(2,R))的约化Casimir及模双代数的双曲矩阵元密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维引力模型（如JT引力）的量子化及其在有限截断下的变形，探索AdS2/CFT1对应中量子群结构的物理实现。

Method: 采用正则量子化方法，在有限Dirichlet边界条件下构造约化相空间；引入两维类空测地线长度及其共轭动量作为基本变量；推导有限差分Schrödinger方程；识别其对称性为量子群U_q(sl(2,R))及其模双代数。

Result: 得到以q-超几何函数为解的宇宙波函数；证明有限截断哈密顿量等于U_q(sl(2,R))的约化Casimir算符；两个散射态对应模双代数U_q⊗U_{\tilde q}(sl(2,R))的双曲矩阵元；唯一在长度为零处正则的组合即为宇宙波函数。

Conclusion: sinh稀释子引力在有限截断下自然诱导出量子群对称性，其量子解构成JT引力超几何波函数的q-变形，为AdS2量子引力提供了具有明确代数结构的可解模型。

Abstract: We canonically quantize sinh dilaton gravity with finite Dirichlet boundary conditions. The reduced phase space is formulated in terms of the length of a two-sided spacelike geodesic in an effective AdS$_2$ geometry and its conjugate momentum. Canonical quantization leads to a finite-difference Schrödinger equation whose wavefunctions of the universe are given by $q$-hypergeometric functions. These furnish a quantum deformation of the hypergeometric wavefunctions of JT gravity. We further show that the finite cutoff Hamiltonian derived from the bulk analysis admits a natural interpretation as the reduced Casimir of $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb{R}))$ acting on hyperbolic matrix elements. The two scattering wave solutions are hyperbolic matrix elements of the modular double $U_q(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb R))\otimes U_{\tilde q}(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb R))$, and the wavefunction of the universe is their unique combination regular at vanishing length.

</details>


### [164] [Conservative tidal effects to third post-Minkowskian order in scalar-tensor theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30762)
*Zhengwen Liu,Hongbin Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用世界线有效场论，计算了无质量标量-张量理论中非自旋致密双星系统在标量-高斯-博内耦合下的保守有限尺寸效应，直至三阶后闵可夫斯基（3PM）精度，并保留了潮汐响应系数线性阶上的全速依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标量-张量引力理论中潮汐形变对双星散射动力学的影响，特别是标量偶极子等新型自由度在低阶后牛顿（PN）与后闵可夫斯基（PM）展开中的作用，以及高斯-博内拓扑项对散射角的修正。

Method: 采用世界线有效场论（worldline EFT），构造含标量场与 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合的非自旋致密物体作用量，提取标量偶极、四极及电磁/混合四极、八极等潮汐响应项；通过世界线路径积分计算3PM阶保守冲量与散射角，保持响应系数对速度的全依赖性，并包含其首阶标量导数。

Result: 获得3PM阶完整的保守潮汐散射角，含标量偶极（始于3PN）、标量导数修正项及线性 Gauss-Bonnet 耦合贡献；结果成功复现广义相对论中至5PN的潮汐散射角与后牛顿动力学，并经测试体极限验证其在质量比首阶（包括GB项）的正确性。

Conclusion: 标量-高斯-博内理论中，标量偶极主导低阶潮汐动力学，其效应早于引力四极两阶（3PN vs. 5PN）；3PM散射角中首次出现标量响应导数与GB耦合的线性项，为区分修改引力理论提供了新的可观测量。

Abstract: Using worldline effective field theory, we compute the conservative finite-size contributions to the impulse and scattering angle of nonspinning compact binaries in massless scalar-tensor theories with a scalar-Gauss-Bonnet coupling through third post-Minkowskian (3PM) order. We retain the full velocity dependence at linear order in the tidal response coefficients. The calculation covers the scalar dipole and quadrupole and the electric, magnetic, and mixed quadrupoles and octupoles. Among them, the scalar dipole enters the dynamics already at third post-Newtonian (3PN) order, two orders before the gravitational quadrupole. At 3PM, the tidal correction to the angle also depends on the first scalar derivatives of the responses and contains terms linear in the Gauss-Bonnet coupling. The results reproduce the general-relativistic tidal angle and the post-Newtonian tidal dynamics through 5PN, and a test-body calculation confirms their leading order in the mass ratio, including the Gauss-Bonnet terms for a companion with vanishing sensitivity.

</details>


### [165] [Gradient formula from the sphere for perturbative RG flows near critical dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31050)
*Marten Reehorst*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在临界维度减ε展开下的微扰重整化群流的梯度性质，证明了球面上带局域耦合的重整化泛函具有有限性、局域Weyl反常、共形Killing矢量守恒律等关键性质，并由此导出一个无反常的Ward恒等式；其一阶变分构成一个梯度公式，其中对称度规由球面上两点函数给出，势能在不动点处正比于球面自由能F̃；该结果适用于多种标量与费米子理论（如φ⁴、Yukawa、六次/三次标量理论），并在特定方案下保持度规非退化；对相互作用规范理论则仍为假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立临界维度附近重整化群流的梯度结构，以统一理解量子场论中单调性（如c定理、F定理）与Weyl反常、共形对称性破缺之间的深层联系，并拓展至含相关算符源项和一般度规背景的情形。

Method: 基于球面S^d上局域耦合的重整化泛函，利用Weyl对称性破缺（局域Weyl反常）、共形Killing矢量Ward恒等式、旋转不变性及自由理论基准，结合ε展开与有限性假设，构造无反常组合泛函并推导其梯度变分结构；特别构造一重整化方案使度规非退化。

Result: 证明存在一个无反常Ward恒等式，其一阶变分为梯度流：β^i = -G^{ij} ∂_j V，其中G^{ij} = ⟨O^i O^j⟩_{S^d} 为球面两点函数构成的对称正定度规，V 在不动点处满足 V* ∝ F̃ + const；该公式对φ⁴、Yukawa、六次/三次标量等多类理论在所有微扰阶成立；相关算符源项通过其Weyl变分自然纳入。

Conclusion: 在d = d_c − ε框架下，只要重整化泛函满足基本正则性与有限性，其RG流即具备几何梯度结构，且势能本质由球面自由能F̃刻画；这为高维a/F定理提供了统一微扰基础，并暗示更普适的Weyl反常主导梯度动力学机制。

Abstract: We study the gradient property of perturbative renormalisation group flows in $d=d_c-\varepsilon$, with a potential and a metric regular at $\varepsilon=0$ up to logarithms of gauge couplings. We state properties of the renormalised functional with local couplings on the sphere $S^d$: finiteness, a local Weyl anomaly, the Ward identity of a conformal Killing vector, rotation properties and statements on the free theory. From them we derive a Ward identity without anomaly for a combination of the functional and its anomaly. Its first variation is a gradient formula whose symmetric metric contains the two-point function on the sphere and whose potential at fixed points is, in an extended form of the properties, proportional to the sphere free energy $\tilde F$ up to a constant. Sources of relevant operators enter through their Weyl variation, and in a scheme which we construct the gradient formula persists, with a nondegenerate metric if a matrix of the free theory is invertible. For multiscalar $\phi^4$ theory and for scalars and Dirac fermions with Yukawa and quartic couplings in $d=4-\varepsilon$, for sextic scalars in $d=3-\varepsilon$ and for cubic scalars in $d=6-\varepsilon$, we derive all these properties from a finiteness hypothesis. The gradient formula then holds to all orders for the beta function defined on the sphere. For interacting gauge theories the properties remain assumptions.

</details>


### [166] [Near-boundary and near-horizon momentum--Krylov response in noncommutative-inspired BTZ spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31106)
*F. A. Brito,F. G. Costa,J. A. V. Campos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了在洛伦兹平滑、非对易启发的BTZ黑洞中下落的有质量探针的径向动量，并将其作为Krylov复杂度的增长率；发现平滑效应导致早期增长系数与晚期指数（即表面引力）对平滑参数依赖不同，因此响应曲线不能由纯BTZ曲线简单标度得到；临界平滑时视界半径与表面引力以1/2和3/2指数消失；边界应力张量与BTZ一致，但Fefferman–Graham展开的z³项和边界锚定测地线长度则不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非对易几何修正（洛伦兹平滑）如何影响黑洞内部动力学及全息复杂度演化，特别是检验Krylov复杂度作为探测量子引力非局域性的工具在平滑黑洞背景下的鲁棒性。

Method: 基于Lorentzian-smearing的非对易BTZ度规，求解测地运动方程获得固有径向动量p_r(τ)；将|p_r|定义为动量Krylov复杂度的时间导数dK/dτ；解析推导早期（τ→0）与晚期（τ→∞）渐近行为，并数值验证；对比BTZ极限、相同Hawking温度的BTZ参考解及高斯质量分布情形。

Result: 平滑使早期增长系数为F'_α(X)/4、晚期指数为F'_α(x_+)/2，二者对平滑参数α依赖不同；临界平滑时x_+ ∝ α^{1/2}, κ ∝ α^{3/2}；边界应力张量T_{ij}^{(0)}不变，但FG系数⟨O²⟩ ∝ z³项与测地线长度ΔL均发生可观测偏离。

Conclusion: Krylov复杂度对非对易结构敏感，其早期/晚期分离行为可作为探测平滑尺度的全息探针；边界CFT一阶数据（能量动量张量）不足以刻画bulk非对易修正，需更高阶FG数据或复杂度动力学。

Abstract: We compute the proper radial momentum of a massive probe falling into a Lorentzian-smeared, noncommutative-inspired BTZ black hole at fixed asymptotic mass, and use it as the growth rate of momentum--Krylov complexity. In the BTZ limit the accumulated complexity is $\mathcal{K}_0 = X(\cosh\tau - 1)$. With smearing, the initial growth coefficient is $F'_\alpha(X)/4$, while the late-time exponent is the surface gravity $F'_\alpha(x_+)/2$. The two depend differently on the smearing parameter, so the deformed response is not a rescaled BTZ curve; we show this analytically and confirm it numerically. For a distant release point the early response approaches BTZ, whereas the late-time rate does not. A BTZ black hole with the same Hawking temperature reproduces the response to within about ten percent, and the remaining difference is set by the early/late mismatch. A Gaussian mass profile gives the same picture. The horizon radius and the surface gravity vanish at the critical smearing with exponents $1/2$ and $3/2$. At fixed mass the boundary stress tensor coincides with that of BTZ, but the $z^3$ Fefferman--Graham coefficient and the lengths of boundary-anchored geodesics do not.

</details>


### [167] [Electromagnetic Memory as a Collider Observable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31107)
*Felipe Diaz,Hernán A. González,Oriana Labrin,Jakob Salzer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了电磁记忆效应与电荷关联器之间的关系，通过大规范对称性的Ward恒等式将软辐射探测器与硬带电轨迹探测器的测量结果联系起来；计算了无质量多点记忆关联器及有质量两点函数，并指出其共线极限给出软光子数；这些关联器具有天球自由玻色子表象，并在首阶辐射修正下保持有限，此时反冲效应产生背对背Sudakov对数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解软物理（如电磁记忆）与硬过程（如带电粒子轨迹）之间的统一描述，特别是在红外安全观测量的构造与天球共形场论框架下的实现。

Method: 运用大规范对称性下的Ward恒等式，结合量子电动力学中的软光子定理与红外结构分析，计算多点记忆关联函数；引入天球坐标系并构造其自由玻色子表示；处理质量效应与反冲修正以提取Sudakov对数。

Result: 建立了电磁记忆与电荷关联器作为包容可观测量的等价性；给出了任意阶无质量多点记忆关联器的解析表达式；导出了有质量两体末态的记忆两点函数，其共线极限复现软光子数算符；证明该类关联器在首阶辐射修正下有限，且反冲导致背对背Sudakov对数。

Conclusion: 电磁记忆与电荷关联器由大规范对称性深层统一；其天球自由玻色子表示揭示了红外结构与共形对称性的内在联系；结果为构建红外安全、天球协变的散射振幅提供了关键可观测量基础。

Abstract: Electromagnetic memory and charge correlators are measured by different detectors, sensitive to soft radiation and to hard charged tracks. We show that the Ward identity of large gauge symmetry relates these quantities as inclusive observables. For lepton pairs produced by an electromagnetic current, we compute all massless multipoint memory correlators and the massive two-point function, whose coincident limit gives the soft photon number. The correlators admit a celestial free-boson representation and remain finite at first radiative order, where recoil generates back-to-back Sudakov logarithms.

</details>


### [168] [Tensionless M5-brane in light-cone gauge. The spinor moving frame method is wanted](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31189)
*Igor Bandos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文完成了M理论中无张力5-膜（其作用量见文献[1]）在光锥规范下的完整固定，重点解决了该极限下局域费米κ-对称性规范固定的非平凡性问题：由于κ-投影算符依赖于非零且非平凡的膜上规范场强，作者引入自旋移动标架方法，基于由规范场强二次构造的非退化矢量与PST标量导数构建移动标架；并简要讨论了规范固定后作用量与运动方程的对称性，同时将此方法推广至无世界面规范场的通常无张力p-膜（即零p-膜）以作对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无张力M5膜在光锥规范下κ-对称性规范固定的技术困难，因其κ-投影依赖于必须非零且非平凡的规范场强，传统方法失效。

Method: 采用自旋移动标架（spinor moving frame）方法，构造依赖于规范场强度二次型与PST标量导数的非退化参考矢量，并以此建立适配的光锥规范。

Result: 实现了无张力M5膜作用量的完全光锥规范固定；导出了具有明确对称性的规范固定作用量与运动方程；统一处理了带规范场的无张力M5膜与不带规范场的零p-膜的自旋移动标架描述。

Conclusion: 自旋移动标架法是处理含非平凡世界面规范场的无张力高维膜κ-对称性规范固定的普适有效工具，为M理论中张力消失极限下的量子化与对偶性研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: We perform the complete light-cone gauge fixing for the tensionless limit of the M-theory 5-brane the action for which was recently obtained in [1]. The problem of gauge fixing of local fermionic $\kappa$-symmetry becomes quite nontrivial because the projector of that is constructed from the gauge field strength which in its turn is required to be nonvanaishing and nontrivial in this limit. To resolve this problem the spinor moving frame method has been adapted. The corresponding moving frame is constructed on a vector quadratic in the gauge field strength, which is required to be nonvanishing and nondegenerate in the tensionless limit, and the derivative of the PST (Pasti-Sorokin-Tonin) scalar. The symmetries of the gauge fixed action and equations of motion are briefly discussed. To make a comparison with the case of usual tensionless p-branes (null-p-branes), those which do not carry worldvolume gauge fields, we also elaborate their spinor moving frame formulation.

</details>


### [169] [Massive de Sitter correlators at one loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31214)
*Amara McCune*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了暴胀时期由费米子和重质量场（带未破缺对称性荷）产生的原初关联函数中的一圈信号，特别是 squeezed 极限下的系数及其偏离该极限的修正；发现无化学势时，精确结果比传统晚期估计大10–30倍（取决于质量与软顶点耦合），有化学势时增幅可达30–100倍；主导项可分解为硬部（精确）与软顶点（本文首次精确计算），相位漂移是偏离 squeezed 极限时主因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晚期时间近似低估了由费米子及重质量场贡献的一圈原初非高斯信号，尤其在 squeezed 极限外失效；需从第一性原理出发，在 de Sitter 时空下获得精确 Mellin-Barnes 表达式以系统修正晚期估计。

Method: 基于 massive de Sitter 关联函数的有限 Mellin-Barnes 积分表示，严格解析计算 squeezed 极限下的三点半谱系数及其动量偏离修正；将系数分解为硬部（树图传播子结构）与软顶点（红外行为），并精确求解二者（含化学势情形）。

Result: 1) 无化学势时，精确 squeezed 系数 = 晚期估计 × 仅依赖 loop 质量与软顶点耦合的普适因子；2) SM 重场 loop 信号被晚期估计低估 10–30 倍；3) 有化学势时，螺旋矢量 loop 增幅达 30×，费米子三角图达 100×（与最新精确结果一致）；4) 偏离 squeezed 极限时，主导项失效主因是振荡相位漂移，本文导出了标量与费米子 loop 的相位修正。

Conclusion: 晚期时间近似在 squeezed 极限下系统性地低估了一圈非高斯信号，尤其对含化学势或重质量场的模型；精确 Mellin-Barnes 方法揭示了软顶点的完整动力学，为 primordial standard clock 和 particle physics in inflation 提供了定量基准。

Abstract: Fermions and massive fields charged under an unbroken symmetry couple to the inflaton only in pairs, so their signals in primordial correlators begin at one loop. Templates for these signals are mostly late-time estimates. Starting from a representation of massive de Sitter correlators as finite Mellin-Barnes integrals, we compute the exact squeezed coefficients of these signals and their corrections away from the squeezed limit. The leading coefficient factorizes into a hard part and a soft vertex. Late-time estimates treat the hard part exactly but approximate the soft vertex by its late-time limit, whereas we compute both exactly. Without a chemical potential, the exact coefficient differs from the late-time one by a universal factor that depends only on the masses in the loop and the coupling at the soft vertex. For the heavy Standard Model loops, the exact signals are ten to thirty times larger than their late-time estimates. With a chemical potential, the ratio depends on the model, and at our benchmarks it reaches a factor of thirty for helical vectors and about a hundred for the fermion triangle, where we agree with recent exact results. Away from the squeezed limit the leading term soon fails, mainly through a drift in the phase of the oscillation, which we derive for scalar and fermion loops.

</details>


### [170] [Nonabelianization map for the $W_{3,c=2}$ conformal blocks. Part I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31232)
*Qianyu Hao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个广义的非阿贝尔化映射，用于从三重分支覆盖曲面$\tilde{C}$上的Heisenberg块构造Riemann曲面$C$上$c=2$的$W_3$代数共形块，并通过$(123)$型分支点的特例验证了该构造对两点关联函数和$\tau$-函数的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Virasoro共形块非阿贝尔化构造的启发，旨在推广该方法至更高秩对称性——即$W_3$代数（对应$\mathfrak{gl}_3$结构）的情形。

Method: 基于$\mathfrak{gl}_3$谱网络定义非阿贝尔化映射，将$C$上的$W_3$块表示为分支三重覆盖$\tilde{C}$上的Heisenberg块；引入谱网络对应的孤子简并度作为新自由度；利用费米子关联函数实现块的显式表达；针对$(123)$型分支点进行具体计算验证。

Result: 建立了$W_3$共形块的新公式，其由$\tilde{C}$上的Heisenberg块与谱网络孤子简并度共同决定；在含两个$(123)$型分支点的最简三重覆盖情形下，成功复现了正确的两点关联函数与$\tau$-函数。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔化方法可推广至$W_3$代数情形，且需额外纳入孤子简并度信息；该构造为高阶$W$-代数共形块提供了几何化、谱网络驱动的统一框架。

Abstract: This work is inspired by the nonabelianization construction of Virasoro conformal blocks. We start the process of defining the nonabelianization map which expresses $c=2$ $W_3$-algebra conformal blocks on a Riemann surface $C$ from Heisenberg blocks on a branched triple cover $\tilde{C}$ of $C$. This generalized nonabelianization map uses the datum of a $\mathfrak{gl}_3$ spectral network on $C$ and we find that this map also depends on the soliton degeneracies corresponding to the spectral network even though the soliton data didn't show up explicitly in the Virasoro case. This nonabelianization construction defines new formulas for $W_3$-algebra conformal blocks on $C$ in terms of fermion correlation functions determined by the Heisenberg blocks on $\tilde{C}$. We provide a check of the proposed construction associated to the more challenging $(123)$ type branch points which start to show up in three-fold covers. To be more precise, the simplest example that we focus on has only two of the $(123)$ type branch points and we show that in this example, our nonabelianization construction gives the desired two-point correlator and $\tau$-function.

</details>
